covered. No serving change or Goal5 completion follows.

2026-10-01 [exact origin-key ambiguity](research/originbinding/EXACT_KEY_AMBIGUITY.md)
rules out the naive fallback override for Goal 5. Two fixture-verified
same-lineage pairs have different bound keys and legacy correlation, but one
is a formatting repeat (`A > B` versus `A  >  B`) and the other reverses the
relation (`A > B` versus `A < B`). Giving key inequality precedence would
count the repeat twice while fixing the conflicting relation. A trusted
relation/occurrence witness is needed before changing packet correlation;
production is unchanged and Goal 5 remains OPEN.

2026-10-01 [guarded group journal v22](docs/experiments/mmm-recall-group-journal-v22-results.md)
REJECTS the simple batch rescue. Its one-per-batch guard validates every
snapshot/as-of and all 192 journals per trial survive WAL/FULL reopen, with
zero guard rejections and actual 2-4 entry batches. Yet 200-event writer
offer p99 is 642.17 ms for group and 669.83 ms with graph cache, versus
203.30 ms for single guarded SQLite and 974.57 ms native. The 8 ms dwell
and serial worker overwhelm the shared-guard benefit. Do not retune the
frozen run or claim a Goal6 pass. Investigate a different publication
ordering architecture; production unchanged, Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [SQLite journal phase profile v21](docs/experiments/mmm-recall-sqlite-phases-v21-results.md)
locates v20's loaded journal tail: writer-arm as-of guard wait p50/p99 is
11.57/15.68 ms, while FULL SQLite insert/commit p50/p99 is 0.51/5.56 ms.
Offer p99 remains 189.91 ms, with complete exact frontiers and durable
reopen. This rejects a fsync-only diagnosis. The next rescue must batch
per-entry as-of validation under one owned writer permit and acknowledge
only after one durable transaction, with backfill and uncertain-ack tests.
Production unchanged; Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [guarded SQLite journal v20](docs/experiments/mmm-recall-sqlite-journal-v20-results.md)
passes focused WAL/FULL acknowledgement, reopen, exact retry/conflict,
future-only acceptance, and backfill/policy rejection. It nevertheless FAILS
the frozen no-learner 200-event writer screen: offer p99 184.89 ms for
SQLite alone and 142.57 ms with graph cache, versus 923.74 ms native.
All 576 offers and 768 future writes per writer cell completed; each trial
reopened with 192 journals. The separate durable path substantially helps
but is not enough. Investigate guarded group commit with per-entry as-of
validation; do not relax durability or the 100 ms gate. Production unchanged;
Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [journal guard-cost v19](docs/experiments/mmm-recall-guard-cost-v19-results.md)
tests whether the as-of publication guard erases the in-memory journal
diagnostic gain. At 200 live events with future writes, native offer p99 is
766.27 ms, unguarded sink 80.77 ms, guarded sink 111.56 ms, and guarded
sink plus immutable graph cache 90.05 ms. All 576 Recall offers and 768
writer operations per cell complete; the guard itself reaches 15.12 ms
journal-boundary p99 without rejection. This leaves an isolated durable
SQLite journal worth testing, but the passing arm is still RAM-only and
borderline. Production unchanged; Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [200-event Recall phase-removal v18](docs/experiments/mmm-recall-phase-removal-v18-results.md)
tests a matched native/graph-cache/in-memory-journal/both factorial with
three paired trials per arm. Native writer offer p99 is 854.21 ms; graph
cache alone 884.88 ms; in-memory journal alone 107.62 ms; both 39.73 ms.
The large journal effect is diagnostic only: its sink retains encoded records
in RAM, not durable journals, and cannot be shipped. Search and graph reads
also slow under writer pressure. Next investigate a genuinely durable,
as-of-validating journal path with restart and uncertain-commit controls,
then repeat the full 200-event bound-worker gate. Production unchanged;
Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [paired full-Recall store profile v17](docs/experiments/mmm-recall-store-profile-v17-results.md)
reproduces v15's 200-event writer overload: offer p99 836.55 ms, of which
queue p99 is 783.32 ms, against 15.15 ms quiet offer p99. Search, graph read,
and durable journal put each grow under writes (marginal p99 15.90, 20.27,
25.63 ms); 200 concurrent residual reads have only a 0.67 ms batch wall
span. These marginal tails are not additive, and no one phase is proven the
sole cause. Next run matched research-only removal controls before touching
the durable service. Production unchanged; Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [raw as-of search v16](docs/experiments/mmm-raw-search-v16-results.md)
isolates the persistent store's `Search` path under the same 50/200-record,
8 ms-offer and future-writer schedule. Exact live-ID returns remain correct;
200-record writer offer p99 is 13.25 ms across576 calls, versus full-service
v15's 788/816 ms. Thus raw search plus writes is not sufficient to recreate
the overload on this fixture. Full service still includes other reads,
candidate work and durable Bayesian journaling; trace their phase costs
before attributing causality or patching. Production unchanged; Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [no-learner probe/writer v15](docs/experiments/mmm-probe-writer-v15-results.md)
reproduces queue overload without the bound worker under the 8 ms-offer
schedule: writer Recall offer p99 is 301/288 ms at 50 records and 788/816 ms
at 200 records across two complete local runs; quiet p99 stays ~14 ms.
All full as-of frontiers and future writes complete. `Service.Recall` still
writes a Bayesian journal, and removing the learner changes scheduling, so
this does not identify the exact v14 cause. Raw store-search versus full
Recall under matched writes is next. Production unchanged; Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [200-candidate bound-worker ceiling v14](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-corpus-v14-results.md)
FAILS the frozen 8 ms-offer writer Recall p99 gate: 580.80 ms versus <100 ms,
with queue p99 528.07 ms and label-age p99 24.79 ms. The paired 50-candidate
writer control passes at 89.22 ms and 200-candidate quiet Recall is 16.66 ms.
All 576 calls, 192 labels, and 768 future writes per writer cell complete,
with exact as-of frontier IDs. This isolates a loaded capacity failure, not
its internal cause; a no-learner writer/probe sibling control is next.
Production unchanged; Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [50-event bound-worker breadth v13](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-corpus-v13-results.md)
passes a stronger opt-in 8 ms-offer load point on persistent local LibraVDB:
the exact 50 as-of IDs remain in all learner frontiers despite 256 concurrent
future writes per writer trial. The definitive three-trial writer-arm Recall
offer p99 is 81.93 ms and label-age p99 21.87 ms, both below the frozen
100/250 ms gates; focused final `-race` correctness also passes. Two earlier
timing repeats reached 84.15/92.35 ms but lacked the exact-ID assertion.
Queue p99 rises to 45.81 ms at 50 events, leaving little tail headroom.
Hash embedding, short same-topic records, one bound label, and local storage
prevent any 200-frontier, real-agent, remote-contract or population claim.
Production unchanged; Goal6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [untouched NASA mission-date transfer](research/public-task-pilot/NASA_TRANSFER_V1_RESULTS.md)
ran the previously frozen sparse weights through 72 isolated service recalls
on 12 new public facts and 24 paired questions. Sparse paraphrase top1 was
5/12 versus baseline3/12, but it lost one baseline-correct literal answer;
the predeclared non-harm gate therefore FAILS. Lexical ties sparse overall
16/24, and all arms pack out the same one support despite complete nomination.
Scored laws stay identical. Three mission clusters and deterministic QA do not
prove agent-answer improvement; Goal5 remains OPEN and this set is consumed.

2026-10-01 [held source proof and rate matrix v12](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-guard-v12-results.md)
removes the bound worker's fail-fast writer conflict without weakening source
authority: all 24 fresh persistent trials completed 64 witnessed labels and
post-load reopen, with 256 future-only writes in every writer trial. On an M4,
the frozen 4ms writer cell FAILS Recall offer p99 (247.29ms, 267.92ms on
repeat, versus <100ms), while the 8ms writer cell PASSES Recall p99
30.03/32.38ms and label-age p99 20.31/23.95ms across the two local runs.
The 1/2ms cells queue heavily. This is a one-event, hash-embedder research
capacity point, not population p99, large-corpus or agent-task evidence.
Production unchanged; Goal 6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [loaded bound-worker v11](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-load-v11-results.md)
FAILED its frozen 1ms-offer screen. Two quiet trials had offer-to-return Recall
p99 356.99/385.84ms; the writer arm rejected its first source-continuity check
as guard-busy, so the full matrix and writer percentiles do not exist. Three
matched probe-only stores still queued at p99 309.55-332.81ms, locating much
of the overload in Recall/journaling throughput at this offer rate rather than
the learner alone. Preserve the failed gate; combine source proof with the
deadline-bound as-of guard before a fresh rate-matrix rescue. Goal 6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [fitted abrupt-exit v10](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-fit-exit-v10-results.md)
passes a stronger fresh-process screen: after one transferred and 31 new
verified labels reach the 32-sample fit boundary, a post-fit probe differs
from baseline (0.79911539 vs 0.62124220); a new query with identical inputs
after abrupt exit and persistent reopen gives the exact same 0.62124220.
Source-index and next-ID continuity pass; deleting the source blocks later
handoff. This does not establish hardware power-loss, uncertain-commit,
loaded p99, or real-agent benefit. Production untouched; Goal 6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [acknowledged abrupt-exit v9](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-exit-v9-results.md)
passes a fresh-process persistent-store screen: one source label, one continuing
worker label, origin-bound motion history, and future-only ingestion survive
without orderly Close; source-index recovery and next-ID continuation work,
while a later backfill rejects. This is not hardware power-loss or uncertain
commit recovery. The two labels are below the 32-sample fit threshold, so
fitted-model prediction equivalence, loaded p99, and agent outcomes remain
unproven. Production untouched; Goal 6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [origin-bound as-of motion v8](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-motion-v8-results.md)
passes a finite opt-in orderly-restart screen on memory and persistent stores:
one transferred witnessed label and one new guarded label survive a future-only
ingestion and reopen, exact retries and next-ID continuation work, and a
backfill rejects. Missing as-of proof, legacy origin metadata, changed seal,
key, outcome, and retain decisions fail closed in adjacent controls. Isolated
M4 component means are 0.801-0.920ms for a 64-label rebuild and 0.370-0.383ms
for empty-journal open with a prebuilt seal. These are not loaded request p99,
power-loss recovery, visible-mutation state transfer, or agent outcomes.
Production is unchanged; Goal 6 remains OPEN.

2026-10-01 [bound-worker motion diagnostic v7](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-motion-v7-results.md)
confirms a safety counterexample on memory and persistent backends: a
backfill leaves the witnessed source valid but invalidates the original
as-of observation set. Therefore a target-independent bootstrap seal alone
cannot rescue future-only restart. Rebinding requires a durable origin
snapshot plus a separate as-of motion proof; the v5/v6 exact-target seal
remains unchanged. This is evidence about the next design, not Goal 6 success.

2026-10-01 [guarded continuing bound worker v6](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-v6-results.md)
partially passes a research-only service boundary: a new journaled observation
receives its own witnessed original, exact source retries survive restart,
verified feedback updates the worker, missing feedback remains pending, and
wrong-query/deleted-source attempts reject on memory and persistent stores.
The first nested-continuity-guard design failed on both backends and was
corrected with prevalidation plus exact final snapshot checks. Isolated M4
memory-store operation means were 0.141-0.183 ms admission and 0.130-0.135 ms
feedback. Exact-target rebinding after future-only motion, loaded p99, crash
recovery, and untouched agent outcomes remain OPEN; Goal 6 remains OPEN.

2026-10-01 [sealed continuing bound worker v5](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-worker-v5-results.md)
passes an opt-in local epoch handoff: a source-validated 64-label history
retains 32 labels, one new label updates the bound research worker, and orderly
restart reproduces its score and next prediction. Changed evidence and stale
source/store handoffs reject; cold replay cannot open a bound journal. On an M4,
64-label sealed rebuild averaged 0.793-0.852 ms and empty bound reopen including
rebuild 1.172-1.195 ms in isolated repeats. This does not authorize subsequent
admissions, preserve unresolved old-epoch feedback, prove loaded p99 or
power-loss recovery, or show improved agent answers. Goal 6 remains OPEN.

2026-10-01 [guarded bound-model publication v4](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-publication-v4-results.md)
passes an opt-in component screen: complete witnessed durable labels by
cutoff, source-filtered rebuild, exact-snapshot and unchanged-log atomic
publication, and as-of guarded reads. Intervening source deletion, new log
entry, competing swap, and premature or post-event scores fail closed. On an
M4 one-label fixture, preparation costs 28.9-30.4 us and publication
11.7-11.8 us mean; fitted-score math was benchmarked separately. This is
not a durable continuing worker, loaded p99, or real-agent improvement.
Goal 6 remains OPEN.

2026-10-01 [durable future-ingestion motion v3](docs/experiments/mmm-source-motion-v3-results.md)
passes a frozen local restart screen: a witnessed old forecast receives its
guarded delayed label after a future-only ingestion and orderly persistent
reopen, while the default wrapper and missing/old/malformed motion histories
fail closed. Component writes cost 76.8-89.0 us on an M4 memory-backend
fixture; the bounded 4096-version as-of scan costs 29.5-38.5 us mean.
This is not loaded p99, power-loss recovery, guarded model publication, or
real-agent learning. Goal 6 remains OPEN.

2026-10-01 [durable research source lineage v2](docs/experiments/mmm-source-lineage-v2-results.md)
passes a frozen local restart screen on persistent LibraVDB: two guarded
labels replay, deletion of A leaves only B eligible for fresh-epoch rebuild,
and exact B recreation cannot revive its old label after restart. A
post-backend sidecar failure, checkpoint mismatch, concurrent owner, and
corruption fail closed. Component benchmark: sidecar continuity 4.7-5.5 us
and writes 62-73 us mean on an M4 memory-backend fixture. These are not
loaded p99 or crash proofs. Publisher as-of motion, model installation,
power-loss recovery, and untouched agent outcomes remained open at v2; the
v3 result above partly addresses as-of motion. Goal 6 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [keyed source witness](docs/experiments/mmm-source-witness-v1-results.md)
passes its component privacy/tamper/cost screens but alone falsely accepts
same-ID event resurrection on both backends. The separately frozen
[wrapper-lineage rescue](docs/experiments/mmm-source-lineage-v1-results.md)
rejects resurrection and lets two replayed guarded labels rebuild from only
the untouched source after an unrelated deletion. It fails closed after a
simulated wrapper restart; duplicate `Put` still quarantines the wrapper,
failing that frozen usability clause. No full durable history transfer,
installation-time publication, loaded latency, or production adoption is
claimed. Goal 6 remains OPEN.

2026-10-01 [bound-label rebuild v1](docs/experiments/mmm-bound-transfer-v1-results.md)
passes its research-only component screen: after a synthetic visible-source
rejection, 32 retained labels refit exactly to fresh component controls,
stale-source outcome flips have no effect, invalid pairs fail closed, and
offline reconstruction p95 is below 1.4ms in three finite repeats. The
immutable forecast/feedback pair is required; old mixture weights and pending
predictions do not cross epochs. This is NOT durable recovery: the service
record lacks enough source/feature witness material to instantiate the
validator for arbitrary mutations, and no new-epoch handoff/load was run.
Goal 6 remains OPEN; next validate a privacy-conscious source witness at
admission and replay it against real retained/deleted store events.

2026-10-01 [fresh Codex-session cold-start replay](research/codex-fresh-2026-10-01/RESULTS.md)
is INCONCLUSIVE under its frozen rule. An offline parser repair was needed for
current-format session records. The holdout yielded 625 scored downstream
tool-use proxy cases, but only two independent scoreable sessions versus the
predeclared minimum of three. Descriptively, EventFrame Brier was
0.30923->0.28386 and Recall@10 was 0.30345->0.44877; update-all was
effectively tied with EventFrame. No real answer labels or selective-update
advantage were established. Private artifacts stay local and unpushed. Goal 5
remains OPEN.

2026-10-01 [Puerto Rico local-time design](research/public-task-pilot/PR_TIME_DESIGN_RESULTS.md)
ran a fresh public 24-event corpus with UTC and AST question pairs. The
existing priority overlay was top1 12/12 on both design wordings, so the
predeclared local-time headroom gate FAILED (no deficit to rescue); focus was
1/12 each. Trace/source inspection shows `what-lexical-v2` and no timezone
normalization, so the successful ranking cannot be credited to canonical
time conversion. All 12 confirmation event clusters remain unrun. This is
diagnostic retrieval evidence, not agent outcomes; goal5 OPEN.

2026-10-01 [USGS near-duplicate retrieval](research/public-task-pilot/USGS_HEADROOM_RESULTS.md)
found real design headroom and passed a separately frozen held-back July 6
gate without changing the existing research overlay. Confirmation priority
top1 was20/24 vs focus2/24, and survival@10 was24/24 vs8/24; nine of twelve
event clusters improved, with zero baseline-success losses. Full-frontier
laws/scores stayed identical, and isolated Recall p95 was23.21ms for priority.
The gain is largely exact timestamp overlap on one earthquake sequence, not
general agent-task or cross-domain validation. Earlier RFC and NARA negative
results remain. No production promotion; goal5 remains OPEN. Next test
semantic time paraphrases and real observed agent outcomes on new tasks.

2026-10-01 untouched [National Archives packet-fusion transfer](research/public-task-pilot/NARA_FUSION_RESULTS.md)
FAILED its frozen gate. Focus top1 was 30/30 positive questions, leaving no
improvement headroom, while priority and set-preserving fusion both fell to
29/30 on one paraphrase. Fusion preserved all 32 baseline packet membership
sets and left full-frontier laws/scores unchanged, but it did not preserve
top1. This disproves the proposed top1 non-harm rule on this finite set and
does not measure agent answer quality. The set is consumed; goal 5 remains
OPEN. A harder untouched agent-task confirmation with real outcome labels is
next, not a post-hoc threshold on the consumed public sets.

2026-10-01 continuation: weekly usage reset below the user's 80% boundary.
The [new RFC retrieval transfer](research/public-task-pilot/RFC_TRANSFER_RESULTS.md)
ran64 actual service calls on15 newly frozen HTTP fact clusters plus two
absent controls, using the current semantic embedding model and unchanged
task-plus-lexical research overlay. Top1 improves literal13->15/15 and
paraphrase5->7/15, but paraphrase support survival falls14->13/15, so the
predeclared useful-transfer gate FAILS. Original laws/scores and journals
match; absent questions still pack unsupported records. No agent completion
model or answer-quality result follows. This set is consumed for tuning;
goal5 remains OPEN. Next evaluate an incumbent-preserving/evidence-gated
packet rule on a separate untouched domain, not a post-hoc RFC threshold.
The [live durable freshness v2 screen](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-live-freshness-v2-results.md)
passes both finite ordinary-build writer-arm gates: Recall p99 52.51ms<100ms
and offered-label-to-observed-publication p99 12.29ms<250ms across192 calls
and 768 future-only writes. All192 labels per arm were observed in a live
published snapshot before Close, not merely reconstructed after replay;
focused race correctness passed. V1's after-return timer mistake is preserved
as a limited result. This is only same-epoch synthetic future-write evidence,
not real-agent population latency or visible-mutation recovery. Goal6 OPEN.
The [guarded durable mixed-write v2 screen](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-mixed-load-v2-results.md)
passed its ordinary-build finite Recall p99 <100ms criterion with 64 guarded
labels and 256 concurrent future-dated writes in each of three writer trials.
Observed p99 rose from7.23ms quiet to51.97ms with writes; 762/768 writer calls
overlapped an active learning operation. Race-instrumented correctness passed,
but its diagnostic p99 was403.16ms. V1's accidental application of the
latency gate under `-race` is preserved as a negative result. This does not
establish population p99, live feedback freshness, visible-mutation recovery
or full agent serving latency; goal6 remains OPEN.
The [guarded durable feedback feasibility v2](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-bridge-feasibility-v2-results.md)
passes a finite persistent-service sequence: 32 chronological admissions and
labels have independently audited source bindings, same-epoch replay restores
them, and visible deletion rejects an unresolved stale label before its
durable write. V1's fixed-time chronology failure remains recorded. This is
not a complete durable bridge or state transfer across mutation epochs;
loaded mixed-write, crash, real-task and tail-latency gates remain OPEN for
goal 6.
The [mixed-mutation bridge test](docs/experiments/mmm-mixed-mutation-v1-results.md)
passes on memory and persistent LibraVDB: future-only insertion preserves old
as-of feedback, visible deletion revokes the old bridge, and a fresh journal
can train a newly bound bridge. This is fail-closed correctness, not continuous
learning recovery: the new bridge starts with a new worker and does not carry
the previous learned state. Mixed-write load and durable state transfer remain
OPEN for goal 6.
The [notification workload repeat](docs/experiments/mmm-notification-repeat-v9-results.md)
ran all 36 off/notified/polling cells on the current research wrapper. Queue64
notification passed all six unchanged finite gates again; polling failed three
queue64 age cells and two queue16 admission cells. Four dependencies changed
since v8, notably the publication-store writer gate, so this is not a strict
same-source replication or production latency proof. Goal 6 remains OPEN;
mixed mutations, durable recovery, realistic task outcomes and population
tail latency still need testing.
The [new-outcome v10 study](docs/experiments/mmm-retained-truth-v10-results.md)
ran 960 fresh synthetic streams with separate fit/evaluation seeds by phase.
All three challenger arms FAIL the unchanged shift-recovery gate under both
uniform and correlated inputs. A post-hoc exact-law diagnostic reconstructs
every outcome and shows large headroom both on the acquired views and from
additional information; it is not a rescue. This limits v9's positive static
result to the earlier outcome family and keeps goals 1/2/4 OPEN.
The [issued-expert follow-up](docs/experiments/mmm-retained-truth-v10-experts-results.md)
finds early tree/neutral headroom but no learned replacement expert beating
neutral over either full shift256 cell. Weighting alone is not a general
rescue. A structured specialist plus guarded switching needs fresh broad
testing; prior standalone ridge results remain negative.
The [consumed-v10 ridge screen](docs/experiments/mmm-ridge-v10-component-results.md)
replayed 960 streams using only arrived paid audits and actual selected views.
Ridge improved the interaction cell but harmed both stable cells, null, and
the primary shift256 cell under both input generators. All fits succeeded and
the independent forecast-tape scorer agrees. This rules out raw ridge as a
general challenger on these tapes; it is not new confirmation, and goals 1/2/4
remain OPEN. The next test should predeclare safeguards and use a fresh
outcome family or untouched agent tasks, not tune on v10.
The [alternative-generator v9 study](docs/experiments/mmm-retained-covariates-v9-results.md)
ran 960 additional 512-frame streams under biased-independent and
latent-correlated input draws without modifying the v8 learner. The static
retained arm passes the unchanged gates in both; adaptive retained fails the
latent shift-256 lower-bound gate. Independent summary arithmetic agrees.
This advances goals 1/4 at synthetic input-generator scope only, not an
independent outcome family or real agent tasks. A v8 documentation erratum
clarifies that design/confirmation reuse the same fitted base per scenario/fit.
The [audit-stream checkpoint](docs/experiments/mmm-audit-localization-stream-v1-checkpoint.md)
replayed byte-exactly. A [frozen restart screen](docs/experiments/mmm-audit-restart-v1-report.md)
improves 0.15-margin audit nominations from 16/128 to 72/128 changed
schedules by frame 511, with 0/128 stable and zero pre-change nominations in
this consumed synthetic sample. Independent score and crossing checks pass.
It remains slower and less complete than the external split gate, whose
stopping-time evidence is different; it cannot authorize Anti-Pigeon splitting
or establish predictive benefit. All seven goals remain OPEN. Next: focus on
untouched agent-task validation or a separately frozen downstream policy,
not another post-hoc threshold adjustment on this audit stream.

[Onset-localization proposal](research/onset-localization-proposal.md) follows
primary-source checks of stopping-aware localization (Saha/Ramdas2025,2026).
Not a drop-in certificate: exchangeability, null-survival calibration, delayed
sample mapping and detector filtration remain to be established. Deterministic
boundary contract passes11968cases: retain origins>=max(C), not min(C), ONLY
conditional on true onset belonging to C. No estimated C or coverage result.
Prior segmentation/window-bank failures reviewed; no duplicate rescue adopted.
NEXT feasibility audit on already-paid random full audits with a frozen score,
keeping onset nomination separate from split/predictive authorization. AllsevenOPEN.

[Actual-split retention](docs/experiments/mmm-split-retention-v1-report.md)
shows strict post-detection reset would lose most useful evidence: active
delayed reverse cases at480 retain5.21/4.78 labels versus32.29/36.56 oracle-clean.
Old snapshots invalidated; unsplit cases remain mixed. All1024 checkpoints and
clean-fit counts verified, replay exact; no predictions changed. NEXT inspect
prior segmentation/window-bank failures before an onset-localized or prewarmed
candidate; onset selection needs uncertainty and forward validation, not oracle
backdating. Earlier birth-weight failures remain closed. All seven OPEN.

[Count-matched clean fits](docs/experiments/mmm-clean-fit-v1-report.md) show useful
hindsight headroom: checkpoint480 delayed reverse subset risk .21427->.11425
and .20623->.10462 at equal sample count. Both directions improve; early delayed
parity remains weak with~15labels. NOT a deployable reset. All1024 checkpoints
retained (179 no-fit neutral),909sourcehashes,1,572,864 independent count checks,
native and summary replay exact; focused race/vet pass. Failed initial no-fit
collection preserved; use -verified raw. NEXT inspect historical reset failures
then assess training evidence retained by actual accepted split boundaries,
not oracle boundaries. All seven OPEN.

[Existing-expert hull diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-window-hull-v1-report.md)
finds reweighting headroom but irreducible-to-reweighting error in current fitted
forecasts. Late reverse coupled served gap .088-.117 becomes .040-.049 even
under unrestricted hindsight leaf mixing. This is not an achievable policy or
a proof of family misspecification. 14,641 projection checks, all prior oracle
gaps reconstructed, pinned tapes and replay exact. NEXT count-matched,
already-arrived, post-change-only diagnostic fits versus actual windows to
separate sparse support from contamination; check historical work first.
Oracle boundaries remain evaluator-only. All seven OPEN; no adoption.

[Oracle-view diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-window-oracle-v1-report.md)
separates missing-variable uncertainty from same-view forecast error on all
256 consumed schedules. Late immediate forward missing-information terms rise
.12775->.14307 and .10165->.13101 under coupling; reverse terms fall while
forecast error remains large. Thus observer-only rescue is insufficient.
39,366 exhaustive partial-view checks plus algebra controls; pinned inputs and
native-tape replay byte-exact. Hindsight only, NOT an efficacy result. NEXT
same-view oracle convex-hull diagnostic for existing constituent forecasts to
separate missing capacity from reweighting headroom. All seven OPEN.

[Event-count proxy ablation](docs/experiments/mmm-window-proxy-v1-report.md)
FAIL: 2/4 recovery, 14/16 nonharm. Neither immediate forward failure clears;
previous coupling had 3/4 recovery. All 256 native replay schedules byte-exact,
895 captured hashes and 5,111,808 independent scalar checks verified; summary
replay exact. Focused proxy prediction ~5us versus ~11us is not service latency
or a quality rescue. Historical family substitution also failed sparse-data
protection; do not repeat it without a distinct mechanism. NEXT diagnose fresh
evidence sufficiency versus acquisition/selection on consumed tapes, keeping
oracle information diagnostic-only. All seven OPEN; no adoption.

[Window coupling](docs/experiments/mmm-window-coupling-v1-report.md) runs all256

[Training exposure](docs/experiments/mmm-training-exposure-v1-report.md) matches

[Separate view experts](docs/experiments/mmm-view-experts-v1-report.md) fail

[Available-evidence diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-available-evidence-v1-report.md)
rejects blind view expansion:0/4 recovery,12/16 nonharm. Stable parity improves

the -verified raw and verified-summary.mjs.882hashes/summary checks pass.
Next separately scored view experts, after checking prior attempts, not assuming
old weights apply to richer information. No extra-read or adoption claim.

[Credit monitoring closed loop](docs/experiments/mmm-credit-learning-v1-report.md)
FAILS quality:0/4 recovery gains,16/16 nonharm. Full256-run replay and shadow
gate/ledger parity verified;878hashes and summary checked. Better detection
alone is not a forecast rescue. Monitor prefixes stay within allowance, but
changed foreground choices add2-4coordinates in3schedules. Prior fresh-weight
rescues already failed; don't repeat them. Next diagnose explicit forecast reuse
of already-paid available observations with honest mask/cost contracts. AllsevenOPEN.

[Shared-read credit gate](docs/experiments/mmm-monitor-credit-v1-report.md)
passes consumed shadow screens: delayed reverse5->15 and1->14, forward16/16
retained, no stable splits, every prefix within old coordinate allowance.
Four reverse detections occur after511; by511 counts14 and11. All256 race
Records exact,875hashes and summary checked. Acquisition/forecast~22us perpair
with2extraallocs,notservice. NEXT closed-loop forecast quality with gate-clock
and budget parity before any fresh confirmation or adoption. AllsevenOPEN.

[Monitoring budget reallocation](docs/experiments/mmm-monitor-budget-v1-report.md)
raises delayed reverse splits5->15 and1->16 of16, but forward drops16->15 in
both cohorts;16/128 trajectories exceed the old budget (max18.25%). FAIL, not

shared-read credit ledger, described in research/monitor-budget-repair-proposal.md,
must prove prefix cost and preserve forward detection before closed-loop tests.
All seven goals OPEN; no downstream-quality result from this shadow screen.

[Audit signal and available-view rescue](docs/experiments/mmm-full-audit-gate-v1-report.md)
both FAIL exploratory detection screens. Full-audit improves signal but loses
support; available-view retains forward counts yet reverse stays5/16 and1/16.
Both256-record race replays exact,870/871sourcehashes and summaries verified.
Crucial qualification: gate null tolerates scalar gap .15; original reverse
gaps are inside tolerance in3/16 and10/16 cases, combined in2/16 and4/16.
Next predictable observation-budget reallocation/target-certificate diagnosis,
not threshold weakening. No closed-loop or goal completion claim.

[Switch-gate signal diagnosis](docs/experiments/mmm-arrival-switch-gate-diagnostic-v1-report.md)
replays every permission/nomination and split clock across256 schedules.
Exhaustive512-input expectations show equal conditional TV .45 but mean reverse
correctness gap only .126 versus .45 forward. Complete invisibility hypothesis
is falsified; substantial attenuation is confirmed. Next test frozen full-input
correctness on existing random audits, preserving the error gate and counting
the smaller effective sample. No threshold weakening or adoption. AllsevenOPEN.

[Current-architecture switch transfer](docs/experiments/mmm-arrival-switch-transfer-v1-report.md)
passes only2/4 recovery screens and16/16 nonharm screens. Complete256-run replay
is byte-identical;868sourcehashes verified. All four delayed switch full-arm
post means remain above.25. External gate splits16/16 versus1/16 in cohort2
opposite directions: next diagnose conditional-change sensitivity before any
threshold rescue. Prior v104/v106 failures stand. All seven goals remain OPEN.

[Arrival-learning breadth](docs/experiments/mmm-arrival-breadth-v1-report.md)
passes 19/20 gain and 48/48 nonharm screens on 768 consumed schedule-runs.
Second-cohort reverses_xor2 remains inconclusive despite mean gain .026382;
six second-cohort changed cases still score above neutral .25 Brier. All 867
source hashes and summary replay verified. Observation cost increases; this
does not validate equal-cost observation or serving performance. Next test
older switching-family failures with the frozen method. All seven goals OPEN.

[Inner-arrival isolation](docs/experiments/mmm-inner-arrival-v1-report.md)
adds .00017-.00241 Brier improvement beyond outer-only across eight consumed
changed cells. All three parent endpoints exact,865sourcehashes and independent
scores verified. Large gain remains outer; stable harm and>.25 cases remain.
Next broaden dependence/switch generators, not another selector parameter sweep.
No fresh confirmation, serving result, or whole-goal completion.

[Coupled arrival diagnosis](docs/experiments/mmm-coupled-arrival-v1-report.md)
shows acquisition does not erase the outer-only arrival gain on current consumed
generators: .019-.036 Brier improvement across eight changed cells, with more
foreground observations and several scores still>.25. Original endpoints and
Go/JS fixed-view scores match;861sourcehashes and summary checks pass. Old v104
failures remain. Next cross-generator/inner-credit isolation before fresh broader
confirmation; do not promote this partial consumed-data improvement.

[Tracking-preserving replica diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-delay-tracking-selector-v1-report.md)
matches128 Go primitive steps and every immediate original forecast, yet replicas
still harm delayed recovery. Single arrival updates improve changed fixed tapes
by~.020, but earlier full v104 carry FAILED. Do not promote this consumed-view
gain. Next isolate selector/acquisition coupling and generator differences
before another fresh test; preserve gate authority and old failures.

[Bounded replica selector](docs/experiments/mmm-delay-replica-selector-v1-report.md)
FAILS the consumed fixed-advice recovery screen. Replicas outperform a simple
arrival selector under delay, but both lose badly to original after shifts;
immediate behavior already regresses. Lifecycle and cumulative observed-loss
checks pass without implying segment recovery. Next preserve a strong immediate
tracking controller before further delay-reduction work; no rate-grid rescue.

[Delayed-replica feasibility](research/delay-replica-feasibility.md) checks a
primary-source BOLD lead against384 consumed feedback schedules. Unexpired
copies grow to97-145 under missing labels; age32 expiry needs24-30 on these
tapes but changes the protocol and leaves unknown losses. No inherited regret
or quality claim. Consider only a bounded identity-safe selector variant, not
unbounded model replication or another holdout/reset parameter sweep.

[Materialized batch lookup](docs/experiments/mmm-materialized-batch-v1-report.md)
narrows the read gain to5-13% in the actual bounded batch API, across grouped
and scattered layouts.96,000 outputs verified; race snapshot/caps/cancellation
and source/summary checks pass. Still no write rescue. Retain as an optional
read lead, not a reason to integrate an incomplete schema. Next return to
unresolved quality directions rather than extrapolate point-lookup gains.

[Bounded materialized reads](docs/experiments/mmm-materialized-bounded-v1-report.md)
retain a scoped37-44% mean point-read gain with the original bounded projection
and shared row validator. Every200-record append cell is slower, so no admission
rescue. Race/vet/source/summary checks pass;48,000 reads verified after reopen.
Candidate remains admission-only and unintegrated. Batch reads and full-service
freshness/tails would be required; read gain does not complete goal6.

[Materialized source transaction](docs/experiments/mmm-materialized-source-v1-report.md)
does NOT show a consistent write rescue: later200-record trials are ~4% slower.
Reads are ~half the time but omit original defensive materialization bounds,
so not an equivalent-contract speedup. 48,000 reopened originals verified;
race/vet and source/summary checks pass. Next equalize defensive read behavior
before crediting a read gain; no production adoption or whole-goal success.

[Canonical source component](research/canonical-source-component.md) passes
decoded-key/alias/byte-preservation tests under race, but costs ~4.2us and
7.8KB/56 allocations per 1KiB-padded record. This nearly matches the isolated
index-row savings per record BEFORE replacement insertion costs. No speedup
claim; requires whole-transaction comparison and explicit legacy Unicode policy.
No production wiring. Avoid simply layering preparation onto the old writer.

[Packed index candidate](docs/experiments/mmm-packed-index-semantics-v1-report.md)
is REJECTED before timing: multiple-path JSON extraction retains lexical escape
differences that scalar extraction decodes. An actual temporary unique index
accepts two semantically identical sources; the original index rejects them.
Race-tested witness and six-case replay recorded. Future typed keys must preserve
decoded identity and payload agreement, not rely on canonical fixture encodings.

[Source-index write diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-index-write-v1-report.md)
confirms additional row work of .21-.23ms per50 and .94-.95ms per200 entries
in all six paired cells. The absent-index arm explicitly admits duplicate sources
and is NOT a rescue. All48,000 originals verified after reopen; race boundary
tests, vet, and independent summary replay pass. Investigate a cheaper typed
source representation preserving the constraint; do not remove it or infer
end-to-end performance from this ablation. All seven goals remain open.

[Append phase attribution](docs/experiments/mmm-append-phases-v1-report.md)
locates an independent goal-6 bottleneck: preflight is 4.60-5.55% of isolated
append time; rows plus durable commit are 92.00-94.24%. All 48,000 originals
survive reopen with exact identities and payloads; parity race tests and summary
reconstruction pass. This is not full-service performance validation. Investigate
row costs before changing representations; preserve validity through commit.
Exact leave-one-out stacking was already tested in v93 and failed its complete
quality gates, so it was not repeated as a new rescue. All seven goals stay open.

[Publication factorial diagnosis](docs/experiments/mmm-publication-factorial-v1-report.md)
replays all192 consumed trajectories under both schedules with four arms.
Immediate harm is mainly withholding; delayed harm is mainly publication reset.
Original endpoints and complete replay match. Even per-trajectory hindsight
constant blends miss .005 in5/8 delayed changed cells. Preserve fresh training
and accumulated selection evidence; investigate validation/refit transfer before
another rescue. No new confirmation or whole-goal success is claimed.

[Publication holdout quality](docs/experiments/mmm-publication-v1-report.md)
FAILS on192 fresh trajectories/two schedules. Warm beats cold on8/8 delayed
gains but unchanged on0/8; only32/48 non-harm screens pass. Immediate changed
cases worsen materially. Full replay and calibration/fit/source reconstruction
pass. Withholding16 audits adds64/85 frames of training staleness; resets are a
second confound. Diagnose these separately, not a holdout/prior parameter sweep.

[Publication holdout component](research/publication-holdout-component.md)
passes label-isolation/provenance and literal-weight race tests. New-model
selection uses16 withheld labels without refitting; old-model advice is not
misattributed. Quality UNTESTED; full bundle ~7.1-7.2ms,6.96MB on M4. Next a
fresh unchanged-control / heldout-reset / heldout-calibrated comparison, retaining
the cost of withholding recent training data and all original quality gates.

[Delayed forest quality](docs/experiments/mmm-forest-delay-v1-report.md) FAILS:
192 fresh trajectories under two paired schedules;0/8 delayed improvement
screens,24/24 relative non-harm screens. Both delayed learners exceed neutral
.25 Brier after changes. Complete forecast replay and independent accounting
pass. Early windows lack good pure experts; late windows show useful short
forecasts underweighted. Inspect prior protection/selection failures before a
new rescue; no forest retuning or unconditional stale-loss carry.

[Delayed subset journal](research/subset-delay-journal-component.md) now passes
immediate parity and delayed lifecycle tests under the race detector. It is
test-only, generation-scoped and conservative about stale advice. Narrow paired
prediction/update costs are 8.5-8.7us direct versus 8.9-9.0us journal. Next is a
frozen delayed-quality experiment, not deployment or a claim of robustness.

[Imperfect/changing dependence](docs/experiments/mmm-forest-dependence-v1-report.md)
fails the improvement screen: 384 fresh trajectories, 24/24 non-harm screens but
0/20 changed-case gain screens in both fixed-observation and coupled arms.
Source integrity and summary reproduction pass. Preserve the forest as an
unpromoted candidate; do not retune on these outcomes. Delayed-feedback testing
requires an explicit issued-forecast journal; the current single-pending-state
adapter cannot safely be tested by merely deferring its feedback call.

[Adaptive forest integration](docs/experiments/mmm-forest-integration-v1-report.md)
is partial progress:320freshstreams, all20non-harm screens, positive dependent
gains in both cohorts but only1/14changed-case .005gain gates. Uniform parity
regression does not recur; no broad success or production promotion. Freeze
candidate for imperfect/shifted dependence and delay tests, no threshold tuning.

[Held-out forest fixed-mask screen](docs/experiments/mmm-forest-input-v1-report.md)
PASSES2/2 primary gains and180/180 non-harm on576freshfits. Full tree fails3
checks on the same cohort. XOR input dependency remains unresolved; no universal
claim. Next unchanged-candidate adaptive integration, fixed/own-observer controls.

[Held-out input forest](research/heldout-input-forest-results.md) component passes
normalization, selection and no-label-leakage checks; fit/select64 ~20.8us.
Half-window training/validation selects uniform or a nested tree prefix, without
refit. Quality still untested; next fresh four-arm comparison, no split tuning.

[Tree input finite-data screen](docs/experiments/mmm-chow-input-v1-report.md)
overall FAIL:576fresh fits,2/2 copied-field gains but179/180 non-harm cells.
Independent-input gain lower-.010101 misses-.01; null cases pass. Higher-order
XOR input is a large representational gap versus histogram. Next research an
evidence-penalized forest and existing family-selection work, not threshold tuning.

[Chow-Liu input component](research/chow-input-component-results.md) passes
algebra/no-label-leakage checks; fit64 plus512weights ~5.6us. Input-law-only
candidate, distinct from failed outcome trees. Next fresh uniform/empirical/tree
comparison with independent/copied/higher-order inputs and low/high/null noise.
No quality claim or runtime integration yet.

[Coherent-count stress](docs/experiments/mmm-coherent-count-stress-v1-report.md)
rejects unconditional replacement:384fresh fits show substantial high-noise/null
overconfidence. Exact fixed-context null identity proves higher variance when
prior mass shrinks with observed depth. Keep the original; structural pooling
or validated model comparison is needed, not a concentration sweep on these data.

[Coherent count component](docs/experiments/mmm-coherent-count-v1-report.md)
passes literal marginal checks;192fresh fixed-mask fits show low-noise benefits
but small uninformative-mask regressions. Fit64 costs72-74us, not serving.
Next null/high-noise stress before joint-observer integration; no replacement
of the original count estimator or whole-goal validation yet.

[Count coherence audit](docs/experiments/mmm-count-coherence-v1-report.md)
confirms a compatibility barrier: per-mask Beta smoothing can produce parent
forecasts outside both child forecasts, so the current count predictor cannot
be wrapped unchanged as a joint law. Separate coherent prior/marginalization
candidate needed before reusing earlier joint observation; deeper lookahead
already failed its extra-gain test in v103. No original estimator change.

[Parity observation trace](docs/experiments/mmm-subset-input-trace-v1-report.md)
replays32trajectories exactly. Candidate loses complete relevant information
on340/387frames, gains on9/36; most losses spend the full budget under count
guides. A subset confidence-stop tweak alone cannot rescue this. Next inspect
prior joint acquisition research before changing guide authority or lookahead.
Use corrected summary only; initial parser artifact is explicitly invalid.

[Empirical input integration](docs/experiments/mmm-subset-input-integration-v1-report.md)
fails on320fresh streams. Component dependency gains shrink; no coupled changed
cell passes .005 gain. Parity4 exceeds .01 mean harm in both cohorts, much worse
than the matched fixed-observation arm. Preserve the current implementation;
next inspect acquisition paths before another estimator or pseudocount change.

[Input marginalization diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-subset-input-diagnostic-v1-report.md)
finds component headroom: copied-field partial Brier .1042->.0497 (n64),
.1005->.0481 (n128) with empirical inputs, but independent inputs worsen
about .004. Full-input forecasts unchanged. Next isolate this in retained-subset
integration with fresh data and unchanged selector/gate; do not repeat the
earlier failed empirical-forest experiment or call this an integrated rescue.

[Integrated Brier selector](docs/experiments/mmm-integrated-brier-v1-report.md)
fails: 160 fresh fitted streams, unchanged observations and gate actions.
Strong substitution beats matched eta2 linear slightly but both degrade against
the existing selector on member/common/recurring shifts in both cohorts.
No .005 gain passes. Preserve the existing selector; no loss-rate sweep on
these consumed data. All seven goals remain open.

[Fixed-observer birth diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-fixed-observer-birth-v1-report.md)
also fails. Earlier low-weight mediation did not justify upweighting: local can
beat pooled while still being worse than the full ensemble. Exact algebra and
matched-observation replay support that distinction. Close the fixed-prior
rescue; preserve Anti-Pigeon structural authority separately from predictive
promotion. Check existing proper-loss selection work before another technique.

[Fresh local-expert prior](docs/experiments/mmm-local-birth-v1-report.md)
fails the quality rescue: member effects change sign and recurring means worsen
in both cohorts. Arithmetic is cheap but acquisition work increases. Preserve
this negative factorial result; no larger-weight sweep. Next distinguish changed
observation choices from forecast effects, after checking earlier decoupling work.

[Split mediation trace](docs/experiments/mmm-gate-mediation-v1-report.md)
preserves all archived outputs and identifies low slot influence: better local
forecasts inherit the pooled expert's tiny weight. Next test an explicitly
bounded prior for a newly activated expert without resetting other histories
or weakening Anti-Pigeon. This is a new action hypothesis, not a proven fix or
permission to claim specialist-expert guarantees. All seven goals remain open.

[Gate isolation with the stronger learner](docs/experiments/mmm-subset-gate-pair-v1-report.md)
still fails the .005 forecast-gain screen despite29-39% faster member isolation.
Both arms use identical subset fits; this removes the earlier learner confound.
Splitting changes only one pooled/local slot. Next diagnose that slot's actual
influence without altering emitted forecasts, rather than lowering thresholds.
This160-trajectory pilot does not establish rare-error or whole-goal success.

[Envelope batch reuse](docs/experiments/mmm-envelope-batch-read-v1-report.md)
does not rescue this storage design. Even with bounded reuse and full source
validation, every read cell loses to the existing indexed batch API; scattered
50-event queries cost8-9x as much. All144,000 originals are verified and tests
pass, but correctness does not establish performance. Close this candidate,
preserve failures, and pursue a distinct mechanism or another open goal.

[Identity-restored envelope test](docs/experiments/mmm-envelope-reference-v1-report.md):
the earlier storage-only advantage mostly disappears. 200-event append improves
about13%, but source lookup doubles; 50-event append regresses in two trials.
No promotion. Atomicity/retry/reopen tests pass and48,000 originals are verified.
Next test bounded batch reads with full source validation against the existing
batch API; repeated envelope access is a hypothesis, not established root cause.

[Envelope storage feasibility](docs/experiments/mmm-admission-envelope-v1-report.md):
200-event envelope append .555-.573ms vs per-event1.676-1.696ms; staged total
.604-.795ms with a .063-.078ms marker. Race/vet/reopen/retry and source replay
checks pass. This excludes real per-source uniqueness, so it is only a storage
lead. Next restore those references and exact per-event retry/replay semantics
in an isolated one-transaction prototype before considering staged activation.
No daemon promotion or goal completion follows from this microbenchmark.

[Admission interleaving audit](docs/experiments/mmm-admission-interleavings-v1-report.md)
finds an explicit stale-commit schedule for both early guard release and an
unguarded final recheck. Reacquiring through commit closes the modeled race but
retains the expensive protected append. Existing runtime interleaving tests
pass under race detection. This rules out an unsafe shortcut, not the research
goal: staged durable acceptance needs a precise recovery contract before coding.

[Direct publication-gate timing](docs/experiments/mmm-gate-timing-v1-report.md)
attributes 350-362 ms of cumulative ingestion waiting per active cell to a
guard held for roughly the same duration by background admission. All work is
accounted for, but active write tails remain worse in every matched trial.
The 12-cell run and exact source-verified summary replay are complete; this is
diagnosis, not a rescue. Next designs must preserve validity through admission
and uncertain-commit recovery, not merely release the guard early. Cold
observations do not establish learning freshness. All seven goals remain open.

[Original recovery protocol feasibility audit](docs/experiments/mmm-recovery-feasibility-v120-report.md):
seven requirements in the original32-index archive are mathematically
unattainable even at the noise floor. Neither cap can pass its full contract
on those records. This is independently checked, not a low-power diagnosis.
All failed results remain failed. Approval requested before drafting a new
post-change recovery protocol; no gate or goal has been changed. Other goals
remain actionable while that decision is pending. All seven remain open.

[Broader forecast pool and incremental computation](docs/experiments/mmm-strong-pool-v1-report.md):
full-pool Brier improves to .154982984, with fewer harmful windows, but only
625/672 protection and 1/96 recovery screens pass. A separate oracle audit
finds one recovery gate unattainable on this exact cohort even at the noise
floor; preserve the failure and inspect the original protocol's feasibility.
An exact incremental kernel preserves all172032 forecasts while cutting warm
weight-update time about73x immediate and18x delayed. This is not a daemon
serving or whole-goal success; batch memory and persistence limits remain.
All seven goals stay open. No threshold edits or production promotion.

[Strong Brier aggregation](docs/experiments/mmm-strong-brier-v1-report.md)
implements and verifies the published binary substitution, distinct from the
older conservative linear mixer. It does not rescue the broad experiment:
608/672 protection and 7/96 gains; matched eta2 linear is numerically better.
Exact selection decomposition also identifies additional-head and approximation
gaps. Those oracle gaps are not proof that a learned router can exploit them.
No delayed theorem inherited, no rate sweep justified. All seven goals open.

[Unguarded matched ablation](docs/experiments/mmm-segment-unrestricted-v1-report.md)
improves pooled Brier .157516545 -> .156123153, but passes only 606/672
protection and 7/96 recovery screens. Removing the pointwise restriction alone
is not a rescue. Five of six Markov protection failures have favorable means
but wide intervals; the Boolean-control gap is larger. Preserve that distinction
and diagnose it before another parameter change. Replays and as-of checks pass.
The new certificate-driven calibration paper offers a useful regularizer,
not a direct Brier guarantee without its unobservable target mismatch term.
All seven goals remain open; these are consumed eight-index screens.

[Guarded segmentation and oracle audit](docs/experiments/mmm-segment-guard-v1-report.md):
segmentation improves changing-scenario Brier but retains stationary harm.
More importantly, even an oracle choosing ANY binary forecast under the current
.01 worst-outcome pointwise guard cannot attain the original .005 recovery gain
in 26/32 measured changing cells. This is an evaluation-only bound on consumed
data, not a deployable learner or general impossibility theorem. Stop tuning
weights within that restriction; investigate uncertainty-aware expected-risk
control matching the original criteria. Equal-prior static/segment averaging
was already tested and failed in v120; do not repeat it as a new lead.
Screen and oracle replays match exactly. All seven goals remain open.

[Contextual mixture screen](docs/experiments/mmm-context-mixture-v1-report.md)
failed quality versus its matchedglobalcontrol despite passing solver/as-of
tests and a positivecontrol. Reference cost~14us/frame excludesbasefits.
Re-audited originalgoals and15existingheads: segment64 has shiftbenefits but
stationaryharm. Next test that relevantmodel tradeoff with matchedstatic64
controls rather than more contextualfeaturegrids. Allseven remainopen.

[Direct-mixture screen](docs/experiments/mmm-direct-mixture-v1-report.md):
four as-of ridge/current-covariance variants offer no clear guarded overall
gain and worsen changing-scenario performance. Tests/replay pass; ridge64
combiner is cheap but not an adaptation rescue. Bound saturation erases many
weight differences. Evaluator-only safe oracle leaves conditional headroom,
motivating a frozen smallcontext-mixture test rather than scalar retuning.
All seven goals remain open; no runtime or production promotion.

[Failure diagnosis / pointwise guard](docs/experiments/mmm-pointwise-guard-v1-report.md):
the prior gain decomposes mainly into forecast diversification. A research-only
per-forecast Brier bound removes >.01 harms but retains only28.23%of priorgain
(~.4251%vsMarkov), on consumeddata. No adaptation or completion claim. Tests,
replays and scalarbenchmark pass. Next investigate directmixture-loss learning
from primaryresearch while retaining safety and explicitutility controls.

[Independent replication completed](docs/experiments/mmm-spike-independent-v1-report.md):
frozencombinedpolicy improves wholeBrier .157516545->.155144958 (1.5056%),
roughlyties reset32overall, andimproves aggregate terminal64. But changing

[Sparse convergence diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-sparse-vrvm-v1-convergence-results.md)
completes 84 matched fits at a 1,024-step cap with exact 64-step prefixes,

numerical stop; forecasts still trail the strong controls. Fit cost rises to
4.17-4.25 seconds versus 295-297 ms on the same fixture. Do not continue a
cap grid or promote faster optimization as a quality rescue. The prior-only
diagnostic proves that just 0.00145% of the original coefficient prior lies
in [-1,1]; this is a modeling concern, not a proven sole cause of forecast
error. Next investigate justified finite-slab/global-local shrinkage with a
new inference contract, distinct from prior failed scalar/degree tuning.
Sources and remaining hypotheses are in `research/sparse-vrvm-prior-diagnostic.md`.

[Logged usefulness v1](docs/experiments/mmm-logged-gain-v1-results.md) separates

CS widths.56-.62 cannot resolve sub-.001 policy differences. No positive lower
bound appears; entropy still has the best realized mean. Independent172032
increment/256 final-interval checks pass. This is measurement infrastructure,
not a new policy or64 fresh datasets. Next test the source's time-varying
variance-adaptive off-policy bound without changing target or coverage; actual
policy improvement and prospective logging remain open. All seven goals open.

[Error-state/model-form factorial](docs/experiments/mmm-query-error-factorial-v1-results.md)
completes all four models and FAILS all eight forced/gated screens. Augmented
quadratic forced Brier.166668 is close to entropy.166630 but establishes no gain;
its gated.166809 loses to same-mask entropy.166192. Its within-pool R-squared
falls from.0803 training to-.0399 evaluation. Exact base control, target isolation,
solver checks and all replays pass. Do not increase capacity against this screen.
Next remove remaining sampled-future-input clairvoyance from oracle targets by
integrating the known generator input law, retaining the distinction from an
online acquisition policy. All seven goals remain open.

[Prequential evidence boundary](docs/experiments/mmm-prequential-projection-v1-results.md)
PASSES the component checks: all2688 records,86016 reconstructed historical
forecasts (max error1.81e-13),92770 independently checked feature values,5376
poisoning/equivalence checks and byte-exact projection replay. Twelve source-fit
fixtures pass under race. Delayed coverage is7-22 of32 labels, so missingness
coverage remains explicit. Next freeze and run the error-state/nonlinear-model
factorial. This verifies inputs, not critic efficacy or any whole goal; all seven
remain open and no production or paper promotion is made.

[Centered query ranking](docs/experiments/mmm-query-centered-critic-v1-results.md)
improves evaluation within-pool R-squared from-.04457 to.00875 but FAILS both
advancement screens. Forced Brier.167149 still trails entropy.166630; gated
.167068 trails same-mask entropy.166444 at588 queries. Mean-value head and old
controls are unchanged; oracle isolation, extreme-target regression and replay
pass. A [primary-source check](docs/experiments/mmm-query-critic-literature-followup.md)
points to a different lead: strictly as-of issued-forecast error evidence and
nonlinear learner-state interactions. Build the leakage-safe evidence projection
before testing that factorial, rather than sweeping the failed critic's penalty.
All seven goals remain open; no production promotion.

[Frozen ten-feature query critic](docs/experiments/mmm-query-critic-v1-results.md)
FAILS both forced/gated advancement screens. Phase1 forced Brier.167675 versus
entropy.166630; gated.167534 versus same-mask entropy.166496 at605 queries.
Phase0-only fit, oracle-field traps, target poisoning, independent solver and
byte-exact summary replay pass. Component p99 selection52.96us excludes Bayesian
feature generation and serving. The diagnostic finds positive between-pool but
negative within-pool R-squared even in training. Next isolate ranking's objective
with within-pool centered learning and a separate pool-value head, not a feature
or penalty sweep on phase1. This remains a hypothesis; all seven goals stay open.

[Outcome-averaged query opportunity](docs/experiments/mmm-regime-query-counterfactual-v1-results.md)
completes all2688 paired inputs and1098144 forecast-entry checks. Original
branches match the envelope artifact exactly; summary replay and supplemental
single-label race isolation pass. Beneficial expected queries exist in1063/1344
delayed runs, but219 have no beneficial paid action. Oracle expected Brier.164373
versus frozen entropy's outcome-averaged.167031 shows opportunity beyond lucky
answers, not an online rescue. Next investigate a predecision-only expected-value
critic with offline synthetic supervision and abstention, without exposing
generator truth or case/seed identity at inference. All seven goals remain open.

[All-candidate query opportunity](docs/experiments/mmm-regime-query-envelope-v1-results.md)
now measures all 11965 branches across 2688 records. Race/leakage contracts and
549072 forecast-entry checks pass, selected-policy forecasts match previous
artifacts exactly, and summary replay is byte-identical. Some paid query helps
in 1314/1344 delayed runs. Best-paid hindsight Brier .161626 versus entropy
.166657 shows available opportunity, NOT a deployable selector: it exploits
future truth and realized answer luck. Next separate those two information
advantages by averaging counterfactual label outcomes under the generator law,
without exposing that law to an online policy. All seven goals remain open.

[Actual queried-label diagnostic completed](docs/experiments/mmm-regime-query-outcome-v1-results.md):
all2688 runs/333312 forecasts audited; full Go and summary replay byte-identical.
Joint-value Brier.167328 versus random.166980 and entropy.166657 on the delayed
equal-cell average; no cell establishes a joint-value advantage over either.
97.69% of its purchases change fitted support, so this is not an unwired-label
problem. The [disjoint-probe control](docs/experiments/mmm-regime-query-disjoint-v1-results.md)
now completes that isolation: 2688 records and 666624 forecast checks pass,
with unchanged controls and byte-identical summary replay. Removing origin
overlap changes 772/1344 delayed selections but Brier only .167328->.167301;
no cell establishes an advantage over random, entropy, or the original selector.
Overlap removal alone is not an established rescue. All seven whole goals
remain open; no production promotion.

[Exact batched regime queries PASS component gates](docs/experiments/mmm-regime-query-batch-results.md):
tiny exhaustive, full63-label and six replay views agree with explicit refits;
maximum discrepancy2.56e-13. Eight-query cold median724.8ms->46.84ms (15.48x),
allocation volume falls95.7%; race and query-order checks pass. Not a quality
or loaded-serving result. Next run the frozen all21-case one-decision diagnostic
with actual paid-label refitting, before full-stream acquisition. All seven
whole goals remain open.

[Joint regime-query conditioning verified](docs/experiments/mmm-regime-query-component-results.md):
96 exhaustive probe/query checks, full63-plus-one capacity/ownership tests,
four concurrent queries and12 replay views pass under race. Unlike the earlier
fixed-expert query utility, a hypothetical label conditions the actual regime
model at its past origin. Efficacy remains untested. Eight candidates cost about
0.65s plus a40ms base fit; next test the frozen exact batched-moment computation
against the retained slow reference before a full acquisition run. All seven
whole goals remain open.

[Bounded one-change snapshot screen FAILED](docs/experiments/mmm-one-change-v1-results.md):
65/72 non-harm and12/24 gains; all gains are against no-change, none against
existing full segmentation. All1152 snapshots/36864 forecasts/57600 weights
independently checked; replay identical. Race contracts pass; isolated full64
fit37.52-37.63ms, not end-to-end latency. No promotion or prior sweep. Next inspect
joint regime-aware acquisition that actually refits the model, including cap,
delay and hypothetical-refit cost, against already-tested acquisition work.
All seven whole goals remain open.

[Count-matched regime diagnostic completed](docs/experiments/mmm-regime-match-v1-results.md):
all1152 snapshots/368640 probabilities independently checked; replay identical.
Current-regime labels beat equal-count random subsets in all12 contaminated
cells, while truncating stationary evidence harms both phases at delayed160.
This is hindsight mechanism evidence, not an online rescue. Next test the frozen
[bounded one-change model](docs/experiments/mmm-one-change-protocol.md) against
the existing no-change AND full segmentation models. All seven goals remain open.

[Family-evidence stream FAILED](docs/experiments/mmm-family-evidence-v1-results.md):
all2688 trajectories and all four variants evaluated. Primary149/168 non-harm,
0/16 gains; faster64-window157/168 and1/16. Independent beta-integral audit
checks215040 window fits and2.75million probabilities, maximum error7.11e-14;
scoring replay identical. Strong majority-to-parity regression remains despite
correct posterior arithmetic. Next inspect per-fit regime composition with a
sample-count-matched hindsight diagnostic; no prior tuning or adoption. All
seven full goals remain open.

[Same-window family adapter verified](docs/experiments/mmm-family-evidence-component-results.md).
Prior-work inspection found the simpler parameterized family model already
exists, not just in segmentation. Reused its marginal-evidence primitive and
verified exact-model agreement, beta integrals and streamed as-of contracts
under race. Aggregation15.47-16.43us; full two-family fit7.29-7.56ms on one fixture.
V91/V92 failures remain; current.95 prior is distinct from V92's.9 and remains
frozen. No efficacy collection yet. Next collector/scorer and full four-variant
run; all seven whole goals remain open.

[Faster-bank decomposition completed](docs/experiments/mmm-cadence-bank-v1-results.md):
all2688 trajectories,10752 additive decompositions, source/loss matches and
byte-identical replay. Reverse-transition served.098235 versus single-switch
hindsight.068195 leaves substantial selection opportunity, not a learned rule.
Stubborn soft cases also retain bank limitations. Next inspect/reuse existing
segment family-evidence machinery for a simpler same-window update; its prior
full-segmentation failures remain constraints, not erased history. All seven
goals open; no production changes.

[Faster refit diagnostic completed](docs/experiments/mmm-refit-cadence-v1-results.md):
all2688 records;168/168 non-harm and2/16 terminal gains. Many full-trajectory
gains are real within the exploratory intervals, but the broad screen fails
and fits cost4x invocations. Independent mixer/origin/paired-signature audit
checks6.88million probabilities; scoring replay is byte-identical. All16 mean
targets have Bayes-floor headroom. Next decompose the faster bank's remaining
error into forecast availability versus selection, without post-hoc gate changes.
All seven goals remain open; no production promotion.

[Query-burst rescue FAILED](docs/experiments/mmm-query-burst-v1-results.md):
all2688 trajectories collected;663/672 non-harm and0/64 gains. All paid delayed
arms use31 labels. Training lead improves41.23%->61.90% and wait18.56->9.27
frames, without reliable quality gain. Independent mixer/selection/fit audit
checks24.08million probabilities; scoring replay is byte-identical. Next isolate
current expert publication cadence from evidence availability, with explicit
extra-compute accounting. No threshold tuning or production promotion; all seven
goals remain open.

[Evidence-triggered query timing](docs/experiments/mmm-query-burst-protocol.md)
is wired into the research learner. [Integration contracts](docs/experiments/mmm-query-burst-contracts.md)
passed under race: original12 fixtures, new4 fixtures/6 paid timing-selector
arms,12 poisoned prefixes, and768 scheduler combinations. Fixed a caught
expired-mixer delivery boundary without removing the late training evidence.
No quality collection yet. Seven-arm collector compiled; independent scorer
and full2688-record run are next. All seven goals remain open.

[Delay/missingness factorial](docs/experiments/mmm-feedback-factorial-v1-results.md)
finds stronger evidence for delay cost in selected shifts than for missingness
alone. All1344 paired trajectories/new-arm fits scored and audited. Next test
WHEN a fixed query budget is spent using only arrived evidence, not another
fixed-clock selection score. No new policy validated; all seven goals open.

[Paired whole-learner feedback diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-feedback-pairs-v120-results.md)
verifies1344 paired Initial/X/Y/Q histories. Immediate complete feedback materially
helps some shifts (case20 Brier.102272->.077009 in phase1), not uniformly.
Next separate delay from missingness with the frozen2x2 protocol; no new policy
validated and all seven full directions remain open.

[Boundary-aligned acquisition FAILED quality](docs/experiments/mmm-acquisition-boundary-v1-results.md):
training lead improves40.77%->58.14%, but669/672 non-harm and0/64 gains pass.
All2688 runs and race/as-of checks complete. Default timing remains unchanged.
Next compare whole-learner immediate versus delayed feedback on verified paired
trajectories before further acquisition tuning. All seven directions remain open.

[Training-lead diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-training-lead-results.md) finds
only~41% of paid labels reach a fit as new/earlier evidence. Boundary queries
wait31 frames after reveal and never advance naturally delivered training labels.
A [frozen timing protocol](docs/experiments/mmm-acquisition-boundary-protocol.md)
moves only those7 queries one clock earlier, with unchanged pools/counts checked.
Implementation/efficacy testing next; all seven whole directions remain open.

[Full acquisition-to-training FAILED](docs/experiments/mmm-acquisition-train-v1-results.md):
all2688 runs complete,504/504 non-harm but0/48 gains. Independent scoring verifies
13.76million forecasts and fit/query contracts. Paid labels change experts, but
reliable equal-cost superiority remains absent. Next quantify effective training
lead time: some natural labels may arrive before the next scheduled fit anyway.
All seven whole directions remain open.

[Acquisition-to-training integration PASS](docs/experiments/mmm-acquisition-train-contracts.md)
reproduces original four-expert forecasts on12 reference fixtures and verifies
paid labels affect later fits without future-data leaks under race. This closes
the fixed-expert test limitation at component level, not an efficacy claim.
Full2688-run quality collection/scoring is next; all seven whole goals remain open.

[Predictive-risk acquisition FAILED](docs/experiments/mmm-predictive-query-v120-results.md):
840/840 non-harm,0/80 gains. Oracle headroom does not make the mean targets
trivially impossible, but three targeted acquisition utilities have not rescued the mixer.
Suspend fixed-tape utility tuning; next let acquired labels train underlying
experts under matched budgets and publication cadence. All seven goals remain open.

[Exact current-state information FAILED](docs/experiments/mmm-current-info-v120-results.md):
672/672 non-harm,0/64 gains.89.91% of paid choices match the previous heuristic.
Temporal marginalization is verified but does not supply meaningful recovery.
Next separate expert-identity uncertainty from predictive decision value, and
mixer-only interventions from representation retraining. No production promotion;
all seven whole directions remain open.

[Timely-label acquisition FAILED](docs/experiments/mmm-expedite-v120-results.md):
504/504 non-harm but0/48 improvement checks. Equal-cost random acquisition beats
the disagreement heuristic on the difficult delayed example. Next distinguish
old-event disagreement from exact information about the current switching state;
the current score is not automatically that conditional mutual information.
All seven whole goals remain open; no production promotion.

[Evidence availability diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-evidence-availability-v120-results.md)
finds both delayed useful evidence and older conflicting evidence. Recent32
supports the hindsight winner in25/25 parity-to-majority opportunities, but only
10 exceed a descriptive log(19) marker;64 includes8 nonpositive comparisons.
This supports testing timely, equal-cost observation, not adopting a tuned short
window. Hindsight selection is not a deployable certificate. All seven goals open.

[Switch-distribution test FAILED](docs/experiments/mmm-switch-distribution-v120-results.md):
both frozen priors pass334/336 non-harm checks but only0/64 and1/64 improvement
checks. [Hindsight switching diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-switch-budget-v120-results.md)
shows few-switch headroom, not evidence-admissible recovery. Next investigate
discriminating evidence availability and observation policy rather than tuning
another mixer on the same consumed tapes. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Batched private serving FAILED](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_BATCH_LOAD_RESULTS.md)
both full runs:109/1236 acknowledgements recovered, but deadlines and quarantine
prevent sustained service. Correctness components do not complete goal6. Retain
these failures and return attention to learning/real-task validation. All seven
whole directions remain open.

[Private construction selection](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_SELECTION_RESULTS.md)
matches six backend selector captures in exact ordered output under race, including
short-list, window cap, diversity and fill behavior. Actual construction traversal,
reciprocal links and overflow pruning remain to compose. All seven goals open.

[Matched backend traversal](research/public-task-pilot/LAYERED_BACKEND_COMPARISON.md)
finds backend search floor ef400 from2*EfConstruction. At matched breadth both
return identical top10 sets and319/320 exact hits per6400 snapshot, but private
search needs up to5990 evaluations. Prior ef100/200 failures remain; compatibility
is not cheap scaling or insertion completion. All seven goals remain open.

[Held-out traversal probes](research/public-task-pilot/LAYERED_HELDOUT_RESULTS.md)
FAIL at6400: recall71.875% ef100 and91.25% ef200 against frozen95% target, while

on identical states/queries before tuning breadth or adopting this traversal.
All seven whole goals remain open.

[Immutable traversal smoke test](research/public-task-pilot/LAYERED_SEARCH_RESULTS.md)
retrieves317/320 exact top10 neighbors on32 easy self-vector probes, zero self
misses. ef100 still needs up to3016 metric evaluations. This is approximate-search
infrastructure, not general retrieval validation or insertion completion. Add
held-out probes and connection selection next; all seven goals remain open.

[Abrupt deletion recovery](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_DELETE_CRASH_RESULTS.md)
passes six controlled child kills: staged deletion recovers revision1/target,
post-commit pre-ack kill recovers revision2/deletion. This adds process-crash
evidence, not power-loss/general crash coverage or full ANN recovery. Production
is untouched; all seven whole goals remain open.

[Real deletion recovery checks](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_DELETE_RECOVERY_RESULTS.md)
pass real WithTx success/rollback/lost-ack/panic-after-commit cases and clean reopen
under race. Authoritative counts/revisions/vectors match; recovery uses known tiny
fixture IDs, not a general enumerator or full ANN rebuild. Abrupt crash and durable
serving/insertion remain open, as do all seven whole goals.

[Private deletion writer](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_DELETE_WRITER_RESULTS.md)
publishes graph/summary/revision together after callback success and quarantines
uncertain outcomes; targeted race tests pass. Persistence is mocked, leases are
not integrated and insertion is absent. Real libravdb transaction/reopen testing
is next. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Full private deletion cost](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_DELETE_COST_RESULTS.md)
measures67-291us including discovery/metric/repair/snapshot work on four captured
cases, but181-721KB allocation per delete. This supports integration; it excludes
metadata, persistence, readers and sustained GC. No serving-latency guarantee.
All seven whole goals remain open.

[Composed private deletion](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_DELETE_RESULTS.md)
matches all16 full backend deletes using actual cosine calculations, per-level
repair, target retirement and entry-summary updates. Capture/race tests pass.
This is graph-only: ID/revision metadata, durability, coherent publication and
insertion discovery remain absent. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Private reconnection](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_RECONNECT_RESULTS.md)
implements bounded one-level lazy repair and matches five backend branch captures
using exact captured distances under race. This isolates update semantics, not
metric runtime. Per-level composition, retirement/entry metadata and durable
publication remain required. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Private incoming discovery](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_INCOMING_RESULTS.md)
discovers owned edge-removal edits from immutable backlinks, matching16 backend
stage captures. Budget/ownership race tests pass. This is not a whole deletion:
private reconnection, retirement/entry metadata and durability remain to compose.
All seven whole goals remain open.

[Fixed lazy-repair follow-up](research/public-task-pilot/LAZY_REPAIR_FOLLOWUP_RESULTS.md)
passes all8 additional comparisons with unchanged thresholds and matching state
hashes; deletion totals70-89% lower. Original failed screen remains recorded.
Carry lazy preparation into research private-update design; no full-system or
sustained-load guarantee. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Lazy repair branch controls](research/public-task-pilot/LAZY_REPAIR_CASES_RESULTS.md)
match dense/sparse/mixed/single/canceled state and errors under race. Necessary
