correctness but leaves the failed timing screen and private-update integration
unresolved. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Lazy neighbor repair](research/public-task-pilot/LAZY_REPAIR_RESULTS.md)
defers unused distance matrices: exact controlled states, pair calls21131->96,
deletion time73-89% lower. Overall frozen screen still fails one seed-phase5.84%
regression; deletion/reclamation race tests pass. Retain promising candidate with

[Summary cost screen](research/public-task-pilot/SUMMARY_COST_RESULTS.md)
fails3/4 comparisons despite exact state-hash matches. N800 gains~1-2%; N6400
gains vary9-25%. Retain architectural candidate, not a throughput rescue; stop
threshold tuning and return to full private update/repair and sustained-load
integration. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Backend summary substitution](research/public-task-pilot/SUMMARY_BACKEND_RESULTS.md)
matches all32 control operations exactly while replacing808/6408-slot scans with
one summary lookup each. This is a serial-initialization backend overlay; batch,

No sustained-load or production rescue claimed. All seven goals remain open.

[Persistent replacement summary](research/public-task-pilot/ENTRY_SUMMARY_RESULTS.md)
matches an independent scan over3000 randomized updates and the controlled HNSW
capture, including actual replacement ordinals. At most33 path nodes per edit,
constant-time selection; not yet maintained/published with the real graph. A
packed-state harness assertion was corrected. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Identical-state work control](research/public-task-pilot/SERIAL_WORK_CONTROL_RESULTS.md)
uses serial fixture initialization: control/probe match all32 operations exactly.
Forced entry deletions scan808/6408 registry slots despite62/87 changed records.
This enables controlled private-summary testing, not a production initialization
change or throughput rescue. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Actual HNSW work probe](research/public-task-pilot/HNSW_WORK_PROBE_RESULTS.md)
measures741-2304 deletion pair calls matching operation-local neighbor sets;
insertion adjacency reads reach hundreds of distinct nodes. Cross-run graphs
differ even in fresh uninstrumented control, so fixed-seed topology equivalence
is not established. Force entry deletion and establish identical-state controls
before private adaptation/performance comparison. All seven goals remain open.

[Private update work audit](research/public-task-pilot/PRIVATE_UPDATE_WORK_AUDIT.md)
finds real deletion work omitted by supplied-edit timing:766-2266 derived pair
distances plus an entry-point registry scan. Changed-record counts do not bound
discovery work. Instrument real access/distance work before private adaptation;
consider a persistent entry-point summary. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Bounded layered owner](research/public-task-pilot/LAYERED_OWNER_RESULTS.md)
adds capped leases, retained-version admission and one candidate, with race-tested
release/publication behavior. This is a version-count limit, not a RAM bound;
mutex-copy reads and allocating commit remain research limitations. Actual ANN
edit discovery and durable serving are still absent. All seven goals remain open.

[Immutable path pruning](research/public-task-pilot/LAYERED_PRUNING_RESULTS.md)
fixes confirmed deleted-path retention and missing-delete allocations. Churn,
historical snapshots, captured replay and race tests pass. Supplied-edit benchmark
is138-139us in separate runs, not a throughput improvement. Prior source hashes
are preserved in archived copies. Enforceable lifetime budgets and real private
ANN updates remain required; all seven whole goals remain open.

[Retained storage accounting](research/public-task-pilot/LAYERED_STORAGE_RESULTS.md)
finds1.63-2.88% added reachable graph storage for current plus eight selected old
readers at6400 records in the short capture. This excludes allocator/GC/string
source costs and is not a total-memory bound. Deletion demonstrably retains empty
radix paths; prune these and add enforceable lifetime accounting before adoption.
Actual ANN preparation and sustained-load rescue remain open, as do all goals.

[Go layered preparation](research/public-task-pilot/LAYERED_GO_RESULTS.md)
passes captured state replay with actual float-vector hash checks under race.
Per-level sharing avoids57.64-61.92% of sampled adjacency copying. Supplied-edit
preparation measures126-128us for one93-record deletion; no edit-discovery or
durability cost is included. Retained-memory accounting and actual private ANN
updates remain unimplemented. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Layered ownership replay](research/public-task-pilot/LAYERED_OWNERSHIP_RESULTS.md)
passes32 captured updates and34 historical snapshots, including layered backlinks
and global state. It shares vector-hash handles, not actual vector storage, and
does not discover updates. Whole-record copying touches up to16274 link entries;
test per-level sharing and retained-memory accounting before dynamic adaptation.
Long-load failures remain unresolved. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Real HNSW net-change audit](research/public-task-pilot/HNSW_TOUCH_RESULTS.md)
finds31-113 changed logical records per sampled update, including backlinks and
one global-state change. Layered links exceed the prototype's flat representation.
A borrowed-string harness crash was corrected/preserved; corrected audit passes.
Replay richer persistent state next; no universal update bound. All seven open.

[Persistent graph ownership](research/public-task-pilot/GRAPH_VERSION_RESULTS.md)
implements private path-copy edits and root publication, preserving historical
traversal while sharing untouched branches. This is bounded per-edit preparation,
not HNSW or bounded total history memory. Measure actual ANN touched state and
add resource accounting before adaptation. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Incremental ownership audit](research/public-task-pilot/INCREMENTAL_OWNERSHIP_AUDIT.md)
confirms HNSW has mutable updates/reclamation but no prepared transaction delta
or historical graph root. Existing backend deletion/reclamation tests pass three
race runs; upstream HEAD is unchanged. Next test bounded persistent graph-state
ownership before adapting updates. All seven whole research goals remain open.

[Schedule model and dynamic-index research](research/public-task-pilot/RUN_SCHEDULE_RESEARCH.md)
shows binary tiering cuts rewrite entries~9.2x but still needs an oversized merge

incremental-update leads, not drop-in snapshot guarantees. Audit prepared graph
ownership before another backend variant. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Long partial-tail growth](research/public-task-pilot/LONG_PARTIAL_RUN_RESULTS.md)
FAILS both4096-write arms:285 capacity rejections,131 read errors and25 late reads.
All7907 acknowledgements reopen; no self misses/build errors. Merges grow past
800ms, so the512-write pass is not sustained capacity. Test size/sub-corpus
bounded consolidation and separately trace admission. All seven goals open.

[Partial-tail durable load](research/public-task-pilot/PARTIAL_RUN_LOAD_RESULTS.md)
PASSES all six arms:3072 acknowledgements reopen, no request/build errors, late
responses or self misses. Partial merges26-76ms avoid whole-history pauses.
Current graphs increase to10 (explicit resource change); tail records still
grow. Longer growth and update/delete load remain required. All seven goals open.

[Compaction-inclusive run load](research/public-task-pilot/RUN_LOAD_RESULTS.md)
FAILS overall:800 passes, but3200/6400 incur773 total capacity rejections and two
self misses. All2299 acknowledgements reopen; no late responses/build errors.
Whole-history consolidation still stalls draining for0.5-1.4s. Test bounded
sub-corpus or concurrent-flush consolidation, not a larger delta. All seven open.

[Retirement-aware publication](research/public-task-pilot/RUN_PUBLICATION_RESULTS.md)
reserves graph capacity before consolidation visibility and serializes current
lease acquisition with retirement. Targeted race tests pass, including unchanged
state on insufficient capacity and distinct old/new answers. Loaded latency and
complete shutdown remain untested. All seven whole research goals remain open.

[Bounded run leases](research/public-task-pilot/RUN_LEASE_RESULTS.md)
preserve existing readers, block reacquisition of retired graphs, and charge
resources through asynchronous close. Race tests cover blocking/failed cleanup
and shutdown. Capacity reservation must still be wired atomically to publication;
the cap counts graphs, not batches. All seven whole research goals remain open.

[Whole-history consolidation](research/public-task-pilot/RUN_CONSOLIDATION_RESULTS.md)
replaces accumulated runs with one graph while preserving writes arriving during
construction and retaining historical answers. Targeted race tests pass. Replaced
graphs are returned alive: bounded leases/retirement and compaction-inclusive
load are next, not yet validated. All seven whole research goals remain open.

[Captured-version flush](research/public-task-pilot/RUN_FLUSH_RESULTS.md)
preserves concurrent delete/overwrite/insert updates while publishing a built
run without changing semantic revision. Targeted race tests pass. Unknown build
cleanup keeps its slot charged; reconciliation, consolidation and bounded reader
leases remain missing. No production adoption. All seven goals stay open.

[Durable run writer](research/public-task-pilot/RUN_WRITER_RESULTS.md)
preflights complete query state before persistence and quarantines uncertain
commits. Callback tests cover blocked visibility, owned vectors, historical
snapshots and late cancellation. Flush/consolidation and bounded graph retirement
are still missing; no concurrent-load rescue claimed. All seven goals open.

[Buffered delta over runs](research/public-task-pilot/RUN_DELTA_RESULTS.md)
passes real-graph shadowing, historical-view and before/after-flush equivalence
tests. Newer overwrites survive the modeled flush. This is a query snapshot,
not a concurrent durable publisher; full-manifest planning cost is explicit.
Durable append/drain and compaction lifecycle remain next. All seven goals open.

[Immutable-run static screen](research/public-task-pilot/IMMUTABLE_RUN_SCREEN_RESULTS.md)
PASSES all16 stages: small-run build+plan16-29ms versus full rebuild79-100ms,
exact top10 on all512 paired probes, and worst read0.648ms. Nine-run reads cost
about1.77x. Consolidation debt and durable concurrent load are not included;
implement/test those before adoption. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Real immutable-run graphs](research/public-task-pilot/IMMUTABLE_RUN_GRAPH_RESULTS.md)
pass small exact-oracle update/delete tests with both ordinary and bulk/candidate
backends, including three race-enabled full overlay suites. Manifest/graph pairs
remain static; durable publication, cross-run leases and sustained compaction
are not implemented. No throughput rescue claimed. All seven goals remain open.

[Bounded immutable-run merge](research/public-task-pilot/IMMUTABLE_RUN_DIRECTION.md)
implements latest-version/tombstone ownership and rejects insufficient older
prefixes. Exact-oracle tests cover updates, demotions and hidden candidates.
This opens a less-frequent reconstruction direction; real graphs, durable
publication, bounded compaction debt and unchanged load gates remain untested.
All seven goals remain open.

[Integrated atomic pair table](research/public-task-pilot/BUILD_PAIR_TABLE_RESULTS.md)
passes real-distance/lifecycle/race and partition exact-oracle checks, but shows
no reliable construction win:77-79ms with caching versus76-86ms without, while
whole-base builds regress. Small-build hit rate33.66%. No adoption or load run.
Investigate avoiding repeated shard reconstruction, not another lookup-only
speed claim. All seven goals remain open.

[Atomic pair-table kernel](research/public-task-pilot/BUILD_PAIR_TABLE_KERNEL_RESULTS.md)
passes three race-enabled kernel runs and avoids per-lookup allocations. A
single-pair contention diagnostic reduces bookkeeping to about13ns versus
58-126ns for the map, but excludes real distance/insertion/locality costs.
Fresh-build integration and matched construction gates remain required; this
does not rescue the rejected map cache. All seven goals remain open.

[Integrated build-pair cache](research/public-task-pilot/BUILD_PAIR_CACHE_RESULTS.md)
is REJECTED for performance:39.30% hits but replacement builds rise74ms to365-375ms.
Bit-identical distance/lifecycle/race and exact-oracle checks pass. No load run or
adoption follows the failed construction gate. Investigate lookup/locking/callback
overhead before any alternate cache design. All seven goals remain open.

[Real selected-pair observation](research/public-task-pilot/PAIR_OBSERVATION_RESULTS.md)
recognizes39.27% repeats in partition builds within65,536 tracked pairs, versus
4.07% for whole bases. Original distances still execute; no speedup or cache hit
rate is claimed. Exact-oracle and observer-lifecycle checks pass. Next integrate
the bounded cache and measure net cost. All seven goals remain open.

[Build-scoped pair cache kernel](research/public-task-pilot/BUILD_PAIR_CACHE_DIRECTION.md)
passes exact-value, capacity and concurrency tests. HNSW selection exposes stable
node IDs for a possible integration, but repeat-pair hit rate and net speedup are
unmeasured. Not wired into construction/serving; next instrument the real build
before claiming a rescue. All seven goals remain open.

[Private candidate-only retrieval](research/public-task-pilot/CANDIDATE_ONLY_RESULTS.md)
passes same-graph/public-payload and exact-oracle checks, but load still FAILS:
four of six arms pass,30 capacity failures and9 reader-admission errors remain.
All3042 acknowledgements reopen; no late responses or successful-read misses.
An isolated pinned-module copy leaves normal dependencies untouched. Construction
still takes~124ms; no robust throughput win claimed. All seven goals remain open.

[Bounded background retirement](research/public-task-pilot/ASYNC_RETIREMENT_RESULTS.md)
passes lifecycle/race tests but load still FAILS:24 capacity rejections,7 read
admission errors and1 late write; three of six arms pass. All3048 acknowledged
writes reopen. Final82-309ms cleanup drain is recorded, not hidden. Next investigate
immutable candidate-only hydration overhead. All seven goals remain open.

[Allocation caller audit](research/public-task-pilot/ALLOCATION_CALLER_RESULTS.md)
finds about37% of sampled allocation under close/checkpoint and32% under search,
including redundant payload hydration for this immutable adapter. Last-reader
release can run checkpoint synchronously. Upstream HEAD is unchanged. Test bounded
background retirement and an explicit immutable candidate-only interface; do not
remove public search payloads or durable checkpoints globally. All seven open.

[Partitioned CPU/allocation profiles](research/public-task-pilot/PARTITION_PROFILE_RESULTS.md)
show compaction near53% of sampled CPU at6400, dominated by distance/neighbor work,
with1.3-1.5GiB allocated per interval. Largest GC pauses stay under11ms, insufficient
to attribute previous100ms clusters. Inspect allocation/construction work and
trace admission separately before another patch. All seven goals remain open.

[Disposable-index no-sync screen](research/public-task-pilot/DERIVED_NOSYNC_RESULTS.md)
still FAILS overall: three of six arms pass,61 capacity errors and a3200-record
admission/deadline cluster remain. Authoritative durability is unchanged; all3011
acknowledgements reopen. Derived sync costs some time but does not explain most
construction cost. Profile remaining work before more tuning; all seven goals open.

[Partitioned growth load](research/public-task-pilot/PARTITION_LOAD_RESULTS.md)
is a partial rescue but FAILS overall: three of six arms pass;98 capacity write
errors,5 reader-admission errors and3 late responses remain. All2974 acknowledged
writes reopen; no successful self-query misses. At6400 average builds clear only
about85 entries/sec against100 writes/sec. Reduce derived-index construction cost
and investigate admission tails without raising budgets. All seven goals stay open.

[Partitioned index/lease publication](research/public-task-pilot/PARTITION_SERVING_RESULTS.md)
now preserves matching views/graphs through writes and compaction, with bounded
leases and retired handles. A reproduced unpublished-cleanup accounting bug is
fixed; three full race-suite repetitions pass. Next run unchanged growth load:
one shard compaction may free fewer than32 entries, so static timings do not
establish service capacity. All seven research goals remain open.

[Partitioned durable publication](research/public-task-pilot/PARTITION_DURABLE_RESULTS.md)
preserves one semantic revision and one total delta cap, including multi-shard
atomic publication, quarantine/recovery and concurrent compaction. Three full
researchindex race-suite repetitions pass, including real lost-ack/reopen checks.
Matching HNSW handle publication/retirement and offered-load tests remain next;
this is research infrastructure, not daemon integration. All seven goals stay open.

[Fixed-partition screen](research/public-task-pilot/PARTITION_RESULTS.md)
passes both discovery arms: eight partitions recover all self queries and128
exact-oracle probes; replacement builds85-87ms versus1081-1085ms, at1.73x median
query cost. This is static, uses more total ANN effort, and has no durable serving
publication. Implement/test global-version publication and unchanged offered load
before any adoption. All seven goals remain open.

[Full-breadth diagnosis](research/public-task-pilot/FULL_BREADTH_RESULTS.md)
finds the missed target stored exactly and reachable at breadth6400 in both fresh
graphs; default400 still misses it. Recovery costs about3.94x median query time
and uses corpus-sized breadth, so it is not a scalable rescue. Investigate better
construction or bounded-fanout index runs without relaxing recall/load criteria.
All seven goals remain open.

[Search-effort diagnosis](research/public-task-pilot/SEARCH_EFFORT_RESULTS.md)
finds no robust rescue: effective800/1600 still miss the known self target in
both6400-record graphs;3200 recovers it in only one while median cost rises2.73x.
The initial below-floor comparison is retained and explicitly corrected. No
serving parameter change. Investigate indexed presence/reachability separately
from rebuild throughput. All seven goals remain open.

[Static recall diagnosis](research/public-task-pilot/STATIC_RECALL_RESULTS.md)
reproduces seed-954's self-query miss in both fresh6400-record static builds,
without writes, delta or compaction. ANN nomination can fail independently of
the rebuild-capacity problem; the original seed-779 miss remains unattributed.
Candidate scores and source hashes are independently checked. This is diagnosis,
not a rescue. All seven goals remain open.

[Larger growth screen](research/public-task-pilot/GROWTH_RESULTS.md)
passes800-record controls but fails3200/6400:1071 writes hit capacity;6400 also
has3 self-query misses and4 late reads. All2001 acknowledgements survive reopen.
Whole-base rebuilds exceed320ms headroom. Investigate tiered immutable runs and
query/version correctness instead of enlarging the buffer. All seven goals
remain open.

[768-dimensional component screen](research/public-task-pilot/WIDE_VECTOR_RESULTS.md)
passes all8 arms:2048 reads and1024 writes meet100ms and reopen checks, with
background builds67-192ms. Larger/longer corpus growth and full EventFrame
contracts remain untested; this is not whole-goal completion. All seven
directions remain open.

[Bulk base construction](research/public-task-pilot/BULK_BASE_RESULTS.md)
passes all8 finite component arms:2048 reads and1024 writes meet100ms, all self-
queries and reopen checks pass. Background builds42-117ms replace1.34-6.18s and
remove401 prior capacity rejections. This is a research overlay, not full service
validation or large-corpus proof. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Compaction-inclusive load](research/public-task-pilot/COMPACTION_LOAD_RESULTS.md)
fails all8 arms:2048 reads pass100ms/self-hit checks, but401/1024 writes hit the
delta cap while graph builds take1.34-6.18s. All623 acknowledgements survive
reopen. Next test bulk derived-index construction, not a larger buffer that only
hides saturation. Initial setup failure is retained. All seven goals remain open.

[Combined durable serving](research/public-task-pilot/COMBINED_SERVING_RESULTS.md)
passes real transaction+HNSW+compaction+reopen tests with bounded active leases.
Released leases drop old resource references. Three race-enabled suite runs pass.
The12-record controlled test is correctness evidence, not service throughput or
semantic validation; sustained compaction-inclusive load is next. All seven
whole research directions remain open.

[Serving pair publication](research/public-task-pilot/SERVING_PAIR_RESULTS.md)
prebuilds HNSW before publishing a matching generation/index pair, preserves
intervening writes, and retains old readers. A reproduced cleanup-accounting bug
is fixed: closing graphs remain charged to retirement capacity. Combined real-
store performance and bounded lease admission remain untested/unimplemented.
All seven whole research goals remain open.

[Real HNSW generation adapter](research/public-task-pilot/HNSW_GENERATION_RESULTS.md)
passes small shadowing/old-view/mismatch tests and guarded durable compaction
tests. Three race-enabled suite repetitions pass. Atomic generation/index-pair
publication and reader retirement still need implementation; current tests
explicitly rebuild after compaction and are not a serving-performance rescue.
All seven whole directions remain open.

[Durable generation boundary](research/public-task-pilot/DURABLE_GENERATION_RESULTS.md)
now uses real synchronous transactions in an isolated fixture, testing rejection
before/staged commit, lost acknowledgement after commit, cancellation, quarantine
and reopen recovery. This is not a daemon replacement or ANN/performance rescue;
idempotency, actual index construction and service integration remain open.
All seven whole directions remain open.

[Generation lifecycle reference](research/public-task-pilot/GENERATION_PUBLICATION_RESULTS.md)
implements invisible prepare, allocation-free publication, abort, bounded delta
and compaction rebasing with preserved old views. It remains in-memory: no WAL,
ANN builder or service-performance rescue. Exact ranking is checked against a
committed-history oracle across lifecycle changes. All seven goals remain open.

[Immutable-index candidate](research/public-task-pilot/IMMUTABLE_INDEX_DIRECTION.md)
rejects naive post-WAL HNSW insertion because PreparedMutation.Commit must only
publish materialized state. A bounded base/delta merge kernel now has exact-oracle
and missing-prefix tests; generation publication/compaction remain unimplemented.
Latest phase recovery verifies74 failed writes absent and438 acknowledgements
present, with successful retries on copies. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Phase-aware queue](research/public-task-pilot/PHASE_QUEUE_RESULTS.md)
distinguishes proven non-entry from unknown commit. Ten race repetitions pass;
the32-arm run still fails every high-rate arm, with admitted write errors74/128
versus91 previously (descriptive cross-run). All906 returned journals and438
acknowledgements survive reopen. Upstream still has non-delta transaction rebuilds.
Continue scalable indexing research; this is partial, not whole-goal success.

[Index-scaling diagnostic](research/public-task-pilot/INDEX_SCALING_RESULTS.md)
confirms a practical corpus-dependent transaction cost:16 HNSW single writes
rise144ms->826ms from200->1600 events; flat stays72-80ms but searches slower.
All24 observations pass reopen checks and self-vector recall, not semantic
validation. Investigate scalable transactional indexing rather than promoting
flat from this small fixture. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Batch reconciliation](research/public-task-pilot/BATCH_RECONCILE_RESULTS.md)
finds all91 failed writes absent, all421 acknowledgements present, all91 retries
successful on copies, and snapshot/search agreement. Pinned libravdb transactional
HNSW rebuilds whole-collection indexes before durable commit: retrieval-frontier
caps do not bound that persistence work. Next distinguish safe non-entry from
unknown commit and measure index scaling before adopting a backend remedy.
All seven whole directions remain open.

[Already-pending batch service](research/public-task-pilot/BATCH_QUEUE_RESULTS.md)
still fails all high-rate arms; all low-rate arms pass. Clean rerun has123/256
read errors and0/128 write errors without admission, versus0/256 and91/128 with
admission. The apparent read gain includes stopped writes, not a full rescue.
Five index-stage timeout outcomes need reconciliation before relaxing the queue
guard. Next inspect retained failed writes and separate pre-commit rejection
from uncertain commit. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Bounded event transaction screen](research/public-task-pilot/EVENT_BATCH_RESULTS.md)
passes focused race/idempotency/parity/reopen checks. Isolated128-event median
falls926ms->69ms at batch16 (13.43x amortized), with normal synchronous durability.
This is not service latency: arrivals, queueing and journal writes are excluded.
Next test already-pending batching with individual deadlines; never wait150ms
to fill16 at100 writes/sec. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Atomic residual allocator](research/public-task-pilot/ATOMIC_SERVICE_RESULTS.md)
passes race/cancellation/parity and4ms skew tests, improves the cheap lookup
helper2.72x, and preserves108 public service outputs. The durable high-rate
rescue still FAILS: admitted106/256 read errors and84/128 write errors versus
110/79 previously. All lower-rate arms pass; all high-rate arms fail. Next
investigate bounded transactional write batching with unchanged durability and
idempotency. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Trace and residual-dispatch experiment](research/public-task-pilot/DURABLE_TRACE_RESULTS.md)
attributes about71-73% of recorded scheduler delay to residual channel loading.
Fixed strides preserve outputs and improve the cheap-lookup helper2.62x, but a
skewed-cost counterexample regresses4ms->25ms. Do not adopt it generally. Next
test atomic next-index allocation to retain load balancing without handoffs.
Allocation remains distributed across string/candidate/metadata work. All seven
whole goals remain open.

[Durable CPU profile](research/public-task-pilot/DURABLE_CPU_RESULTS.md)
completes256 correct read returns. After incremental diversity, sampled CPU is
dominated by runtime wakeups (about49-51% flat), with substantial memory-management
work;128-call allocation volume is1.18/1.33GB. These are not pure I/O timings or
proof of a scheduler bug. Next attribute goroutine creation and allocation sites
before changing search concurrency. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Zero coalescing delay ablation](research/public-task-pilot/ZERO_WINDOW_RESULTS.md)
FAILS the durable high-rate rescue: admission has116/256 read errors and82/128
write errors versus110/79 previously. Synchronous durability was retained;
757returned journals and394acknowledged writes survive reopen. All lower-rate
arms pass, all high-rate arms fail. Reject this as a sufficient rescue and
investigate remaining read/transaction cost. All seven full goals remain open.

[Durable phase diagnostic](research/public-task-pilot/DURABLE_PHASE_RESULTS.md)
measures384 operations: lower-rate journal commits average about6ms, event puts
about9ms, search1-2ms and remaining read work6-8ms. High-rate failures spend
about96-100ms waiting for admission. Pinned storage's deliberate WAL coalescing
is a testable lead, not yet the proven cause; preserve synchronous durability
in any ablation. All seven full directions remain open.

[Durable cancellation reconciliation](research/public-task-pilot/DURABLE_RECONCILE_RESULTS.md)
audits copies of32 retained stores: all111 failed writes are absent, all401
acknowledged writes are present, and111 idempotent retries succeed. Direct/search
sets agree and32 fresh recalls work before retries. Original hashes unchanged.
This finite audit finds no residual corruption; it does not prove rollback or
crash safety. Next measure durable phase costs; the throughput failure and all
seven whole goals remain open.

[Durable fixed-arrival test](research/public-task-pilot/DURABLE_OPENLOOP_RESULTS.md)
passes all50-read/s+25-write/s conditions but fails all200-read/s+100-write/s
conditions with the real local libravdb adapter. High-rate admission still has
110/256 read errors and79/128 write errors despite zero stale rejections.
All777 returned journals and401 acknowledged writes survive orderly reopen.
Failed-write outcomes and durable phase costs remain unverified; investigate
those before choosing a storage rescue. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Incremental service rescue](research/public-task-pilot/INCREMENTAL_SERVICE_RESULTS.md)
preserves all108 saved public outputs exactly (except timing) and passes every
admitted fixed-arrival arm:512reads+256writes,zero failures/stale rejections,
maxread20.136ms/maxwrite11.487ms. High-rate unadmitted experimental visible writes
still fail39reads; the combination matters. This is a finite memory-store rescue,
not production or whole-goal completion. Durable storage and longer independent
arrival validation remain next; all seven directions stay open.

[CPU-guided incremental diversity](research/public-task-pilot/INCREMENTAL_DIVERSITY_RESULTS.md)
finds repeated diversity comparisons dominate sampled CPU (80% experimental,
58% control). An exact incremental maximum matches generated reference outputs
and benchmarks about7x faster for the isolated200-item diversity operation.
Production Select remains unchanged. Next integrate via a new overlay, replay
saved public results and rerun fixed-arrival load; no throughput rescue claimed
yet. All seven full directions stay open.

[Fixed-arrival admission screen](research/public-task-pilot/TASK_LEXICAL_OPENLOOP_RESULTS.md)
passes all admitted50-read/s+25-write/s arms but fails all200-read/s+100-write/s
arms. At the higher rate admission has112/256 read errors and83/128 write errors,
versus68/256 and0 without admission, despite eliminating stale rejections.
The coarse gate is not a general throughput rescue. Independent arrivals and
100ms arrival-relative deadlines expose queueing hidden by closed-loop pacing.
Next investigate compatible-mutation admission and bounded batching; durable
backend validation remains. All seven full research directions remain open.

[Admission lifecycle component](research/public-task-pilot/ADMISSION_COMPONENT_RESULTS.md)
passes twenty race-enabled repetitions covering queued cancellation, error/panic
release, active cancellation and reader/writer exclusion. A negative control
shows separate gates do not protect shared state. This new research package is
not installed in the daemon; its service integration, durable writes and
open-loop load remain next. No new latency claim; all seven goals remain open.

[Request-lease finite scheduling rescue](research/public-task-pilot/TASK_LEXICAL_LEASE_RESULTS.md)
completes512 leased reads+256 writes without failures/stale rejections. At200
candidates, visible-write experimental p95 falls159-161ms->30-31ms; writer wait
rises toabout24ms and is included. Largest leased reader47.193ms. This is a
runner-only admission prototype, not daemon integration or an open-loop guarantee.
Future-compatible writes are unnecessarily delayed; cancellation/durability and
participant boundaries remain. All seven whole goals stay open.

[Visible-ingestion load regression](research/public-task-pilot/TASK_LEXICAL_CHURN_RESULTS.md)
finds17 genuine experimental stale-retry failures out of128 in-window requests;
200-item p95 reaches157ms. Control has no service failures. Future ingestion is
compatible with256/256 successes and no future evidence in successful returns.
An additional215 raw errors were a harness mistake: Nominated is overfetched,
not the later recallK cap. Raw artifacts are preserved and classifications audited.
Next correct measurement and test read-lease scheduling with writer wait included;
all seven whole research goals remain open.

[Task+lexical isolated read-load](research/public-task-pilot/TASK_LEXICAL_LOAD_RESULTS.md)
completes512 full Recall calls with no errors. At200 candidates/four workers,
experimental p95 is30.209/28.980ms versus control27.570/28.018ms. Largest sample
30.225ms; NOT a production/p99 guarantee. Closed-loop reads, hash embeddings,
memory journals, no writes/fitting; durable/version-churn load remains next.
This advances direction6's runtime evidence without closing any whole goal.

[Task+lexical actual-service integration](research/public-task-pilot/TASK_LEXICAL_SERVICE_RESULTS.md)
passes108 calls; all54 experimental winners match offline traces. Positive counts
remain12/12,8/8,8/8,7/8,9/10 across the five sets. Original scores/laws are restored;
lexical/task explanations match journals. Race tests cover lifecycle and packing,
including original-boundary adaptive expansion. Eight absent cases still pack
candidates. Fresh scope/attribute tests and loaded cost remain; all seven goals open.

[Frozen lexical Go port](research/public-task-pilot/WHAT_LEXICAL_GO_RESULTS.md)
matches108 saved orders across54 queries, maximum score discrepancy2.78e-17.
Race-enabled boundary/golden tests pass. Standalone cost76.125us at50 and293.073us
at200 candidates, not serving p99. Actual selection integration must preserve
lexical order through diversity and retain original laws/scores; it remains next.
This is a port, not new learning evidence. All seven whole directions stay open.

[What-field lexical design rescue](research/public-task-pilot/WHAT_LEXICAL_RESULTS.md)
finds complementary information: task+lexical reaches landing9/10 and W3C8/8,
preserving all prior task successes. V1 first failed by erasing version numbers;
v2 preserves them while separating date spans. Pure lexical retrieval is worse,
so task constraints remain necessary. This is offline on54 consumed queries,
not live packing, learned semantics or fresh validation. Port/verify/cost and
absent-answer work remain; all seven whole directions stay open.

[Frozen landing-family transfer](research/public-task-pilot/LANDING_TRANSFER_RESULTS.md)
passes the finite improvement screen4/10->6/10 with no regressions, without
changing task-role-v1. Three remaining cases select the right mission but its
launch rather than landing; another misses "wasn't" negation. Both absent
controls still pack evidence. The gain transfers selection logic, not learned
relation grounding. Next compare relation-aware what-field retrieval against
lexical/whole-frame controls; all seven whole goals remain open.

[Task-aware design rescue](research/public-task-pilot/TASK_ROLE_RESULTS.md)
passes84 actual service calls: role pairs6/12 baseline and3/12 old priority become
12/12, while W3C7/8, ESA written8/8 and ISO7/8 are preserved without lost baseline
successes. Laws/numeric scores unchanged. Explicit role, relation and polarity
replace indiscriminate date filtering. This is a bounded handwritten interpreter
on consumed design data, not learned semantics or exclusion authority. Fresh
transfer, missing answers and variant cost remain; all seven goals stay open.

[Paired semantic-role service diagnostic](research/public-task-pilot/SEMANTIC_ROLE_RESULTS.md)
confirms actual harm, not just parser labels: ordinary retrieval6/12 versus
priority3/12, one rescue and four regressions over fixed public Rosetta facts.
Laws/scores/journals remain intact. Next rescue must separate intent, event
relation and proposition truth, preserving positive selection without discarding
counterevidence. A guard alone leaves baseline relation failures unresolved.
These dependent designed pairs are not population accuracy; all seven goals open.

[Semantic-authority audit](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_SEMANTIC_AUTHORITY_RESULTS.md)
confirms five logical-role counterexamples where useful evidence is labeled
contradicted (polar questions, falsification, contractions, alternatives, quotes).
Hard exclusion is therefore not justified by the current parser. Artifact census
also shows all six absent cases are all-unknown: all-contradicted abstention would
rescue none. Revised lead: explicit task/relational plans and paired semantic
controls before packet exclusion, with separate risk/coverage evaluation.
No frozen parser or production behavior changed; all seven directions stay open.

[Fast priority actual-service replay](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_PRIORITY_FAST_SERVICE_RESULTS.md)
integrates the faster helper through a new overlay and reproduces all60 recorded
v1 results exactly except timing. Race tests include actual adaptive expansion.
Important limitation: expansion can still pack a contradicted event after the
correct first result; priority is not exclusion or abstention. Thus top1 gains
are not packet-consistency or answer-quality guarantees. Loaded cost and these
semantic contracts are next; all seven whole directions remain open.

[Priority lifecycle and cost rescue](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_PRIORITY_COST_RESULTS.md)
passes192 concurrent request/journal bindings and injected persistence failures.
Benchmarking finds duplicate full selection under adaptive packing. A separate
equivalent fast helper passes300 randomized comparisons and reduces the200-item
adaptive hook from19.201ms to9.493ms; this is component timing, not serving p99.
The faster helper has not yet replaced the service overlay hook. Earlier
"original-score expansion" wording is corrected: the rule uses forecast laws
and priority in original candidate order. All seven whole directions stay open.

[Post-correction priority service overlay](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_PRIORITY_OVERLAY_RESULTS.md)
now passes60 public retrieval calls with diversity packing: W3C4->7/8,
ESA written6->8/8, ISO4->7/8, no lost baseline successes. Original numeric scores
and full-frontier laws are unchanged;30 priority explanations match journals.
Race-enabled service tests cover opposite exclusions, explicit uncalibrated
status and stale-commit retry. Ordinary sources remain unchanged. This supersedes
the earlier "not integrated" notes below only for this opt-in research overlay.
Absent-answer cases still pack candidates; loaded cost, broader lifecycle and
fresh agent usefulness remain open. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Priority-aware diversity packing](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_PRIORITY_PACKING_RESULTS.md)
reproduces diversity undoing a plain permutation, then passes a selection-local
priority rescue with original records/scores restored. Token budgets, duplicate
suppression and original-score adaptive expansion remain enforced. Actual delta
composition passes2/50/200 with diversity on. Still test-composed; real service
hook/sidecar, snapshot journal and performance remain open, as do all whole goals.

[Separate temporal priority prototype](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_PRIORITY_RESULTS.md)
passes actual delta/packing composition at2/50/200 without changing numeric
scores, proper laws or earlier modulation certainty. It returns a permutation,
typed predicate decisions and calibration status not_evaluated. This avoids
synthetic confidence from score bands, but is test-composed: the service's new
post-correction hook/output contract is NOT implemented. All whole goals remain open.

[Margin full-service replay](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_MARGIN_RESULTS.md)
preserves all30 ordinal winners and every proper law across90 isolated calls.
But packet rank certainty moves by up to0.592105 solely from score encoding;
this is not calibrated confidence. Component cost116.167us at50 and402.563us
at200, with more allocations; not loaded p99. Separate constraint priority from
confidence/modulation before adoption. All whole directions remain open.

[Calendar/delta composition](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_DELTA_RESULTS.md)
finds a confirmed failure: allowed opposing0.25 deltas displace the original
calendar winner at2/50/200 candidates. A separate bounded-margin adapter survives
the same actual delta/packing tests and a gap grid under a declared bound.
Conditional rescue only: full-service replay, artificial-margin calibration,
new-variant cost and durable explanations remain open. All whole goals stay open.

[Calendar lifecycle and cost](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_LIFECYCLE_RESULTS.md):
three race-enabled repetitions pass192 concurrent request bindings, stale-result
retry, cancellation and five-attempt exhaustion. Actual memory-service/journal
boundary exercised; no production mutation. Median component cost67.118us at50
and217.063us at200 candidates; not serving p99. Learned-delta interaction and
durable explanations remain before broader use; whole goals stay open.

[Actual calendar pre-packing integration](research/public-task-pilot/CALENDAR_CONTRACT_RESULTS.md)
PASSES the isolated pack1 screen: W3C4/8->7/8, ESA written6/8->8/8 and ISO4/8->7/8.
Eight answers entering at ranks2-4 reach the final slot, no correct case is lost,
and every full-frontier forecast law is unchanged. Uses actual what-field text
and original-query context via the existing ranker contract, not oracle lookup.
Race-enabled unit checks pass. Snapshot/retry stress, learned-delta interaction,
durable explanations and serving benchmarks remain; no whole direction is complete.

[Date normalization rescue](research/public-task-pilot/DATE_NORMALIZATION_RESULTS.md)
improves ISO4/8->7/8, preserving W3C7/8 and ESA written8/8. A token-prefix bug
was found and repaired in a separate strict variant; boundary tests, a complete
Gregorian400-year cycle and all30 public replay records verify the repair.
This remains consumed-data offline research. [Runtime boundary inspection](research/public-task-pilot/TEMPORAL_RUNTIME_BOUNDARY.md)
identifies the pre-packing input gap; post-pack sorting or fixture-text lookup
must not be counted as integration. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Frozen ESA chronology transfer](research/public-task-pilot/ESA_DATE_RESULTS.md)
PASSES written-date improvement6/8->8/8 but FAILS the paired ISO-date condition
(4/8->4/8, all dates unrecognized). No correct focus case is lost. Ambiguity
checks pass; implicit-language and absent-answer gaps remain. These are new
task-family component results, not learned temporal reasoning or integrated
agent validation. Next: semantics-preserving date normalization and an explicit
original-query/constraint input boundary; all whole directions remain open.

[Frozen-rule W3C transfer](research/public-task-pilot/W3C_FOCUS_RESULTS.md)
improves2/8 to4/8 but proves clause deletion erases necessary exclusions.
[Explicit date-constraint replacement](research/public-task-pilot/DATE_CONSTRAINT_RESULTS.md)
then improves4/8 to7/8 in offline consumed-design replay, preserves correct
cases and resolves both exclusion questions. It retains the original question,
uses a restricted calendar-pattern parser and preserves laws by record identity.
This is not learned temporal reasoning or daemon integration. Next: untouched
public chronology with ambiguity controls; all seven directions remain open.

[Temporal contrast diagnostic](research/public-task-pilot/TEMPORAL_CONTRAST_RESULTS.md)
finds baseline6/10 and bounded7/10; only1/5 and2/5 opposite-target pairs are
both correct. All support remains in the pack. This broadens the relation-role
lead beyond the original Ceres example; both fail temporal adequacy.
[Contrast-clause focus rescue](research/public-task-pilot/CONTRAST_FOCUS_RESULTS.md)
improves6/10 to7/10 with the original7/8 preserved, but leaves temporal failures
and changes forecast values by up to0.010515. It is a consumed-design partial
rescue, not a rank-only upgrade, calibrated improvement or goal5 completion.
Next: explicit target/anchor/exclusion representation with fresh-domain tests,
including necessary exclusions; do not promote blanket clause deletion.

[New public context pilot](research/public-task-pilot/CONTEXT_RESULTS.md)
scores7/8 for baseline and bounded correction. Draft/adopted distinctions pass;
Ceres's subsequent classification is ranked below its initial classification.
This identifies a temporal-relation lead, not a successful learning upgrade.
The newly tested set is now consumed and cannot serve as fresh confirmation.

[Bounded semantic correction](research/public-task-pilot/BOUNDED_SEMANTIC_RESULTS.md)
preserves11/12 positive top1 results versus sparse direct8/12, but adds no
correct case and FAILS improvement. All forecast laws remain identical. This
is failure containment, not successful learning or fresh semantic validation.

[Public feature collision diagnostic](research/public-task-pilot/FEATURE_COLLISION_RESULTS.md)
finds support/distractor nine-bit collisions in10/13 consumed positive tasks.
This constrains feature-only challengers, not the baseline-preserving full
system. Richer features remove these sample collisions but earlier semantic
ranking regressions remain; test semantic-conditioned corrections next.

[Fixed-rate fresh-fit service test](research/segment-paced-fit-results.md)
PASSES the finite10/s operating point (64/64 completed, three trials, scheduled
latency non-harm) but FAILS40/s (7-11/64). Earlier overload failures remain.
This is a synthetic operating envelope, not real-task usefulness or a final
publication timing guarantee; direction6 remains open.

[Cold-prefix service validation](research/segment-prefix-load-results.md)
FAILED: shared-prefix completion4/5/7 vs2, but changed-prefix completion1 vs2-3
with zero reuse and two latency regressions. Cold preparation is included;
this is not a default rescue for new evidence or a reason to bypass invalidation.

[Full prepared-prefix fit](research/segment-prefix-full-results.md) passes36
bitwise posterior pairs plus concurrent ownership/cancellation/changed-evidence
checks. Warm full fit29.1->15.1ms; preparation remains28ms and>5.5MB retained.
Cold-start service integration and invalidating-prefix controls remain required.

[Prepared-prefix likelihood prototype](research/segment-prefix-results.md)
passes36 bitwise table comparisons and changed-prefix rejection. Compatible
one-outcome extension takes13.8ms versus27.8ms rebuild, but preparation costs
28ms and retains>5.5MB. This is not yet full-fit integration or general online
learning; arbitrary earlier changes must invalidate the prepared state.

[Finite-table loaded validation](research/segment-table-load-results.md)
improves completion1->2/64 and passes paired latency limits, but fails52/64
in every trial. Most starts still have<25ms left. Exact arithmetic reductions
are real; they have not solved fresh-work capacity or validated learner quality.

[Finite beta-log table](research/segment-table-results.md) passed independent
math/race checks and25 bitwise full-fit comparisons against batch normalization.
Median fresh-fit component time fell42.2->29.1ms including table construction;
64KiB fit-local stack table is additional scratch. Loaded completion remains
unverified; this does not rescue separate learning-quality failures.

[Fixed start allowance](research/segment-allowance-results.md) improves fresh
completion1->6/64 and eliminates interrupted fits, but refuses52-53 jobs and
fails the original screen (plus one latency cell). This is a partial rescue,
not successful full-capacity learning; processor time is roughly unchanged.

[Processor deadline trace](research/segment-deadline-trace-results.md) found
63/68 batch starts with <25ms left versus58-61ms successful loaded fits.
Late entry is now an observed failure mechanism, though pre-entry time is not
split into queue/store/scheduling. Test budget-aware refusal next without
removing refused jobs from completion denominators or relaxing the screen.

[Batch-normalized service test](research/segment-batch-service-results.md)
FAILED all six completion cells:1/64 for both fitters despite the27% component
gain. Returned forecasts match; two latency cells also fail. Next diagnostic
must measure remaining deadline/compute/post-fit checks, not infer the cause
from aggregate stale counts or stack another untested optimization.

[Stable batch normalization](research/segment-batch-results.md) passed
independent math, as-of and race checks. Fresh-fit component median fell from
58.5ms to42.7ms (about27%), with unchanged allocations. This avoids relying on
cache hits, but changed-work scheduler and quality criteria remain unproven.

[Changing-work service controls](research/segment-transform-changing-results.md)
FAILED all six completion cells: grouped reuse completes25/25/47 of64; distinct
work0-1 with zero hits. Every returned forecast matched its own reference.
Caching helps repeats but does not rescue fresh fitting; incremental/cheaper
changed-evidence computation is the next lead, retaining prior quality failures.

[Exact-work reuse pilot](research/segment-transform-memo-results.md) PASSED
the repeated-work service screen:58/60/61 of64 completed versus1 each without
reuse, with latency non-harm. Dependency-change and cancellation tests passed.
This favorable fixed-workload result is not a real-task hit-rate estimate;
distinct/changing-work service controls and shadow-off comparison remain open.

[Temporal-reuse comparison](research/segment-transform-temporal-results.md)
failed:1/0/1 completed jobs out of64, with19-21 interrupted fits and21-24 drops.
Avoiding snapshot mismatch alone does not rescue the100ms workload. Next lead
is dependency-correct work admission/sharing, retaining offered-work accounting
and distinct-work controls; no production policy change is warranted yet.

[Actual shadow scheduler experiment](research/segment-transform-service-results.md)
used temporary persistent LibraVDB with concurrent Recall/Observe and the
context-aware fixture fitter. Foreground latency non-harm passed all three
pairs, but only1/64 jobs completed in each;63 were stale. This FAILS completion
criteria. Deadline versus snapshot invalidation is not separated by current
counters and must be investigated before proposing a scheduler rescue.

[Cancellation integration prerequisite](research/segment-transform-cancellation.md):
the transform now has a research-only context-aware variant. Race tests cover
partial-output rejection, actual deadline expiry, unchanged input and recovery.
Component timings show no observable checkpoint overhead in this small sample.
The actual shadow scheduler has not yet been tested with this fitter.

[Isolated transform burst test](research/segment-transform-burst-results.md)
completed216 parity-checked fits. Deadline non-harm passed, but all three worker
counts failed the predeclared10% median speedup criterion (observed6-9%).
No additional fits met100ms. This is not a backlog rescue or serving-latency
result; scheduler/foreground interference and useful-before-stale remain open.

[Full-fit transform validation](research/segment-transform-full-results.md)
passed independent partition, as-of, ownership and concurrent-fit checks under
the race detector, plus 27,648 paired forecasts. Full fitting improved about
12% on the maximum-history fixture, with an extra 160 KiB scratch per fit.
This advances the performance lead but is not loaded-serving validation;
all seven directions remain open.

[Go tail-transform pilot](research/segment-transform-go-results.md) passed
783,360 numerical forecast comparisons with unchanged likelihood tables.
Component time fell about 22% at 16 labels and 11% at 64 labels; scratch reuse
removed per-tail allocation growth but still adds about 160 KiB per fit.
This is research-only, not loaded-serving evidence or a learning-quality rescue.
Full-posterior integration and realistic background-load tests remain open.

[Bound-origin conflict nomination](research/public-task-pilot/ORIGIN_CONFLICT_RESULTS.md)
detects the known bound-key/legacy-grouping disagreement without overriding AP
or packing. All52 public repeat outputs remain unchanged with zero unnecessary
review nominations; focused race tests cover tampering, tenant/ID boundaries
and truncated coverage. The packing gap is detectable, not resolved; durable
review/outcome integration and trusted registry construction remain open.
All seven whole research goals remain open.

[Offline origin bindings](research/public-task-pilot/ORIGIN_BINDING_RESULTS.md)
rescue all52 public repeat pairs in packing GIVEN a correct external registry;
missing/tampered bindings reject and AP/declared-occurrence checks pass. A
widened negative test finds a remaining legacy fallback collapse of distinct
same-lineage relations despite different bound keys. This is partial rescue,
not end-to-end authentication or complete separation. Next reconcile verified
bindings with fallback without weakening trust/AP boundaries. All goals stay open.

[Public provenance boundary test](research/public-task-pilot/PUBLIC_PROVENANCE_RESULTS.md)
uses13 existing NASA facts through real grouping and packet-selection components.
Stable origins suppress13/13 repeats; missing/per-fetch origins or producer
changes suppress0/13 each, with both copies occupying slots. Replay and existing
unit tests pass. This is not an end-to-end confidence exploit or a truth sensor.
Investigate trusted origin preservation/equivalence before changing grouping;
the runtime remains unchanged. All seven whole research goals stay open.

[Joint noisy-audit optimization](research/hypothesis-observation/AUDIT_JOINT_RESULTS.md)
retains a positive numerical violation lower bound .000326081643 despite
optimizing forecasts and actions. The mixture passes160/160 final checks but
120/150 area checks. Separate pre-audit recursion,20million branch equalities
and replay pass; no future-audit conditioning or free observation is used.
This stronger universal finite screen is not the entire direction7 objective.
Prioritize measurable observation/provenance mechanisms and explicit task
populations over more unvalidated sensor assumptions. All seven goals stay open.

[Equal-cost noisy source audit](research/hypothesis-observation/NOISY_AUDIT_RESULTS.md)
tests a new hypothetical observation channel shared by all controls. The audit
costs one renewal; total budget stays16. The one-step pilot FAILS:152/160 final
nonharm and105/150 positive-area comparisons. Posterior marginalization,
independent quadrature, backward scoring and replay pass. No measured provenance
reliability is claimed. Next assess joint-policy headroom with a shared pre-audit
forecast, avoiding future-audit leakage. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Rational joint-policy certificate](research/hypothesis-observation/EXACT_JOINT_RESULTS.md)
establishes a positive lower bound .000501222366 for the exact-decimal finite
model using integer outward enclosures. The witness weights ONLY area rows,
so final-error slack is not the blocker. Full replay,15360 independent integer
likelihood checks and3200 enclosure checks pass. Tuning forecast/acquisition
within this information model cannot meet the full area-dominance requirement.
Test genuinely additional observations with equal control access/cost; all
seven whole research directions remain open.

[Joint forecast/acquisition game](research/hypothesis-observation/JOINT_POLICY_RESULTS.md)
still has positive numerical lower bounds: .000438704 with random/archived
entropy and .000501222 with random/normalized entropy. Independent optimal-
forecast recursion, replay and mixture checks agree. The finite model's full
screen appears infeasible even without a frozen forecast map; do not keep
tuning within that class. Certify the witness, then test additional observable
information with equal control access/cost. All seven whole goals remain open.

[Global trajectory-policy game](research/hypothesis-observation/GLOBAL_POLICY_RESULTS.md)
finds independently reproduced positive numerical infeasibility bounds for
frozen forecasts: .00249445 versus random/archived entropy and .00269119 versus
random/normalized entropy. This is not merely local-guard conservatism or an
extra-control artifact. Exact-arithmetic oracle reasoning covers adaptive
policies and mixtures; floating-point bounds are not outward-rounded proofs.
Stop acquisition-only tuning here; test joint forecasting/acquisition with
unchanged success criteria. All seven whole directions remain open.

[Copy-trigger audit](research/hypothesis-observation/COPY_TRIGGER_RESULTS.md)
finds all32 reachable changed decisions require a source0 contradiction.
For all odd masks, the budget policy and normalized entropy have identical
complete traces, not merely near-equal scores. Separate32480-prefix enumeration
and128 root/mask trace checks verify this structural tie. Further noise sampling
cannot rescue it; test population risk allocation across histories without
requiring copied sources to contradict themselves. All seven goals remain open.

[Continuous risk diagnostic](research/hypothesis-observation/CONTINUOUS_RISK_RESULTS.md)
constructs degree10 population-risk envelopes. On [.1,.3], eight masks show
positive area lower bounds over normalized entropy and seven remain tied;
the tied masks all copy source0. Final numerical bounds stay within .01 over
[.05,.35], but four area regressions remain above .30. Replay and1408 direct-
likelihood score comparisons pass. Ordinary floating point is not a rigorous
interval certificate; the learning failures remain. All seven goals stay open.

[Frozen risk-budget off-grid test](research/hypothesis-observation/RISK_BUDGET_OFFGRID_RESULTS.md)
passes288/288 final nonharm checks but only183/270 positive-area conditions.
Interpolation has32/60 area improvements and28 ties versus normalized entropy;
noise.35 adds four genuine area harms (worst .0002106644). Frozen hashes, replay
and360 backward checks pass. Next use a continuous noise-risk envelope rather
than extrapolating protection from a grid. All seven goals remain open.

[Remaining-horizon risk budget](research/hypothesis-observation/RISK_BUDGET_RESULTS.md)
preserves240/240 final nonharm checks and improves positive-area passes from
116/225 to156/225. Against normalized entropy,40/75 regimes now improve and35
remain exactly tied; maximum area gain is .0003417352. Full screen still FAILS.
Zero-budget action parity, replay,880 control-score objects and360 backward
checks pass. Freeze this partial candidate for off-grid noise testing before
further tuning. All seven research directions remain open.

[Regime-safe acquisition](research/hypothesis-observation/REGIME_SAFE_RESULTS.md)
passes240/240 final nonharm comparisons but FAILS the learning-speed screen:
116/225 positive-area conditions, with zero area gain over normalized entropy.
Its statewise guard permits reached action changes only at the last query;
final gains over normalized entropy top out at .00079129. Replay,800 archived
score checks and330 backward checks pass. Next investigate trajectory-level
risk budgeting rather than requiring conditional dominance at every state.
This is finite protection, not completion of any whole research direction.

[Canonical tie-policy comparison](research/hypothesis-observation/TIE_POLICY_RESULTS.md)
does not rescue acquisition: normalized entropy still beats two/full beyond
the .01 nonharm allowance in noise.30/mask3 (harm .01028345), and32/75 area
conditions fail for each. Target-planner final/area scores stay unchanged;
entropy final Brier moves by up to .00543750. Exact replay,480 archived-score
parities and300 separate backward checks pass. Move to source-regime-aware
objectives, not more planning depth or tie cleanup. All seven goals stay open.

[Numerical tie/order audit](research/hypothesis-observation/TIE_ORDER_RESULTS.md)
confirms action differences despite posterior and objective agreement at floating-
point precision: reversing equivalent histories changes2 one-step,2 two-step,
and11 entropy choices across752 states. Do not equate older ordered-history
rollouts with count-state policies. Next freeze canonical evaluation and an
explicit tie rule in a separate comparison before source-prior/objective changes.
This diagnostic leaves all seven research directions open.

[Exact80-regime evaluation](research/hypothesis-observation/EXACT_REGIME_RESULTS.md)
confirms four distinct population nonharm failures versus entropy for each of
two/full planning: masks3/7 at noise .25/.30, with worst final harm .015721.
These masks were absent from the recent rollout grid. This is a genuine
source-regime tradeoff, not just sampling uncertainty. Replay, ordered likelihood
checks and separate backward scoring pass. Source-prior/objective robustness
and numerical tie/order auditing remain leads. All seven goals stay open.

[Exact six-query planning](research/hypothesis-observation/COUNT_PLANNING_RESULTS.md)
solves48048 sufficient-count states. Two-query lookahead already matches the
full-horizon model-prior objective within floating-point precision; deeper
planning offers no meaningful rescue for this fixed model/budget. Forward
occupancy, Bellman checks and full replay pass. This does not overturn failed
fresh per-regime screens. Investigate source/model adequacy or regime-sensitive
objectives instead of further depth sweeps. All seven goals remain open.

[Fresh two-query lookahead](research/hypothesis-observation/INTERVAL_LOOKAHEAD_RESULTS.md)
FAILS at20/108 checks across1536 new-seed equal-credit episodes. Pooled final
Brier improves .002667 versus one-step, but learning-area gain is only .000096;
subgroup protection and superiority remain unvalidated. Replay,7680 budgets
and sampled as-of reconstruction pass. Next diagnostic: verify sufficient
count-state compression and compute the full six-query finite-model optimum,
not an unbounded series of lookahead-depth tweaks. All seven goals remain open.

[Fresh interval-aware acquisition](research/hypothesis-observation/INTERVAL_ACQUISITION_RESULTS.md)
FAILS at19/84 checks across1536 new-seed equal-credit episodes. Target Gini
selection improves pooled Brier versus random (.333168 vs .405275), but is
close to entropy (.332866) and does not establish protected faster learning.
Some subgroup point means fail; other failures are uncertainty-limited.
Full replay,6144 cost checks and sampled as-of reconstruction pass. The old
fixed-observation failure stays intact; multi-step observation remains a lead.

[Unrestricted endpoint oracle](research/hypothesis-observation/VECTOR_ENDPOINT_RESULTS.md)
establishes an information ceiling: in counts[1,1,3,1] at .30 genuine noise,
even the true conditional forecast can improve baseline Brier by only .000261,
below required .005. An independent direct-product calculation confirms it.
The900-gate fixed-observation screen is therefore unattainable, not rescued;
retain that failure without dropping the case or weakening the threshold.
Full-vector endpoint optimization passes862/900. Redirect the next experiment
to goal7's equal-cost acquisition question: better evidence, not another
correction-only objective on this same limited history. All seven goals open.

[Full-vector risk allocation](research/hypothesis-observation/VECTOR_ALLOCATION_RESULTS.md)
passes889/900 checks, one more than the line-restricted average objective.
All population protection and false-confidence checks pass, but11 noisy-world
gain requirements fail. Average risk improves in every allocation; replay and
scoring checks pass. Worst individual outcome harm remains .334035. Endpoint
or worst-regime objectives in the larger vector family remain live leads.

[Endpoint-risk objective](research/hypothesis-observation/POPULATION_ENDPOINT_RESULTS.md)
passes873/900 checks, below the average objective's888/900. Numerical dual bounds
exclude the .005 endpoint gain in five allocations for the current fixed-line
family, regardless of step-size objective. All protection checks and replay
pass, but worst individual outcome harm remains .287691. Full-vector forecast
optimization is the next distinct lead; no whole direction is complete.

[Population-risk allocation](research/hypothesis-observation/POPULATION_ALLOCATION_RESULTS.md)
passes888/900 checks, rescuing15 more gains while retaining all population
protection and false-confidence checks. Twelve noisy-world gains still fail.
The finite solver completes with unchanged accuracy after an explicit work-
budget extension; replay and scoring checks pass. Important tradeoff: worst
single history/outcome harm is .334452 even though population harm stays .01.
This is not per-event protection. Worst-regime objectives remain a live lead.

[Noise-marginalized target](research/hypothesis-observation/UNIFORM_NOISE_TARGET_RESULTS.md)
still passes873/900 checks, with exactly the same27 gain failures as the fixed-
noise interval line guard. Joint-likelihood integration is independently checked;
replay, controls and scoring agree. Cell Brier changes range from-.002603 to
+.000531, insufficient to rescue a gate. Population risk allocation remains a
distinct live lead; this target-only change does not complete any direction.

[Continuous-noise envelope](research/hypothesis-observation/NOISE_ENVELOPE_RESULTS.md)
protects the full [.10,.30] common-noise interval through nonnegative polynomial
coefficients, subject to the declared likelihood family. Closest-point projection
hits its unchanged iteration cap (failure fixture preserved). A separately
declared analytic line guard passes873/900 checks: all protection and false-
confidence gates, but27 gain failures remain. Replay, control parity,107100
envelope checks and3200 scoring checks pass. No whole direction is complete.

[Outcome-simplex protection](research/hypothesis-observation/SIMPLEX_GUARD_RESULTS.md)
rescues all800 harm and50 false-confidence checks across noise worlds but
FAILS all50 genuine-gain checks (850/900 overall). Matched-world projection
optimality gives numerical gain ceilings below .001547 versus required .005:
changing the target alone cannot rescue this stronger universal guard. This
does not rule out the original population-risk requirement, which is weaker.
Justified likelihood envelopes and population risk allocation remain live leads.

[Noise-mismatch testing](research/hypothesis-observation/OPTIMISTIC_NOISE_MISMATCH_RESULTS.md)
limits the guarded-optimism rescue: 713/900 gates pass overall (FAIL), despite
180/180 at each of .10, .15 and .20 actual noise. With the predictor fixed at
.20, actual .25/.30 noise causes 78/91 protection failures; worst harm reaches
.023101 versus the .01 allowance. Matched-world parity, byte-exact replay and
3200 alternate scoring checks pass. The next rescue should protect against
measurement-model uncertainty without relaxing gain or harm criteria.

[Guarded optimistic forecasting](research/hypothesis-observation/OPTIMISTIC_TRANSFER_RESULTS.md)
passes all180 finite allocation stress gates with unchanged risk budget and
counterfeit controls. It changes the decision target, not the evidence's claimed
authenticity. Genuine gains are0.008014-0.009586; worst harm uses essentially the
full0.01 allowance. Reference projection p99 is2.67ms, excluding law construction
and serving. Model mismatch, new structures and adaptive integration remain
required; this finite rescue does not complete any whole research direction.

[Closest-feasible transfer projection](research/hypothesis-observation/PROJECTED_TRANSFER_RESULTS.md)
retains all protection checks but still FAILS at175/180 gates, with the same
five gain misses as the line guard. Independent analytic/boundary and dual-gap
checks plus replay pass; at most123 cycles were needed in the full stress run.
Changing correction geometry alone produced only tiny quality differences.
The model-adequacy assumptions remain; all seven goals stay open.

[Conditional robust transfer](research/hypothesis-observation/ROBUST_TRANSFER_RESULTS.md)
repairs all160 finite-family protection checks but FAILS the overall screen
(175/180): five genuine-gain requirements are missed. It constrains movement
using all supported counterfeit-pattern laws, not the selected true pattern.
Maximum population harm is0.005473; the model-family adequacy assumption and
exponential law-construction cost remain explicit. All seven goals stay open.

[Exact allocation stress](research/hypothesis-observation/RENEWAL_ALLOCATION_RESULTS.md)
breaks the narrow shared-freshness pass:173/180 gates pass, but seven partial-
counterfeit cells exceed the0.01 Brier-harm tolerance (worst0.011908). One uses
the original allocation with a previously untested mask. Independent800-cell
verification agrees. These finite-law harms are not sampling uncertainty;
shared Bayesian support alone is not a nonharm guarantee. All goals stay open.

[Shared-versus-local freshness](research/hypothesis-observation/HIERARCHICAL_RENEWAL_RESULTS.md)
is implemented and exactly verified. Broad consumed-trace quality FAILS (8/14
gates,0/4 gains). A separate exact16-credit mixed-counterfeit control passes6/6
limited gates, but harms mixed-source Brier by0.00871/0.00986 within the0.01
tolerance and changes no classification accuracy. This is not robust pooling
or closed-loop validation. All original failures and seven open goals remain.

[Closed-loop uncertain renewal](research/hypothesis-observation/ADAPTIVE_RENEWAL_RESULTS.md)
tests640 new synthetic episodes with six cost-matched policies. It changes
acquisition behavior and reduces false-freshness overconfidence, but FAILS the
complete screen (10/32 gates). Independent56,410-forecast reconstruction passes.
Per-type versus shared measurement mechanisms remain a modeling question, not
a validated reason to pool source trust. No direction is declared complete.

[Uncertain renewal inference](research/hypothesis-observation/UNCERTAIN_RENEWAL_RESULTS.md)
models a shared latent root and both source modes. Replaying all640 consumed
episodes reduces split1 false-renewal confidently wrong outcomes from24/64 to0/64
but leaves accuracy unchanged; genuine copied/mixed renewals lose Brier quality.
The screen FAILS (4/10 gates). Independent35,894-forecast reconstruction passes.
This is fixed-trace inference, not a new acquisition policy or authenticity
certificate. Closed-loop uncertainty-aware acquisition remains an untested lead.

[Costed renewed measurements](research/hypothesis-observation/RENEWAL_RESULTS.md)
complete640 fresh synthetic episodes. Genuine independent draws substantially
improve copied/mixed confirmation cases at equal16-credit cost, but the full
screen FAILS two split0 uncertainty gates (14/16 pass). False fresh labels drive
confidently wrong forecasts to37.5% in the negative control. Independent batch
posterior reconstruction checks35,894 forecasts. Strong conditional gains do
not establish authenticity or justify operational promotion. All goals stay open.

[Known-mode planning comparison](research/hypothesis-observation/PLANNING_CEILING_RESULTS.md)
finds depth2 already matches exact optimal terminal Brier at every tested budget
in copied05/copied20/null. Depth3 adds nothing; greedy differs only at one
four-report budget. Independent108-cell verification and replay pass. This
rules out deeper lookahead as a useful next change in this family, not in all
observation models. Unknown provenance and new independent measurements remain
open; prior failed unknown-mode screens are not superseded.

[Falsification-observation information audit](research/hypothesis-observation/IDENTIFIABILITY_RESULTS.md)
computes an exact copied20 Bayes Brier floor of0.471910 and classification-error
floor of33.6261% with all eight unique reports. Repeats cannot beat that bound
under the declared simulator law. Independent original-likelihood enumeration
agrees and replay is exact. Earlier policy error still exceeds the floor;
better planning and genuinely independent observations are distinct remaining
leads. This diagnoses direction7's evidence limit without changing its criteria.

[Exact finite posterior calibration](docs/experiments/mmm-finite-calibration-v120-results.md)
is implemented with a prior-only control and tested on all 2,688 consumed runs.
Independent 6,912 small-model checks pass; both version-local and moving-history
quality candidates FAIL (614/672 and 563/672 nonharm gates, 6/96 and 0/96 gains).
Some stable regressions improve relative to MAP, but both switch directions
still trail Markov. Prior/model changes prevent attributing improvements solely
to integration. No calibration rescue is qualified; all seven goals remain open.

[Version-local calibration](docs/experiments/mmm-version-logit-v120-results.md)
and a cadence-matched moving-history control are implemented and tested on all
2,688 consumed v120 runs each. Both FAIL. Same-version isolation helps one
switch direction but hurts the null case; delayed feedback yields only about
2-3 labels per fit. Independent reconstruction checks every fit and score.
The matched cadence prevents attributing all improvement over the old pilot to
versioning. Strict resetting is not a validated rescue. All seven goals remain
open; no serving or whitepaper changes.

[Residual-logit calibration](docs/experiments/mmm-logit-v120-results.md) is
implemented and tested across all 2,688 consumed v120 runs. Independent
reconstruction checks every fit and forecast; diagnostic and reference replay
are byte-exact. All four candidates FAIL predictive-quality gates. A timing
diagnostic finds positive expected correction gains on old switch-case forecasts
but substantial harm on subsequent improved forecasts. This supports studying
predictor-version/calibration alignment, without claiming to have isolated
predictor evolution from environmental shift. No rescue is yet validated.
All seven directions remain open; no production or paper promotion.

[Contextual expert routing](docs/experiments/mmm-context-v120-results.md) is
implemented as a finite latent-mask/cell model and tested on all2688 consumed
v120 runs.32768 exhaustive small-model comparisons and672 as-of checks pass;
an independent688128-forecast global reference agrees. Full output replay is
byte-exact. Quality FAIL: context passes168/168 protection cells but0/32 gains
against its matched global control, and155/168 protection plus1/32 gains against
Markov. Extra context structure is not justified here. The
[residual-logit calibration lead](research/residual-logit-lead.md) was the next
candidate with an explicit baseline-only control and overfitting falsifiers;
its subsequently completed negative results are recorded above. All seven
directions remain open; no production or paper promotion.

[Normalized interval surrogate](docs/experiments/mmm-interval-v120-results.md)
is implemented as a bounded research reference.4096 independent component
checks and672 as-of prefix checks pass across1,376,256 forecast evaluations on
all2688 consumed v120 runs. Quality FAIL: four/eight experts pass117/168 and
116/168 Markov protection cells. The source-inspired changing-environment
construction does not rescue the workload; no delayed regret theorem is claimed.
Prior v96 interval failures remain visible. Next investigate input-conditional
expert assignment with a declared model and matched evidence, not another
time-only prior sweep or an oracle router. All seven directions remain open.
No Go/production, OpenClaw, whitepaper or publishing changes.

[Credit decomposition and Squint diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-segment-v120-credit-results.md)
complete on all2688 consumed v120 runs.688128 exact decomposition checks show
feedback delay is not the whole reverse-switch gap: removing all mixer delay
and missingness still leaves substantial oracle selection and forecast-hull
headroom. Plain finite-grid Squint is implemented and tested, but rejected:
four/eight-expert versions pass126/168 and117/168 Markov protection cells.
The changing-environment Squint-CE source supplies a distinct next lead, not
a result of the current code. Check normalized interval mixtures and delayed
evidence ordering before a fresh candidate; retain every existing comparison.
All seven directions remain open. No production or whitepaper change.

[Segment-membership v120](docs/experiments/mmm-soft-learners-v120-results.md)
completes2688 fresh runs, independent verification and full exact replay with176
source hashes. Both candidates FAIL: Segment64 passes493/672
non-harm and19/96 gain screens; Segment32 passes432/672 and6/96. Segment64
improves both Boolean switch directions but harms stationary patterns. A
[consumed static-atom diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-segment-v120-static-atom-diagnostic.json)
raises64-label protection to609/672 and gains to24/96, still FAIL and not fresh
confirmation. The [consumed mixer diagnostic](docs/experiments/mmm-segment-v120-composition-diagnostic.json)
passes168/168 protection cells versus raw Markov but zero meaningful gain cells,
and only162/168 protection cells versus variational augmentation. Merely adding
the old mixer does not realize the segment specialist's headroom. Next isolate
remaining early stationary/family and evidence-credit costs before a fresh
integrated candidate; do not relax the retained comparisons. All seven
directions remain open. Production and the whitepaper are unchanged.

[Bounded segment posterior](research/segment-posterior-component.md) now computes
regime membership from the same eligible labels, using genuine generic/Boolean
marginal likelihoods. Independent interval/partition references, delayed-cap
invariants and first-use concurrency checks pass. Full64-label fitting costs
62.6-63.2ms on the measured fixture, mostly likelihood construction; this is
slow-path component cost, not serving latency. Next run the frozen full-case
quality comparison. No rescue or promotion; all seven directions remain open.

[Full-input uncertainty v119](docs/experiments/mmm-soft-learners-v119-results.md)
completes2688 fresh runs, independent reconstruction and exact replay with170
hashes. Neither candidate passes the complete rescue. Variational32 passes36/40
MAP gains but harms all four reverse transitions; both widths pass168/168 MAP
non-harm under the allowed.01 tolerance. Consumed six-expert composition still
fails delayed reverse-transition protection. The new
[segment-posterior lead](research/segment-posterior-lead.md) has32766 tiny exhaustive
checks, not a learned-model rescue. Next implement and test its bounded delayed
evidence model. No promotion; all seven directions remain open.

[Variational logistic fitter](research/variational-logistic-component-status.md)
now passes focused numerical/race checks against an independent repeated-row
solution. QA required a larger iteration cap and tighter convergence tolerance;
neither is a predictive quality result. Predictive integration, performance and
fresh full-case comparison remain pending. All seven directions remain open.

[Full-input learners v118](docs/experiments/mmm-soft-learners-v118-results.md)
completes2688 fresh runs and exact replay: no standalone candidate passes.
Ridge64 passes7/12 additive-target gains (including stationary in both phases),
but delayed recovery and broad protection fail; ridge32/tree32 pass no gain,
tree64 passes2/12 hierarchy-target gains. All162 hashes,1376256 ridge forecasts
and43008 fitted states verify. Next test the separately declared variational
predictive, retaining all specialists and all failed cases. No promotion; all
seven directions remain open.
