[Regularized logistic component](research/ridge-challenger-component.md) is
implemented and tested. A near-optimum loss-subtraction bug was fixed without
relaxing convergence. All1152 consumed QA fits, derivative/analytic/symmetry tests
and39366 partial-law checks pass. Fit64 costs35.67-36.02 microseconds; compilation
adds72.25-73.22 microseconds on the measured fixture, not serving latency. Fresh
quality and composite integration are next; no promotion and all seven directions
remain open.

[Transfer diagnosis v117](docs/experiments/mmm-transfer-diagnosis-v117-results.md)
reconstructs all1152 consumed runs and replays exactly. In all36 terminal cells,
generic full-input fit excess exceeds the absolute view/routing terms combined;
this includes estimation and stale-window effects, not just representation bias.
All157 source hashes,3538944 partial checks and1769472 identities pass. Next test
the [soft-response learner lead](research/soft-response-challenger-proposal.md)
with unchanged evidence budgets and retained specialists before more weight tuning.
Diagnosis is not a rescue; v116 remains FAIL and all seven directions remain open.

[Independent transfer v116](docs/experiments/mmm-transfer-v116-results.md)
completes all1152 runs and full replay: FAIL,290/348 screens. All288 non-harm
checks pass, but only2/60 recovery gains pass; every failed gain also falls below
the .005 mean floor. All154 source hashes and independent probability/score,
clock, cost and fit-origin checks pass. New noisy families do not share the
old95% accuracy ceiling. Next diagnose fitted-model versus observation loss on
the consumed transfer artifact before another rescue. All seven directions
remain open; no promotion, deployment or publication.

[Independent transfer runner](research/independent-transfer-runner.md) now wires
unchanged generic64, arrival-log and delayed-Markov controls to the new generators.
As-of fitting, paired schedules, future/current-label non-leakage and evaluator
oracle isolation are tested across all nine family/dynamics cells on consumed QA
seeds. Quality comparison on fresh trajectories is still pending. The terminal
recovery segment is ticks192-255, after every generated gradual change completes.
All seven directions remain open; no production change or promotion.

[Independent response generators](research/independent-generator-contract.md)
now cover soft additive, hierarchical and local-table responses under stationary,
abrupt and gradual dynamics. Scoped race/reference tests and vet pass, including
59,049 conditional-average checks and effective QA seed separation. No learner
quality comparison has run on these generators yet. Next freeze and run transfer
controls; no promotion, and all seven research directions remain open.

[Snapshot-specialist v115](docs/experiments/mmm-snapshot-v115-results.md) completes
all1,536 runs and replay: FAIL,754/1,018 gates (739/960 non-harm,15/58 gain).
Stationary and both switch families regress against stronger controls, including
the matched12800-budget Markov control. All58 hashes and independent scores,
costs, clocks and as-of fits reconstruct. No promotion. Next prioritize
[independent-generator transfer](research/independent-generator-transfer-proposal.md)
over another same-fixture weighting variant. All seven directions remain open.

[Snapshot-specialist integration](research/snapshot-specialists-integration-results.md)
now wires the gate, observer and delayed journal. Scoped race/reference checks
pass; a publication-reset flaw was corrected so retained rejected IDs cannot
re-enter or pass credits to reused slots. Full256-frame lifecycle costs9.4-9.7ms
immediate and17.7-17.8ms delayed, including publication copies but not fits/I/O.
Quality is UNTESTED. Next add the matched12800-budget four-role control, then
freeze a complete fresh comparison. No promotion; all seven directions remain open.

[Snapshot-specialist component](research/snapshot-specialists-component-results.md)
now implements two immutable banks, identity-bound delayed evidence and detached
coherent laws. Path/dense references, rollback and race checks pass. A256-frame
filter lifecycle costs.48ms immediate or5.15-5.23ms at delay31, excluding
observation, gating and fitting. Quality is UNTESTED. Next integrate identity-scoped
gate budgets and the controller before any fresh full comparison. No promotion;
all seven research directions remain open.

[Error decomposition v114](docs/experiments/mmm-decomposition-v114-results.md)
completes192 consumed trajectories and full replay. All47 hashes and589,824
independent partial-forecast checks pass. Final reverse-switch harm is mostly
model/mixture (.023770), not observation (.001176), but early models remain poor
and the opposite switch has model-specific observation loss. This is diagnosis,
not validation. Next investigate [bounded snapshot specialists](research/snapshot-specialists-proposal.md)
with identity-scoped evidence and gate accounting. No promotion; all seven
research directions remain open.

[Hazard-mixture v113](docs/experiments/mmm-hazard-v113-results.md) completes
1,536 runs: 804/818 gates pass, but the candidate FAILS (767/768 non-harm,
37/50 gain). All failures concern delayed switch recovery. All44 hashes and
independent scores, costs and clocks reconstruct. The eleven-rate mixture
does not justify its extra cost; no promotion. Next distinguish model error,
weighting error and observation selection on consumed trajectories before
proposing another rescue. All seven directions remain open.
Full replay passed; whole nine-policy fixtures took137-160ms immediate and
168-170ms delayed/missing, including fits across256 frames, not serving latency.

[Hazard-mixture component](research/hazard-mixture-component-results.md) now
implements joint inference over11 fixed switching-rate hypotheses and four
roles. Path enumeration, dense filtering, point-mass recovery and race/lifecycle
checks pass. An equivalent arithmetic optimization reduces delayed lifecycle
cost by about17-18%, but delay31 still costs39.5ms per256 frames versus3.2ms
for fixed Markov. Quality is UNTESTED; next freeze the complete fresh comparison.
No promotion; all seven research directions remain open.

[Delayed Markov v112](docs/experiments/mmm-markov-v112-results.md) completes
all1,536 runs and full replay. All672 non-harm gates pass, but only33/46 gain
gates pass:705/718 overall, FAIL. Confirmation delayed switch gains against
original log are.003930 and.003062, below the.005 floor. All37 hashes and
independent scores/clocks reconstruct; all196,608 immediate outputs match
original log exactly. Next investigate [hazard uncertainty](research/hazard-mixture-advice-proposal.md)
without a retrospective rate sweep. No promotion; all seven directions remain open.

[Delayed Markov component](research/delayed-markov-component-results.md)
now matches literal state-path enumeration, dense full-history checks and
exact immediate-feedback log/share journal behavior. Delayed labels refilter
their original states without altering issued forecasts or gate ownership.
Race/rollback/expiry checks pass. Delay31 and reversed-batch lifecycles cost
3.09-3.24ms per256 events versus1.31-1.56ms for arrival log. Quality remains
UNTESTED; next freeze the complete fresh recovery/stationary comparison.
No candidate is promoted; all seven directions remain open.

[Compatibility handoff v111](docs/experiments/mmm-compatibility-v111-results.md)
completes all1,536 schedule-runs and exact replay. Publication-only705/718 and
plus-pending691/718 both FAIL. Stationary protection passes, but switch recovery
against the stronger log controls does not; pending attenuation worsens both
delayed switches against publication-only in both phases. All37 hashes and
independent scores/clocks reconstruct. No promotion. Next investigate
[delayed Markov advice](research/delayed-markov-advice-proposal.md), not another
affinity-rate sweep. All seven research directions remain open.

[Compatibility handoff component](research/compatibility-handoff-component-results.md)
implements publication transfer and optional pending-loss transfer. Both pass
race/lifecycle checks, exact no-movement journal parity and independent formula
checks. The isolated 256-frame lifecycle costs about 1.93-1.99ms versus
1.55-1.60ms for original log advice. Quality is UNTESTED: next freeze a fresh
paired comparison with every stationary/recovery protection, not a tuned
replay of v110. No candidate is promoted; all seven directions remain open.

[Handoff diagnosis v110](docs/experiments/mmm-handoff-v110-results.md)
replays all128 consumed delayed switch trajectories unchanged. It confirms
late short-window weighting lag: generic32 keeps84.2% mean weight while its
full-input Brier is .1201 versus generic64 .0615. Most of the reverse-change
gap remains at matched views; observation effects still matter in the other
direction. All37 hashes,52,526 updates and65,536 forecast rows reconstruct.
Next test [forecast-compatibility handoff](research/compatibility-handoff-proposal.md),
including pending-loss transfer. This is diagnosis, not a rescue; all seven
directions remain open and no candidate is promoted.

[Log-score advice v109](docs/experiments/mmm-log-v109-results.md) completes
both candidates on1,536 schedule-runs. Log/no-neutral506/518 and log/neutral
498/518 remain FAIL, but every failed gate is now delayed late parity-to-majority.
Stable parity and majority-to-parity improve substantially. Full replay,
33 source hashes, independent scores and race checks pass; no promotion.
The [block diagnosis](docs/experiments/mmm-log-v109-blocks.json) shows both early
and late harm. Next trace [model/weight handoff](research/model-handoff-diagnosis-proposal.md)
and distinguish it from acquisition changes. All seven directions remain open.

[Neutral/age ablation v108](docs/experiments/mmm-advice-v108-results.md)
implements and tests all three candidates on1,536 schedule-runs. None passes:
neutral-only398/418, age-only357/418, combined360/418. The combined candidate
improves confirmation delayed switches over arrival, but sacrifices stable
parity and fails stronger recovery comparisons. Full replay,29 source hashes,
independent reconstruction and race checks pass. No candidate is promoted.
Next investigate [log-score advice](research/log-score-advice-proposal.md)
without unconditional forgetting. All seven directions remain open.

[Mixture headroom v107](docs/experiments/mmm-mixture-v107-results.md) completes
a consumed diagnostic on all128 delayed switch trajectories and512 bundles.
At the change publication, even hindsight-optimal raw mixtures are worse than
neutral in62/64 confirmation-labeled trajectories. Later bundles recover useful
forecasts. Full replay,28 source hashes and independent numerical checks pass.
This is not a quality rescue or a universal impossibility bound. Next isolate
explicit neutral competition from origin-aged selection on fresh streams,
without weakening the evidence gate. All seven directions remain open.

[Arrival-time quality v106](docs/experiments/mmm-arrival-v106-results.md)
FAILS16/318 gates. Timing gives small gains over full carry on delayed switches,
but below .005 and still not better than conservative. Delayed late parity4
retains94.76% expected accuracy. All1,536 schedule-runs replay exactly; as-of
fits, both update clocks, scores and23 hashes reconstruct. No candidate is
promoted. Next bound recoverable error from existing model mixtures, including
neutrality, before selecting another clock/age ablation. All seven directions
remain open; prior failures remain recorded.

[Origin timing diagnosis v105](docs/experiments/mmm-origin-v105-results.md)
replays all128 consumed delayed switch runs and26,230 updates without changing
any original result. Missing-head ordering adds roughly13 ticks beyond arrival;
old-regime updates usually harm the current raw-mixture risk while new-regime
updates help on average. This is diagnostic, not a served-law causal estimate.
The [arrival-time selector component](research/arrival-routing-component-results.md)
now passes lifecycle/race checks, preserving the ordered version-scoped gate.
Next freeze its fresh quality comparison before testing other aging mechanisms.
All seven directions remain open; v104 remains FAIL.

[Delayed routed quality v104](docs/experiments/mmm-delayed-v104-results.md)
FAILS12/218 gates: unconditional role carry improves stable parity, but worsens
delayed regime recovery relative to the conservative journal. Confirmation
late parity4 reaches94.47% expected accuracy; both switch Brier comparisons
regress with approximate intervals excluding zero. All1,536 paired schedule-runs
replay; as-of fitting, journal counts and21 source hashes reconstruct exactly.
Retention is validated, general delayed quality is not. Next diagnose
[loss age versus the forgetting clock](research/origin-age-routing-proposal.md),
then test a separately frozen rescue. All seven directions remain open.

[Delayed routing journal component](research/routed-journal-component-results.md)
passes immediate parity, reordered feedback, version isolation, bounded expiry,
backpressure and race checks. Across a 64-schedule retention grid, the role-carry
variant preserves all delivered selector losses without updating new-version
tests from stale forecasts. The conservative control can lose 166 of 204
delivered losses through missing-head waiting and publication changes.
This is a mechanism result, not a quality pass. Next freeze the
[as-of delayed quality comparison](research/delayed-routing-proposal.md), then
restore member-split/generation checks. All seven directions remain open.

[Adaptive observation v103](docs/experiments/mmm-adaptive-v103-results.md)
FAILS 2 of 108 frozen gates: exact lookahead does not establish an additional
late-parity gain over routed one-step. All 96 non-harm gates and 10 gains against
the generic observer pass. Confirmation late parity4 accuracy is 94.74% for
one-step and 94.80% for lookahead, with much greater planning cost for lookahead.
All 768 trajectories replay exactly; scores/costs, 21 hashes and race checks pass.
Retain the cheaper coherent observer, not a tuned or expanded lookahead claim.
Next: explicit delayed-feedback/version binding, then fresh member-split and
scarce-feedback integration. All seven directions remain open.

[Coherent routed-observation component](research/joint-observation-component-results.md)
now passes exhaustive marginal, fixed-mask routing parity, lifecycle and race
checks. Snapshot plus lookup is approximately 144ns without allocations;
six-coordinate mixture acquisition is 3.5-3.7us in the bounded synthetic fixture.
Two initialization/binding bugs were caught and fixed before quality testing.
This is implementation progress on 1/2/4, not a new adaptive quality pass.
Next: bounded lookahead reference tests, then a frozen fresh adaptive experiment.

[Fixed-size routing replication v102](docs/experiments/mmm-replication-v102-results.md)
PASSES106/106 quality gates and all four finite null screens on3,072 fresh
streams, with unchanged v101 kernels and criteria. Both parity-to-majority
recovery intervals are now positive; confirmation gain0.006793 has interval
[0.003398,0.010187]. All earlier failures remain recorded. Complete replay,
21 source hashes, race checks and block reconstruction pass. This validates
the finite immediate-feedback screen, not the whole roadmap. Next
[integrate the coherent routed law with adaptive observation](research/routed-observation-proposal.md)
and retain the [full-scope audit](research/scope-audit-v102.md). All seven
directions remain open; no more sample-size ladder is proposed for this screen.

[Evidence routing v101](docs/experiments/mmm-routing-v101-results.md) FAILS104/106.
All96 non-harm and6 interaction gains pass. Both parity-to-majority recovery
means now exceed0.005, but both confidence lower bounds remain negative.
Confirmation gain0.005140 has interval[-0.001183,0.011464]. Block diagnostics
show most gain comes from post-change neutralization, not strong promotion of
the useful short-window model. All four null screens, full replay,21 hashes,
race and block-summary checks pass; comparative test states match after every
outcome. Routing costs roughly8% extra monitor arithmetic, zero allocations.
Next do [one fixed-size precision replication](research/routing-replication-proposal.md)
without changing the algorithm or criteria. All seven directions remain open.

[Comparative falsification v100](docs/experiments/mmm-comparative-v100-results.md)
FAILS104/106: all96 non-harm and6 interaction gates pass; both parity-to-majority
positive-recovery gates still fail. Confirmation gain0.002474 has interval
[-0.001500,0.006448], essentially no improvement over matched neutral-only.
All four finite null checks pass. Full768-stream/null replay,19 source hashes,
race checks and independent summary pass. Extra rejection helps the other
direction but does not sufficiently promote the useful short-window model.
Next test [evidence-attributed routing](research/evidence-routing-proposal.md)
without changing models, outcome volume or rejection thresholds. Full comparison
fixture177-198ms includes fitting, not serving. All seven directions remain open.

[Comparative falsification v100 component checks](docs/experiments/mmm-comparative-v100-component-results.md)
pass literal-reference, adaptive conditional-expectation and lifecycle tests,
including race execution and vet. The richer monitor costs about1.1us per
forecast/update pair versus0.22us for neutral-only in a paired arithmetic
benchmark, both zero-allocation. At this earlier component stage, learned-stream
quality and family-null simulations had not been run. The subsequent full v100
result above supersedes that status; no direction is complete.

[Version-scoped falsification v99](docs/experiments/mmm-falsification-v99-results.md)
FAILS104/106 quality gates: all96 non-harm and6 interaction gains pass, but both
parity-to-majority positive recovery gates fail. Confirmation gain is0.000546
with interval[-0.003933,0.005025]. The finite null-family checks pass (6/4096
independent families reject;0/4096 shared families), without certifying learned
forecasts true. Full learned/null replay, reference/lifecycle race tests, source
checks and vet pass. Gate~226-233ns per update averaged over256 rounds, zero
allocations; whole all-control stream177-204ms includes fitting, not serving.
Next test [comparative alternatives](research/comparative-falsification-proposal.md)
under the same error budget and fresh quality tests. No direction is complete.

[Consumed window-headroom diagnosis v98](docs/experiments/mmm-window-headroom-v98-results.md)
preserves all128 selected v97 records exactly and exposes a short useful window:
after parity-to-majority, generic32 helps in128-191, then hurts after192 as
generic64 catches up. The bank assigns too little weight during the useful
period and retains harmful forecasts later. Oracle block mixtures show available
gain, but use hidden simulator truth and are not deployable or new confirmation.
Next test [version-scoped forecast falsification](research/forecast-falsification-proposal.md)
with explicit sequential error accounting, rather than permanently boosting
short memory. Full diagnostic replay, independent trace/score reconstruction,
race math checks and vet pass. v97 remains FAIL; all seven directions stay open.

[Short-window expert bank v97](docs/experiments/mmm-window-bank-v97-results.md)
FAILS102/106:48/48 whole-stream non-harm,46/48 late non-harm,6/6 interaction
gains and2/4 recovery gains pass. Both phases still fail late parity-to-majority.
Confirmation bank Brier0.177947 exceeds generic64's0.170529; the useful generic32
expert has0.153458 but only0.55% mean weight at the first fully post-change
publication. Blind promotion is unsafe: generic32 harms stationary parity4.
Next [diagnose forecast headroom](research/window-bank-headroom-proposal.md)
on explicitly consumed traces before another fresh-data rescue. Full768-record
replay, reference/equivalence/lifecycle race checks and vet pass. Bank update
~160ns, zero allocations; all-control256-step fixture162-183ms including fits.
All seven directions remain open; improved gate count is not full success.

[Interval-weighted learned experts v96](docs/experiments/mmm-online-interval-v96-results.md)
FAILS98/106: all96 whole-stream/late non-harm gates and2/4 recovery gains pass,
but all6 stationary interaction gains fail. Same-stream confirmation parity4
gain falls0.022709 to0.004082 versus fixed sharing; parity-to-majority late harm
falls0.009916 to0.001400 but remains harm. This is a tradeoff, not a rescue.
Reference/lifecycle race tests, exact768-stream replay, source checks and vet
pass. Kernel~360ns/update averaged over256 rounds with511 allocations/lifetime;
full comparison stream114-134ms includes all fits and controls, not serving.
Next test [short-window experts](research/short-window-experts-proposal.md)
alongside retained models, keeping the106 gates and measuring every extra fit.
All seven directions remain open; none is exhausted by this negative result.

[Online learned experts v95](docs/experiments/mmm-online-learned-v95-results.md)
FAILS54/58: all48 whole-stream non-harm and6 stationary interaction-gain gates
pass, but all4 recovery gates fail. Confirmation parity4 reaches94.96% late
expected accuracy; parity-to-majority late Brier worsens0.172585 to0.184354
despite whole-stream improvement. Thus lifetime protection is not recent-regime
protection. Full768-stream replay, race smoke, source checks and vet pass;
these verify the failed result, not a successful rescue. Full experimental
stream cost119-146ms includes eight refit rounds and all controls, not serving.
Next investigate [interval-aware weights](research/interval-brier-proposal.md)
before separately testing shorter model windows. Preserve all previous failures;
the full seven-direction goal remains open.

[Immediate-feedback Brier kernel v94](docs/experiments/mmm-brier-kernel-v94-results.md)
passes component lifecycle, cumulative-bound, race and full replay checks over
448 records. Fixed sharing recovers after 18 outcomes in the deterministic
reversal where plain weighting does not recover within 2,048 steps. It costs
114ns per predict/update cycle versus 38ns without sharing, excluding all
model and serving costs. Duplicate deterministic tapes are not independent
confirmation. This does not rescue the learned-model or delayed-stream failures.
Next: prospective learned-model testing with fresh seeds and honest feedback;
keep all seven directions open, including recommendations 1, 2 and 4.

[Exact LOO stacking v93](docs/experiments/mmm-stacking-v93-results.md) FAILS:
regularized stacking passes12/12 targeted gains but56/60 non-harm cells;
unregularized passes54/60. Skeptical BMA passes this fresh batch, but v92's
failure still stands, so protection evidence remains mixed. The consumed severe
tail is not rescued by either stacking variant. Exact LOO matches explicit
refits and removes the held-out label from both means and likelihood weights.
At64 labels the shortcut takes11.3ms versus478ms for refits, but full stacking
is~51% slower than skeptical BMA and does not improve protection. Retain the
verified primitive; do not adopt this stacking upgrade. Next investigate
[online cumulative Brier-loss control](research/online-brier-aggregation-proposal.md)
with honest sequential feedback and explicit delayed/comparator boundaries.
No whole-direction or delayed-stream success is established.
Full3,840-record replay, exact-refit/leakage race tests, vet and independent
source/summary checks pass. They verify the evidence, not a passed upgrade.

[Skeptical family prior v92](docs/experiments/mmm-prior-v92-results.md) FAILS
overall:12/12 targeted gain gates and59/60 non-harm cells pass, versus52/60
for equal-prior BMA on identical fresh samples. Design n16 majority still fails;
one recorded fit raises Brier0.23695 to0.40899 despite the skeptical prior.
All confirmation cells passing does not erase that design failure. Confirmation
parity4 at32 labels improves Brier0.14909 to0.05350 and expected accuracy78.95%
to95%. Same-fixture fitting cost is comparable to equal-prior BMA (~7.7ms at64).
Do not retune this prior on consumed confirmation. Next test
[predictive validation/stacking](research/predictive-stacking-proposal.md), with
exact conjugate LOO checked against refits and its small-sample risk kept explicit.
No delayed-stream rescue or whole-direction success is established.
Full3,840-record replay, race contracts, vet and independent source/summary
checks pass; these verify the failed experiment rather than override its verdict.

[Generic/Boolean family v91](docs/experiments/mmm-family-v91-results.md) FAILS
the complete advance rule: all12 targeted excess-risk gates pass, but5/60
case/phase/sample-count cells fail non-harm, all at16 labels. At32 labels,
confirmation parity4 full-input Brier improves0.14601 to0.05129 and expected
accuracy79.65% to95%. Majority/multiplexer retain small regressions at32 labels
and negligible ones at64, rather than the specialist's large failures. Sparse
evidence still permits premature specialist influence. Same-fixture64-label
fit costs7.54ms versus6.98ms (~8% extra); this is not daemon latency. Next test
a separately frozen [skeptical family prior](research/family-prior-rescue-proposal.md)
on fresh data with unchanged gain/non-harm requirements. No delayed-stream
rescue, production adoption or whole-direction completion is established.
Full v91 and predecessor v90 replays, focused race tests, vet and independent
source/summary checks pass. They verify the evidence, not a passed rescue.

[Boolean specialist v90](docs/experiments/mmm-boolean-specialist-v90-results.md)
FAILS its frozen advance conjunction (10/12 required cells pass), while providing
strong finite parity-learning evidence. Confirmation parity4 with32 labels
improves full-input Brier0.15995 to0.05512 and expected accuracy77.35% to94.30%.
However majority/multiplexer regress badly, so this cannot replace the generic
learner. The n64 parity3 gain threshold was impossible given the control's
remaining headroom; preserve that protocol failure rather than revise its gate.
At64 labels, fitting costs0.507-0.533ms versus6.949-6.980ms for the subset model.
The fixed partial-view test still limits parity4 accuracy to59.14%; this is not
a delayed-stream or adaptive-observer rescue. Next test a separately frozen
[generic/Boolean family mixture](research/boolean-family-rescue-proposal.md)
with fresh evidence and non-parity protection. All seven directions remain open.
Full3,840-record replay, focused race tests, vet and independent source/summary
checks pass. These verify the experiment, not a successful adoption verdict.

[Interaction diagnostic v89](docs/experiments/mmm-interaction-diagnostic-v89-results.md)
preserves all2,560 v88 records and separates observation coverage, fitted model
quality and selector weights. Late confirmation parity under combined delay
has41.99% required-variable coverage versus82.18% immediately. Yet the age
model's full-input Brier remains0.22300 (actual-input0.23392), so this is not an
attention-only failure. Early full-input age forecasts are worse than neutral;
blindly increasing their weight is not justified. Coverage is a syntactic
generator diagnostic, not a universal sufficiency metric. The v88 failure stands.
The next lead is a [bounded Bayesian Boolean specialist](research/boolean-specialist-proposal.md),
using all subsets rather than a known target mask, alongside generic learners.
It is proposed, not implemented or validated. Freeze a new protocol, test unseen
subsets and non-parity controls, and charge added compute before any adoption.
Full2,560-record replay, focused race tests, vet and independent summary/source
checks pass. This advances diagnosis for directions1,2 and4; all seven directions
remain open.

[Age breadth v88](docs/experiments/mmm-age-breadth-v88-results.md) FAILS the
overall breadth gate: parity4 under combined delay fails in both phases, with
confirmation gain0.00347 spanning zero, Brier0.27172 and accuracy53.91%.
Six other changed cases pass; XOR2 reaches67.46%, dependent bit72.60%, and
stationary protection holds around95%. This uses1,280 independently fitted
base/stream pairs under2 schedules (2,560 runs), with unchanged v87 candidate.
Full replay, generator/seed/parity race tests and vet pass;38/40 cells passing
does not satisfy the required conjunction. Next replay read-only actual/full
input model scores, required-variable coverage and selector weights to separate
learning, observation and weighting bottlenecks. Do not hard-code parity or
lower the threshold. All seven directions remain open; production unchanged.

[Age challenger v87](docs/experiments/mmm-age-challenger-v87-results.md) PASSES
the frozen matched pilot in both phases, all40 cells. On fresh combined-delay
member shifts, retained Brier0.26353 improves to0.20838 and accuracy56.49% to
70.06%; common shifts reach70.36%. Stationary accuracy95.13% is preserved on
these fresh streams. Delayed recurring cases regress slightly within the0.01
tolerance, so this is not universal improvement. One extra age-bounded fit at
existing opportunities costs5.6-6.3ms; forced active-guide cost~3.2us versus
~0.6-0.7us in the fixture. Full2,560-record replay, race and vet pass. Next test
the unchanged candidate on interaction/dependence breadth and different fitted
bases before adding selector changes. All seven directions remain open; no
production, commit or push changes.

[Delay diagnostic v86](docs/experiments/mmm-delay-diagnostic-v86-results.md)
preserves all2,560 v85 records while exposing two bottlenecks. Late combined
member-shift windows contain only54.77% current-regime training examples versus
75.96% immediately; oldest-example age rises284.61 to363.89 steps. Subset Brier
is then0.16311 versus count0.23288, but inner subset weight is39.75% and outer
short weight17.98%. Earlier the subset is genuinely worse, so blind weight
boosting is not justified. This is post-hoc diagnosis, not a passed rescue.
Full original parity/diagnostic replay, race and vet pass. Next test an added
event-age-bounded challenger at unchanged audit/refit cadence, then separately
test version-aware delayed selection. Delayed-learning publications are linked
with explicit guarantee boundaries. All seven directions remain open.

[Delayed learning v85](docs/experiments/mmm-delayed-learning-v85-results.md)
FAILS overall in both phases: combined jitter0..31 plus20% missing feedback
fails member/common-shift gain gates. On640 fresh trajectories under4 paired
schedules, immediate member gain0.03542 falls to0.00194 with an interval spanning
zero; candidate Brier worsens0.20489 to0.26454 and accuracy66.68% to56.32%.
Delay alone and missingness alone retain relative gains, but not immediate
absolute quality. Stationary accuracy remains~94.80%. Full2,560-record replay,
race, accounting and vet pass; no invented labels or production changes.
Next isolate training-window age, refit lag and stale selector feedback before
testing an age-aware rescue. All seven directions remain open; lifecycle
correctness and immediate-feedback gains are not delayed-learning success.

[Feedback journal v84](docs/experiments/mmm-feedback-journal-v84-results.md)
adds a bounded64-entry, single-owner research journal for emitted forecasts.
Immediate-feedback parity, publication/split invalidation, duplicate/expiry
isolation and40,960 delayed/missing-label stress issuances pass, with no phantom
labels. However7,809 received labels (~24%) are skipped for current selector
updates as stale; the learning cost is untested. Full replay/race/vet pass and
immediate microbenchmarks remain near9.8us. Next run actual delayed/missing
forecast-quality comparisons, not infer intelligence from lifecycle correctness.
All seven directions remain open; no production or durable-store changes.

[Subset breadth v83](docs/experiments/mmm-subset-breadth-v83-results.md) PASSES
both phases across1,280 fresh trajectories: XOR, majority, multiplexer, parity4
and two dependent-input cases, with unchanged candidate settings. Every changed
case meets the0.005 mean-gain/positive-lower-bound rule; stable/null protection
holds. Parity4 accuracy remains57.34%, and dependence tests are narrow, so this
is relative breadth improvement, not strong absolute recovery or calibration.
Full replay/race/vet pass. Next add a bounded pending-forecast journal for honest
delayed/missing-feedback tests; preserve immediate-feedback parity and no
invented labels. All seven directions remain open and production is unchanged.

[Retained subset v82](docs/experiments/mmm-retained-subset-v82-results.md) PASSES
the matched-input integration pilot in both phases on640 fresh trajectories.
Member Brier0.24026 to0.20666 and accuracy58.51% to66.56%; common/recurring
improve and stationary/null protection holds. No extra labels or foreground
budget; added subset fitting costs~6.5ms and651KB allocation per64-label fit.
Full replay, archive-manifest replay, race and vet pass. Uniform-input and
9-dimensional bounds remain explicit; earlier broader subset failures stand.
Next test interactions, input dependence and delayed/missing feedback without
retuning this candidate. All seven directions remain open, not production-ready.

[Post-split diagnostic v81](docs/experiments/mmm-post-split-v81-results.md)
replays640 v80 trajectories without changing any actual forecast. Late after
member shift, the relevant bit is observed97.02% of the time, yet short-model
Brier is0.17927 on the actual mask versus0.06839 on a generator-known reduced
mask; all9 coordinates worsen it to0.24039. This supports sparse overconditioning
as a bottleneck, alongside early model lag/neutral weighting. Full replay and
diagnostic race/vet pass. Next integrate learned subset marginalization as a
retained challenger on fresh data, keeping its previous broader failures and
stationary controls visible. No oracle-mask shortcut or completed direction.

[Member integration v80](docs/experiments/mmm-member-integration-v80-results.md)
runs640 fresh trajectories through actual isolated MMM forecasts, Bayesian
nomination, split transitions and refitting. Member splits occur35% sooner,
but post-Brier gain0.000145 has an interval spanning zero and fails the0.005
target. Stable predictions are unchanged;0/64 false splits cannot certify2%.
Overall pilot FAIL/incomplete coverage. A scalar roundoff bug was reproduced
and fixed before artifact generation; full replay/race/vet pass. Next inspect
post-split model quality, mixture influence and observation selection, returning
to directions1/2/4 rather than claiming the faster gate solved learning.
All seven directions remain open; production is unchanged.

[Evidence mixture v79](docs/experiments/mmm-rate-mixture-v79-results.md) PASSES
the frozen finite gate screen in both phases on10,240 fresh paired streams.
Fixed/long/short weights(.5,.25,.25) give26.56%/29.22% sparse confirmation gains
and acceptable homogeneous delay/miss bounds. Full replay/race/vet pass;
bounded loops cost~1.9-2.1us without allocation. Weak detections remain5/512,
and the aggregate mean is not a member-equivalence certificate. Next test actual
research MMM investigation/member-split integration, not another mean-only win.
All seven directions remain open; nothing is enabled in production.

[Short rate v78](docs/experiments/mmm-short-rate-v78-results.md) tests an8-item
rate window with unchanged32-item acquisition/augmentation on10,240 fresh
streams. Sparse confirmation detection gains35.33%/37.57%, but homogeneous
misses rise0 to7 and fail the frozen risk gates in both phases. Weak detections
fall41 to0 vs the long-rate control. Overall FAIL; no serving change. Exact
replay/race/vet pass; balanced/positive microloops ~0.47/0.78us, zero allocations.
Next test a fixed/adaptive evidence mixture, including its initial-wealth
penalty, on fresh streams. All seven research directions remain open.

[Rate diagnostic v77](docs/experiments/mmm-rate-diagnostic-v77-results.md)
replays all 10,240 v76 streams post hoc. In the first32 steps after a homogeneous
change, the learned favorable rate is zero99.43% of the time; the true-growth
gap largely shrinks by steps128-255. This supports rate-estimation lag as a
mechanism. A simulator-only oracle improves delay but still misses151/512 weak
changes; it is not a deployable rescue. Exact replay/race/vet checks pass.
Next test a faster separate rate estimator on fresh streams with unchanged
acceptance gates. All seven directions remain open, and v76 remains FAIL.

[Predictable bets v76](docs/experiments/mmm-predictive-bet-v76-results.md) tests
10,240 fresh paired streams. Sparse confirmation detection improves 15.88% and
17.42%, but homogeneous detection slows 18.23% and fails the paired miss bound;
the design phase also fails protection. Overall FAIL, with no production change.
Full replay and focused race tests pass. The balanced-input microbenchmark is
about 0.52us with no allocations, not a worst-case or serving measurement.
Next isolate rate-adaptation lag and test a protected fixed/adaptive combination
on fresh data. All seven directions remain open; no failed screen is relabeled.

[Betting headroom v75](docs/experiments/mmm-bet-headroom-v75-results.md) checks
the next mechanism before runtime changes: bounded known-law rates increase
sparse expected log growth0.0580 to0.0901, with all-channel factor bounds intact.
This is not measured stopping-time improvement. Twelve sign/case calculations
pass grid checks; next test predictable three-outcome rates from existing past
histories, including zero bets, on fresh data. No adoption or completed direction.

[Augmented evidence v74](docs/experiments/mmm-augmented-evidence-v74-results.md)
uses fresh10240 paired streams and a predeclared paired-risk bound. A predictable
control variate raises primary detection gains to7.21%/6.81%; all confirmation
reliability checks pass, but the10% speed target still fails. Proposal-only
changes do not help. Full replay/race/vet pass; bounded loop ~0.335us. Keep this
research-only; next test stopping-time-aware predictable bets and remaining
real observation/lifecycle contracts. All seven directions remain open.

[Paired-risk v73](docs/experiments/mmm-paired-risk-v73-results.md) improves the
evaluation method, not the detector: a binomial rectangle accounts for both
harmful and beneficial discordances. Full outcome-space enumeration passes660
parameter cells; an alpha-validation bug was caught and fixed. Post-hoc v72
late-case upper changes13.053% to0.596%, without relabeling the original failure
or its unchanged speed shortfall. Freeze the new method before fresh studies;
this analysis supplies no new model outcomes or completed direction.

[Evidence allocation v72](docs/experiments/mmm-evidence-allocation-v72-results.md)
tests a direction3/7 prerequisite in10240 fresh paired streams. Naive targeting
causes472/512 alarms under a heterogeneous population-mean null; known-propensity
correction reduces that to1/512. Corrected primary delays improve4.88%/4.97%,
below10%; late net misses decrease but the conservative harmful-discordance
bound fails. Full replay/race/vet pass. Preserve selection accounting, not the
invalid speed gain; actual MMM trigger/diameter/agent integration remains open.

[Start-pooled evidence v71](docs/experiments/mmm-start-pool-v71-results.md)
advances direction3 without changing serving:10240 fresh paired streams verify
first-alarm dominance when arithmetic wealth is pooled across existing starts.
Fixed pooling gains2.19%/2.46% primary delay and reduces late misses, but fails
the10% speed target. Pooled grid gains about13% but still fails miss limits.
Exact simultaneous binomial discordance bounds replace degenerate normal bounds
for this new screen. All candidates fail overall; retain the partial component
and investigate discriminating follow-up acquisition, not threshold relaxation.

[Context-tree v70](docs/experiments/mmm-context-tree-v70-results.md) returns to
directions1/2/4: exact branch-specific Bayesian pooling tested in512 paired fits
with64 identical labels. Clean multiplexer improves, but all8 primary gates and
26 protection cells fail; noisy majority/parity regress. The absolute-gain gate
also hits a control-error floor in clean cases; preserve results and predeclare
floor-aware thresholds for future screens. No MMM replacement is enabled.
Temporal adaptation and partial-view integration remain unresolved; exact
inference is not evidence of an adequate model family.

[Exclusive ledger v69](docs/experiments/mmm-exclusive-ledger-v69-results.md)
FAILS fresh speed:200-record changes range1.43% faster to7.61% slower, with no
pair meeting10%. All96000 originals/terminals, settings/replacement, external-
reader exclusion, process-exit recovery and race/vet checks pass. Default normal
locking/prepared SQL remains. Stop combining failed SQL/locking variants; require
mechanism-specific evidence before further storage tuning. Service non-harm and
all seven learning/evidence directions remain incomplete.

[Prepared append profile v68](docs/experiments/mmm-append-profile-v68-results.md)
finds commit/shared-memory lock activity, with no SQL compiler samples in the
short run (not proof of zero compilation cost). All48000 originals/terminals
verify. Next test opt-in SQLite exclusive locking for the already single-owner
research ledger, preserving FULL WAL, external-access boundaries and connection-
replacement/recovery guarantees. Driver DSN pragma ordering needs explicit
verification. No runtime changes, speed rescue or completed direction.

[Bounded multi-row admission v67](docs/experiments/mmm-bulk-admission-v67-results.md)
FAILS fresh speed:200-record batches regress2.84-8.50%, though retries improve.
All96000 originals/terminals, sequence/replay parity, late-chunk rollback and
uncertain-commit recovery checks pass; race/vet pass. Keep bulk SQL unused in
serving. Two statement-count rescues have failed; next attribute compilation,
record/index and commit costs before more SQL rewrites. No completed direction
or default change; the mixed-service non-harm gap remains.

[Conditional insertion v66](docs/experiments/mmm-conditional-insert-v66-results.md)
FAILS: fresh200-record admission is37.76-40.85% slower and retries also regress.
All96000 original/terminal checks, paired hashes, race/vet and uncertain-commit
recovery pass. EXPLAIN shows extra ephemeral/coroutine work in INSERT SELECT;
fewer Go/SQL calls did not mean less database work. Keep the strategy opt-in and
unused in serving. Next investigate bounded multi-row VALUES with transactional
preflight and unchanged ordering/conflict/durability. All seven directions open.

[Admission phase diagnostic v65](docs/experiments/mmm-admission-phases-v65-results.md)
finds fresh append accounts for71.84-74.96% of mean admission time; source and
preflight take18.91-20.75% and are not automatically movable outside locks.
All48000 originals/terminals, timing parity, race and vet checks pass. Retry
cost composition differs substantially. Next investigate insert-first atomic
append with narrowly scoped conflict handling, retaining source uniqueness,
exact retries and durable acknowledgments. No completed direction or default
promotion; prior non-harm failures remain.

[Admission audit and group caps v64](docs/experiments/mmm-group-cap-v64-results.md)
reject moving actual forecast/ID staging outside the guard without a new
validity/recovery contract. Fixed smaller groups FAIL:cap2 drops24-33 observations
and cap1 drops64-65 per trial, with worse writer tails despite shorter callbacks.
Cap4 completes all observations but still fails writer non-harm. All72500 admitted
originals/terminals verify; race/accounting and vet pass. Keep batching; next
measure strictly side-effect-free preflight before designing a split admission.
No default change; all seven directions remain open.

[Arm-specific four-slot profiles v63](docs/experiments/mmm-writer-profile-v63-results.md)
identify1.090s cumulative ingestion waiting at the publication gate, alongside
1.096s of guarded callback wall time (0.838s durable admission). Native Put
blocking is lower with active scheduling, so native concurrency alone is not
the remaining issue. All28800 originals/terminals verify; race/vet checks pass.
Next audit immutable staging versus validity-sensitive commit boundaries before
shortening guards. Profiling does not rescue v62; all seven directions stay open.

[Scheduled native writers v62](docs/experiments/mmm-writer-load-v62-results.md)
reduces active writer p99 from337-350ms to121-231ms with four slots; all57600
originals/terminals verify and all observations complete. Joint non-harm passes
only1/3 four-slot trials, versus0/3 two-slot controls, so the rescue still FAILS.
Race/vet checks pass; a source-capture preflight failure is retained separately.
Next attribute remaining four-slot journal/event/publication contention by arm.
Default Open stays two slots; all seven research directions remain open.

[Bounded native writer concurrency v61](docs/experiments/mmm-writer-cost-v61-results.md)
improves four-submitter elapsed time44.76-46.45%, with1536 reopened journals,
full record parity and passing race/recovery checks. The frozen overall screen
FAILS: one sequential p99 regresses11.95% against a10% ceiling. Only an opt-in
research constructor uses four slots; ordinary Open retains two, with unchanged
durability and locks. Next test scheduled mixed-service load, not assume the
isolated gain rescues serving. All seven directions remain open.

[Unchanged guard profile v60](docs/experiments/mmm-guard-profile-v60-results.md)
preserves v59's source hashes and integrity. It identifies publication-gate
waiting and ordinary native journal/WAL durability waits, not heavy validation
CPU, as leads. Totals aggregate all arms and concurrent goroutines; they are not
latency confirmations. Next inspect bounded native flush/batching policy while
preserving durable acknowledgments and snapshot barriers. No runtime changes or
completed direction; prior non-harm failures remain.

[Guard-only diagnostic v59](docs/experiments/mmm-guard-only-v59-results.md)
reproduces a large read improvement without learner persistence in3/3 trials,
but increases writer delay. Guard-only validates28800 candidates without durable
admissions; active durable arms verify86400 originals/terminals. Full resolved
non-harm still fails3/3. Next profile publication-gate versus native-store waits
before changing critical sections or validation reuse. No runtime/default change
or completed direction; all seven remain open.

[Idle-wrapper diagnostic v58](docs/experiments/mmm-idle-wrapper-v58-results.md)
does not reproduce the active read gain: idle scheduled read p99 is478-617ms,
versus27-46ms for resolved source, at the5/10ms offered schedule. Its contribution
is variable, not proven absent. All86400 active originals/terminals verify;
resolved writer non-harm still fails3/3. Next isolate real guarded union
validation without learner persistence. No runtime or default change; all seven
directions remain open.

[Matched offered-load v57](docs/experiments/mmm-offered-load-v57-results.md)
FAILS the full non-harm screen in all nine resolved cells. At100reads/s and
50writes/s, all observations finish and age p95 is26-29ms, but writer overhead
remains. At200reads/s and100writes/s, scheduled write p99 reaches350-406ms.
All258650 accepted originals/terminals and schedule/race checks pass. Active
arms trade read backlog for writer delay; next isolate the publication-store
wrapper with an idle wrapped control. No general latency or completed direction.

[Isolated admission cost v56](docs/experiments/mmm-resolved-cost-v56-results.md)
passes its frozen diagnostic:200-event fresh admission means improve11.60-13.30%
and exact retries30.64-32.35%, with matching cold/trained histories over96000
originals and terminals. No runtime change; race/vet and replay checks pass.
This does not rescue v55 or writer overhead. Next use matched offered-arrival
load, including producer lateness/backlog, to separate queue pressure from
per-batch cost. All directions remain open; no default or publication change.

[Resolved source admission v55](docs/experiments/mmm-resolved-admission-v55-results.md)
removes the duplicate learner-ID preflight read through private, call-local
resolution reuse, retaining exact atomic conflict checks and actual forecasts.
Warm/retry/replay/fault/race/vet checks pass, but the loaded rescue FAILS:
age p95 is235.352/250.052/276.898ms, only one trial within250ms. Completion is
569/576 versus566/576 for same-run combined control; writer overhead remains.
Next separate isolated admission cost from closed-loop arrival/scheduling effects
before another optimization. No default, publication or direction-completion claim.

[Verified cleanup v54](docs/experiments/mmm-verified-cleanup-v54-results.md)
improves completion to570/576 versus533/576 for two-step source cleanup, but age
p95 is266/259/240ms: only one trial passes250ms. Read/integrity and full race/vet
checks pass; writer overhead remains. Audit also fixed a reproduced timezone
retry-byte conflict in shared batch discard. Next investigate reuse of validated
source resolution during admission under exclusive ownership, retaining atomic
conflict checks and original forecasts. No default change or completed direction.

[Owner batch-read integration v53](docs/experiments/mmm-source-batch-load-v53-results.md)
is a partial rescue, still FAILING age:303-321ms p95, with539/576 completions
versus400/576 for point source. Warm-original/reopen/uncertainty and full race/vet
checks pass; read tails pass but writer overhead remains. Next test combined
verified discard under the owner lock to avoid resolving originals twice, while
retaining actual readback, canonical identity and atomic terminals. No threshold
relaxation, production default change or completed direction follows.

[Transactional source reads v52](docs/experiments/mmm-source-reads-v52-results.md)
pass the isolated screen: 200-key hit means improve77-78%, misses88%, with all
96,000 results verified. Snapshot/cancellation/panic/error/byte-cap and full
race/vet checks pass. Next integrate opt-in owner batch resolution/readback,
preserving canonical originals and uncertainty stops, then rerun loaded screens.
The owner still uses point reads; v50/v51's age failure is not yet rescued, and
all seven research directions remain in progress. No default or deployment change.

[Unchanged source profile v51](docs/experiments/mmm-source-profile-v51-results.md)
repeats the failed age screen (554-566ms; 404/576 source observations complete).
Source hashes match v50. Samples implicate repeated SQLite shared-memory locking
around point reads; canonical decoding also allocates. Next test a bounded
transactional source-read batch, preserving source identity, snapshot/error/byte
checks and canonical originals. No parsing-dominance or loaded-success claim,
and all seven directions remain in progress.

[Loaded source owner v50](docs/experiments/mmm-source-load-v50-results.md) FAILS
the unchanged completion-age screen in all trials: 539-591ms p95 and 395/576
observations completed, versus 576/576 with the raw prepared control. Read tails
pass but writer overhead remains. All accepted originals/readbacks/terminals
match. Next profile repeated source resolution, then test bounded transactional
source reads if supported by evidence. v49's isolated gain does not establish
loaded success, and feedback/history authority is still open. Defaults unchanged.

[Source-owner cost v48](docs/experiments/mmm-source-owner-cost-v48-results.md)
identified per-item cleanup overhead. [Atomic source discard v49](docs/experiments/mmm-source-discard-cost-v49-results.md)
passes the frozen isolated screen: 200-event whole-cycle means fall 22-23%, from
about 43.6ms to 33.5-33.8ms, with original/retry/replay integrity over 48,000
records. Full race/vet pass. Next test the source owner in the guarded loaded
fixture; these cold lifecycle timings do not complete direction 6, establish
feedback/history authority, or revalidate any accuracy claim. Defaults unchanged.

[Source owner v47](docs/experiments/mmm-source-owner-v47-results.md) now resolves
service journal/event retries before staging learner IDs. Canonical bound replay,
conflict preflight, restart/discard, concurrent retry and uncertain-commit tests
pass, including guarded real-service-journal integration. The owner remains opt-in
and exposes no feedback or scoring API; fresh feedback/history authority and
loaded wrapper cost are still open. This is identity integration progress, not a
completed learning direction or a new accuracy/latency claim.

[Source identity primitive v46](docs/experiments/mmm-source-index-v46-results.md)
now enforces persistent uniqueness atomically with bound original rows when
explicitly enabled. Duplicate/conflict/reopen/concurrency/process-exit and read
bound checks pass; full race/vet pass. Point lookups use all five index fields at
100-10,000 records (26-46us mean hits), not a lifetime scan/map. Next integrate an
opt-in owner that resolves source retries before staging IDs; default unsafe
composition and feedback/history-authority gaps are not yet fixed by the index.

[Lifecycle audit v44](docs/experiments/mmm-identity-audit-v44-results.md) reproduces
the missing durable service-owner boundary in all eight cases: one journal/event
can obtain two learner IDs and replay two labels. Exact-ID retries and the older
bridge's deduplication remain correct. Raw durable feedback/replay do not claim
service authority. Next implement the indexed atomic source-identity primitive
under the [v45 owner contract](docs/experiments/mmm-service-owner-contract-v45.md),
then enforce separate fresh feedback/history authority. No production path was
enabled; earlier cold storage success does not establish safe continuous learning.

[Per-guard union reads v42](docs/experiments/mmm-union-reads-v42-results.md) and
[unchanged confirmation v43](docs/experiments/mmm-union-confirmation-v43-results.md)
PASS all six finite age/read screens: 1,152/1,152 observations complete without
drops, retaining actual durable originals and terminals. Age p95 spans 118-218ms;
writer overhead and high-overlap/same-as-of restrictions remain. Source hashes
match across runs. Next audit unique durable service admission identity and
history/feedback authority, alongside broader traffic. This cold, unlabeled
fixture does not complete direction 6 or any whole-goal learning claim.

[Prepared wrapper load v41](docs/experiments/mmm-prepared-load-v41-results.md)
is MIXED: completion rises from 522/576 to 543/576 overall, but one trial regresses
and age remains 281-310ms, failing 250ms. Matched-history warm originals, replay,
uncertain commits, race and vet pass; an initially confounded asynchronous parity
test is documented. Next test per-guard union event reads across overlapping
frontiers, retaining all per-journal/query/feature/publication checks. No default
switch or completed research-direction claim follows from isolated SQL speedups.

[Prepared write statements v40](docs/experiments/mmm-prepared-v40-results.md)
PASS the isolated ledger screen: size-200 admission/terminal mean pair time falls
42-47%, with FULL commits and all 96,000 original records checked. Prepared/control
parity, cancellation/panic/prepare failure and process-exit recovery pass, as do
full race suites and vet. Next wire an opt-in durable wrapper and test actual
loaded completion; this micro-level gain does not establish the 250ms target or
complete any research direction. Default APIs and production remain unchanged.

[Post-guard readback v39](docs/experiments/mmm-postverify-v39-results.md) completes
529/576 observations versus 483/576, with full originals and durable cleanup.
Age p95 improves to 290-302ms but still FAILS 250ms; write tails remain above off.
Missing/mismatched/canceled readback, publication/reopen, race and vet checks pass.
Next measure transaction-scoped write-statement reuse against an unchanged ledger
control before another load claim. Warm learning, unique service identity and
history/feedback authority remain open; narrower guard scope does not grant them.

[Post-guard discard v38](docs/experiments/mmm-postdiscard-v38-results.md) improves
completed observations from 423/576 to 479/576, preserving actual durable
terminals and original readback. Age p95 remains 337-361ms, failing 250ms; write
tails improve in two trials and worsen in one. Publication/recovery tests, full
race suites and vet pass. Next investigate moving immutable ledger readback
outside the guard, with full checks before acknowledgment and admission still
protected. This remains partial infrastructure progress, not completed learning.

[Unchanged profile v37](docs/experiments/mmm-snapshot-reads-profile-v37-results.md)
replicates the remaining durable admission deficit and shows prominent SQL
commit/locking/page-write paths. Next investigate releasing the service guard
before typed discard, with publication interleaving and failure/recovery tests.
Admission stays guarded; discard cannot create evidence or silently disappear.
This is a proposed scope reduction for terminal cleanup, not a validated rescue.

[Snapshot-read integration v36](docs/experiments/mmm-snapshot-reads-v36-results.md)
preserves original-record checks and improves readback cost, but does not rescue
the frozen age target: 394-399ms p95 versus 250ms. Admission is 418/576 versus
407/576 with per-key reads; write tails still exceed off. Full ledger, learner
and service race suites pass. Next profile remaining callback work before
another change; no durability weakening, production adoption or direction-level
success is inferred from this cold, unlabeled storage experiment.

[Transactional read batches v35](docs/experiments/mmm-read-batch-v35.md) now
preserve ordered keys and explicit missing records within one bounded snapshot.
Interleaved-writer snapshot, byte/corruption, cancellation and panic tests pass;
full ledger/learner/service race and vet pass. Next integrate preflight/readback
with same-run per-key controls. No performance benefit or completed research
direction is inferred from the primitive tests.

[Batched API profile v34](docs/experiments/mmm-transactions-profile-v34-results.md)
preserves v33 source hashes and replicates its remaining admission deficit.
SQLite locking/page-write paths are prominent; repeated Get calls remain outside
shared read transactions. Next test bounded transactional multi-key reads with
strict found/missing, identity and byte accounting. Statement parsing alone is
not established as the bottleneck. Durable learning and all other criteria remain
open; no configuration or published claims changed.

[Loaded batch transactions v33](docs/experiments/mmm-transactions-v33-results.md)
improve admission from 233/576 to 393/576 and age tails from 821-849ms to 424-435ms,
while retaining full readback and actual discard writes. Write p99 falls to about
39ms but remains above off; the 250ms age screen still FAILS. No records mismatch.
Next profile remaining batch API/SQL costs before another optimization. This is
partial infrastructure progress, not a durable-learning or real-agent result.

[Durable wrapper batches v32](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-batches-v32.md) now
stage actual owned-worker forecasts and atomically persist admission or typed
discard. Warm-original replay, strict preflight, pending caps, commit-error/panic
and process-exit recovery tests pass; full learner/ledger/service race and vet
pass. These APIs are not yet in the loaded group4 comparison. Next measure them
against individual transactions with readback retained; do not infer throughput
or goal completion from wrapper correctness tests.

[Atomic ledger batches v31](docs/experiments/mmm-ledger-batch-v31.md) now preserve
ordered identities, exact retries and all-or-nothing commit under explicit count/
byte caps. Rollback, cancellation, concurrent retry, exact-cap and actual process
exit tests pass; full ledger/learner/service race suites and vet pass. This is
the storage primitive only. Next integrate original-forecast admission and typed
discard in the durable wrapper before measuring whether v29/v30 is rescued.
No throughput or learning success is inferred from transaction unit tests.

[Durable load v29](docs/experiments/mmm-grouped-durable-v29-results.md) FAILED:
real SQLite admission/discard reduces acceptance to 232/576, with 788-858ms age
tails and large write-latency transfer. [Phase diagnostic v30](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-phases-v30-results.md)
replicates failure and attributes about 87% of callback time to admission/discard
APIs, not integrity readback. Next implement bounded group transactions with
rollback/retry/recovery tests, preserving FULL durability and original records.
Cold preview-ID abandonment/reopen tests pass; this is not a learned-model ID
remapping contract. All direction-level success claims remain unestablished.

[Unchanged grouping confirmation v28](docs/experiments/mmm-grouped-confirmation-v28-results.md)
admits 576/576 with no drops/expiry and 84-123ms age p95. Across v27/v28 all six
finite age/read screens pass; the prior four-observation deadline failure remains.
Source hashes match exactly. This supports ready grouping for the next durable
admission test, not a production or learning claim. Actual durable writes,
verified feedback/history authority and all other direction-level criteria remain
open; no production integration or push has occurred.

[Phase diagnostic v26](docs/experiments/mmm-batch-phases-v26-results.md) shows
guard entry now dominates batch processing. [Ready grouping v27](docs/experiments/mmm-grouped-load-v27-results.md)
admits 572/576 observations with zero queue drops and 90-109ms age p95, passing
the finite age/read screens. One deadline rejects four observations, and write
tails can rise. This is an uninstalled, unlabeled, non-durable research consumer;
all seven directions remain open. Repeat the unchanged protocol before extending
the scheduling result to durable learning or real tasks.

[Profile v24](docs/experiments/mmm-asof-profile-v24-results.md) identified repeated
journal decoding as the dominant sampled validation CPU/allocation cost.
[Batch v25](docs/experiments/mmm-batch-load-v25-results.md) improves admission
from 225/576 to 530/576 while retaining per-record checks. However, 45 drops,
one expiry and 314-388ms age tails leave the 250ms screen FAILED. Unit/race
checks pass; all seven research directions remain open. Next separate guard
waiting from callback work; do not mistake this unlabeled infrastructure rescue
for predictive or durable-learning validation.

[As-of load v23](docs/experiments/mmm-asof-load-v23-results.md) partially restores
admission: 224/576 observations and 11,200 candidate validations, with no stale
as-of rejection. But 352 observations drop and accepted-age p95 is 1.14-1.17s;
performance remains FAILED. Read p99 improves while write p99 worsens. This is
unlabeled, non-durable infrastructure evidence, not a MMM accuracy rescue.
Next profile repeated per-candidate validation before a bounded batch design.

[Queued load v22](docs/experiments/mmm-queued-load-v22-results.md) FAILED
availability: 576 immediate attempts were busy and 576 queued attempts were
stale, with zero admitted observations. The separate as-of guard now has initial
race-tested future-only and forbidden-motion controls on actual service records;
loaded rescue and full durable learning remain unestablished. Existing exact
guards remain unchanged. No production configuration or published claim changed.

[Guard isolation v20](docs/experiments/mmm-guard-isolation-v20-results.md)
accepted384/384 read-only observations and0/384 under future writes; moving
journal reads did not help. Read-only p95 age461-507ms also exposes drain cost.
[Queued guard v21](docs/experiments/mmm-queued-guard-v21.md) now has deadline-aware
entry with unchanged under-lock exact validation; targeted/full race tests and
vet pass. Loaded availability and as-of compatibility are NOT established.

[Exact-guard load v19](docs/experiments/mmm-guard-load-v19-results.md) completed
nine persistent arms but rejected ALL1,152 enabled observations as busy. Zero
records reached validation or SQLite, so request timings do not establish ledger
or learning performance. Race accounting checks and artifact verification pass;
availability fails. Next isolate contention and test bounded queued/as-of guards
without weakening under-lock validation. Production remains untouched.

[Persistent guard v18](docs/experiments/mmm-persistent-guard-v18.md) verifies
actual memory/LibraVDB Recall admission, concurrent writer exclusion and exact
SQLite record preservation after reopen. Four race-tested cells pass. Future-only
motion remains as-of valid but is rejected by the stricter exact guard, so prior
temporal load results cannot establish guarded durable throughput. Next measure
this boundary under writes; full feedback/history authority remains open.

[Stopping v10](research/hypothesis-observation/STOPPING_RESULTS.md) FAILED
accuracy protection on1,792 fresh episodes. Cautious stopping saved38%/61% of
reports in copied/noise05 cases, but final-Brier harm exceeded ceilings in three
populations. All replay, prefix-isolation and cost tests pass. Do not replace
accuracy gates with penalized utility or retune on these outcomes. Next return
to persistent shadow integration; real-agent validation also remains open.

[Two-step v8 diagnostic](research/hypothesis-observation/LOOKAHEAD_RESULTS.md)
warranted a fresh rollout. [V9 results](research/hypothesis-observation/LOOKAHEAD_ROLLOUT_RESULTS.md)
then FAILED the full rescue on1,792 new episodes: copied/mixed early forecasts
improved in both splits, but independent speed/protection and misleading-signal
uncertainty gates failed. Full replay, four-leaf value checks and budget tests
pass. Keep this as localized evidence, not a replacement observer. No deeper
lookahead is justified without a new discriminating reason.

[Mixture acquisition v7](research/hypothesis-observation/MIXTURE_ACQUISITION_RESULTS.md)
FAILED the equal-budget rescue screen on1,792 fresh episodes: acquisition speed
did not improve, and the misleading-signal gain's interval crossed zero. Mean
non-harm ceilings passed. Full replay and analytical/counterfactual gain checks
pass. Next inspect two-step value before paying for another rollout; retain
fixed acquisition as control and preserve all prior failures.

[Reliability averaging v6](research/hypothesis-observation/RELIABILITY_RESULTS.md)
partially rescues misleading signals (final Brier0.408 to0.299) but FAILED the
full screen: independent-stream learning slows versus reliable-only weighting.
All fixed-control mean harm gates passed, without proving population non-harm.
Full replay and12,288-state enumeration pass. Next test mixture-aware acquisition
at the same budget; uncertainty cannot be removed before discriminating evidence.

[Provenance factorial v5](research/hypothesis-observation/FACTORIAL_RESULTS.md)
separates selection from scoring on1,792 fresh episodes. Informed scoring improves
independent-noise forecasts but harms them under misleading provenance; final
acquisition-only contrasts remain inconclusive. Copied-stream v4 harm did not
reproduce decisively. Full replay/isolation tests pass. Next test uncertainty
over signal reliability, not a retuned fixed prior. No adoption claim is made.

[Informed provenance v4](research/hypothesis-observation/INFORMED_RESULTS.md)
FAILED its frozen screen across 1,792 episodes. Disjoint signal calibration
improved mean independent-noise Brier but did not establish the recovery bound;
copied-noise harm exceeded both ceilings. Misleading signals worsened forecasts.
Full replay and exact joint enumeration pass. Next separate acquisition policy
from posterior weighting on fresh seeds; this is not authenticated provenance.

[Snapshot guard v17](docs/experiments/mmm-service-guard-v17.md) excludes owned
store mutations through validation and a bounded durable-admission callback.
Stale/unsupported guards reject, errors/panics release, and a concurrent writer
resumes after release. Targeted race repetitions, three full package race suites
and vet pass. This is not a full durable bridge or cross-database transaction;
feedback authority and loaded contention remain open.

[Service interleave v16](docs/experiments/mmm-service-validation-interleave-v16.md)
validates real Recall records on memory/persistent stores and rejects a policy
change after the event read at the final dependency check. Both controls pass
three race repetitions and vet. This is not atomic validation-plus-persistence;
the live durable bridge and whole-history dependency authority remain open.

[Service validation v15](docs/experiments/mmm-service-validation-v15.md) now
checks bound records against actual Recall journals, query digests, candidates,
features and current dependencies. Mutation/stale negative tests pass three
targeted race runs, full service race and vet. This is point-in-time admission
validation, not an atomic live bridge or feedback-truth authentication.

[Service binding v14](docs/experiments/mmm-service-binding-v14.md) persists actual
journal/event identity and snapshot beside original forecasts. Rebinding/removal
reject, detached reads and reopen preserve metadata; three package race runs and
vet pass. This is the storage contract only: journal and current dependency
verification still belong to the unimplemented durable service bridge.

[Ledger ownership v13](docs/experiments/mmm-ledger-ownership-v13.md) fixes a
confirmed second-owner gap with a lifetime local file lock. Duplicate/symlink
owners reject; close and actual child exit permit reopening. Combined package
race runs, alias coverage and vet pass. This is not distributed/hard-link-safe
ownership; service dependency binding and loaded durable serving remain open.

[Discard failure v12](docs/experiments/mmm-discard-failures-v12.md) passes
before/after-commit uncertainty and actual-wrapper process exit after64 discards.
No labels are invented; retries deduplicate and later labels reject. Expanded
six-case/four-case suites pass three race repetitions and vet. Service binding,
ownership, retention and loaded durable serving remain open.

[Durable discard v11](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-discard-v11.md) now persists
unlabeled abandonment in a distinct typed terminal record.300 discarded records
reopen with zero labels/pending; label/discard conflicts reject. Three package
race runs and vet pass. Discard-specific failure injection, service expiry and
dependency integration remain open; no negative evidence is inferred from absence.

[Durable cost v10](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-cost-v10-results.md) measured12
arms:1024-label processing takes145-150ms durable versus44ms memory-only; full
replay46-48ms, closed DB933888 bytes. All restart forecasts match. This measures
component cost, not an e2e latency success gate. Durable off-path service load,
dependency binding and checkpoint/retention remain required.

[Uncertain-write v9](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-uncertain-write-v9.md) tests
admission/feedback errors before and after actual SQLite commit. All four cases
stop the wrapper and recover/retry without duplicate or lost labels. Three
targeted/full race repetitions and vet pass. Physical disk faults, restart cost,
durable latency, discard and live service dependency binding remain open.

[Durable wrapper exit v8](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-wrapper-exit-v8.md) passes
three actual-API process-exit cases: pending admissions, queued feedback and
applied feedback. All512 forecasts match control; retries deduplicate and pending
feedback continues. Three race repetitions and vet pass. Uncertain-write handling,
durable latency, discard and service event/dependency binding remain required.

[Durable wrapper v7](docs/experiments/mmm-durable-wrapper-v7.md) now persists new
admissions/feedback before acknowledgment, provides exact retry lookup and resumes
after orderly reopen.48-label512-state parity, retries and rejection tests pass
three package race runs and vet. Wrapper crash/I/O-error tests, durable latency,
discard and service dependency binding remain required; it is unconfigured.

[Background resume v6](docs/experiments/mmm-background-resume-v6.md) restores
64 labels plus16 pending records, continues delayed feedback with exact512-state
parity, handles an in-memory discard and resumes IDs. Three race runs and vet
pass. New operations are not automatically durable; discard logging, live
acknowledgment ordering and service dependency authority still need integration.

[Replay API v5](docs/experiments/mmm-replay-api-v5.md) validates canonical typed
records and stream/ID binding, returning no partial model on failure. All four
process-recovery cases match control through the API; malformed/missing feedback
and identity mismatches reject. Three race runs and vet pass. Live service-event
binding, acknowledgment pipeline and dependency revalidation remain unimplemented.

[Warm recovery v4](docs/experiments/mmm-warm-background-recovery-v4.md) extends
process-exit parity to64 applied plus64 queued outcomes and fully applied128
outcomes. Ready-state assertions prove non-cold records; all512 forecasts match
the uninterrupted control. Four cases pass three race repetitions and vet.
Live consumer acknowledgment/dependency recovery and realistic workloads remain.

[Background process-exit recovery v3](docs/experiments/mmm-background-process-recovery-v3.md)
now combines SQLite and original background records: queued-before-learning and
fully-applied child exits both replay64 labels exactly across512 states, with
deduplicated retries. Three race repetitions and vet pass. This is full-log
fixture recovery, not a live consumer, checkpointing or dependency restoration.

[Ledger abrupt-exit v2](docs/experiments/mmm-ledger-abrupt-exit-v2.md) passes four
admission/feedback commit boundaries across three race runs: committed records
survive/deduplicate, uncommitted records disappear, integrity checks pass. This
uses isolated child processes, not power loss or interruption inside COMMIT.
Background learner recovery across these boundaries remains to be integrated.

[Lagging background replay v2](docs/experiments/mmm-async-original-replay-v2.md)
matches512/512 final forecasts using recorded experts. The recompute negative
control changes32 admissions and512/512 final forecasts, demonstrating why
original journals matter. Three race repetitions/full package runs and vet pass.
This is JSON-roundtrip asynchronous parity, not process-crash integration.

[Original-record replay v1](docs/experiments/mmm-original-record-replay-v1.md)
reconstructs320 labels from640 SQLite records after reopen, matching all512
feature forecasts exactly. Typed original experts avoid recomputing past
predictions. Three race runs and corrected vet pass. This is trusted synchronous
replay, not background durability, crash recovery or removal of the bridge cap.

[Research ledger v1](docs/experiments/mmm-research-ledger-v1.md) provides durable
ordered admission/feedback identities with conflict rejection and bounded replay
pages. Three race runs and vet pass, including concurrent retries and reopening.
It preserves opaque JSON, not validated predictions: typed records, learner
reconstruction and interrupted-commit tests remain required. No bridge wiring.

[Combined integration checkpoint v9](docs/experiments/mmm-integration-checkpoint-v9.md)
passed worker/publication/store/service race tests together. Load experiments
were not rerun by this command. Source audit confirms the256-journal in-memory
replay guard still bounds lifetime; orderly store reopening does not restore
learner state. The next durable-consumer contract explicitly preserves original
forecasts, feedback identity and atomic applied position; it is not implemented.

[Orderly restart coverage](docs/experiments/mmm-publication-restart-v1.md) now
verifies persisted events/vectors and current snapshot authority across three
adapter lifetimes, while rejecting missing pre-restart motion proofs. Three
race runs and vet pass. A known duplicate quarantine permits orderly reopen,
but ambiguous transaction/crash recovery and durable learner replay are unproved.

Posterior/snap success-path parity now covers outcome/duplicate, graph publish,
dependent residual invalidation and rollback on both backends. Three corrected
race runs and vet pass. An initial timestamp comparison failure was a test
assumption: each rollback timestamp is now interval-checked and read back before
normalization. Certified-to-uncertified transition and recovery remain open;
this is not complete mutation or continuous-learning validation.

Agency success-path tests through the research publication adapter now pass on
memory and persistent stores (three race repetitions and vet): insert/duplicate,
claim/leased no-op and approve/duplicate, with explicit status and snapshot
checks. No executor was involved. This closes one lifecycle test gap, not
restart recovery, autonomous safety, posterior/snap coverage or direction6.

[Same-run notification v8](docs/experiments/mmm-notification-comparison-v8-results.md)
completed36 arms. Queue64 notification passes all six cells with age p95
172-192ms; polling fails two age cells. Queue16 gains16 admissions on average
but fails one serving-p99 cell (ratio1.1975). This supports a finite queue64
rescue, not universal latency or learning claims. Move to realistic workloads,
remaining mutation paths and durable replay rather than tune the fixture again.

[Completion notification v7](docs/experiments/mmm-notified-v7-results.md) PASSED
all18 finite load arms after three worker race-test runs. Long queue16 admits
167-170/192; queue64 admits all192 with age p95 165-187ms. This is a provisional
rescue, requiring same-run polling comparison and replication, not completion
of direction6. Old artifacts retain pre-notification source; no outcome rewrites.

[Consumer timing v6](docs/experiments/mmm-consumer-v6-results.md) measured all18
arms: long-workload completion wait3.77-3.97ms/frontier exceeds admission
1.79-2.22ms, with approximately three polls. Wait includes useful worker fitting,
so it is not all removable overhead. Four frozen cells failed. Next test a
race-safe completion notification against polling, without weakening gates.

[Same-run adapter comparison v5](docs/experiments/mmm-adapter-comparison-v5-results.md)
completed18 arms. General minus ingestion admissions averaged-0.5 frontier,
but general failed two six-trial cells at153/192 versus154 required. All latency,
delay and label-completion checks passed. This is not equivalence or a proven
adapter regression. Measure consumer stages and polling before the next rescue;
the workload has little throughput headroom. Earlier failures remain recorded.

The [general-adapter load v4](docs/experiments/mmm-adapter-load-v4-results.md)
FAILED: queue16 admitted149/157/148 of192 (two below80%); queue64 admitted all but
one completion-age p95 was264.744ms against250ms. Serving-p99 gates and label
completion passed. All18 arms and source/count accounting were audited. This
does not transfer the old ingestion-only pass to the general adapter. Compare
